<script>
/* Orexis KPIs on Google: a small database client that talks to Code.gs and
   looks like the claude.use("db") / claude.use("user") API the app is written against. */
(function(){
  window.KAIZEN_GOOGLE=true;
  const TOK='kaizen_token';
  const ls={get:k=>{try{return localStorage.getItem(k)}catch(e){return null}},set:(k,v)=>{try{localStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}},del:k=>{try{localStorage.removeItem(k)}catch(e){}}};
  function call(fn,...args){return new Promise((res,rej)=>{google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(res).withFailureHandler(e=>rej(e))[fn](...args)})}
  let session=null;let sessionReady;const ready=new Promise(r=>sessionReady=r);
  const store={};const listeners=new Set();let since=0;let syncing=false;let pending=false;
  /* ---- local store + live queries ---- */
  function snapDoc(path){const d=store[path];return{id:path.split('/').pop(),exists:!!d,data:()=>d,metadata:{fromCache:false,hasPendingWrites:false}}}
  function childDocs(path){const n=path.split('/').length+1;return Object.keys(store).filter(k=>k.startsWith(path+'/')&&k.split('/').length===n)}
  function runQuery(path,f,ob,lim){let ks=childDocs(path);
    let docs=ks.map(snapDoc).filter(d=>f.every(([fld,op,v])=>{const x=d.data()[fld];
      return op==='=='?x===v:op==='!='?x!==v:op==='<'?x<v:op==='<='?x<=v:op==='>'?x>v:op==='>='?x>=v:op==='in'?(v||[]).includes(x):op==='not-in'?!(v||[]).includes(x):op==='array-contains'?Array.isArray(x)&&x.includes(v):true}));
    if(ob){const [fld,dir]=ob;docs.sort((a,b)=>{const x=a.data()[fld],y=b.data()[fld];if(x===y)return 0;if(x==null)return 1;if(y==null)return -1;return(x<y?-1:1)*(dir==='desc'?-1:1)})}
    else docs.sort((a,b)=>a.id<b.id?-1:a.id>b.id?1:0);
    if(lim)docs=docs.slice(0,lim);
    return{docs,size:docs.length,empty:!docs.length,docChanges:()=>[],metadata:{fromCache:false,hasPendingWrites:false}}}
  function notify(){listeners.forEach(l=>l.run())}
  function addListener(compute,fn){const l={last:null,run(){let r;try{r=compute()}catch(e){return}const sig=JSON.stringify(r.docs?r.docs.map(d=>[d.id,d.data()]):[r.exists,r.data()]);if(sig===l.last)return;l.last=sig;try{fn(r)}catch(e){console.error(e)}}};
    listeners.add(l);ready.then(()=>setTimeout(()=>l.run(),0));return()=>listeners.delete(l)}
  function merge(a,b){const o={...(a||{})};for(const k in b){const v=b[k];o[k]=v&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v)&&o[k]&&typeof o[k]==='object'&&!Array.isArray(o[k])?merge(o[k],v):v}return o}
  function applyDocs(docs){(docs||[]).forEach(x=>{if(x.del)delete store[x.p];else store[x.p]=x.d})}
  /* ---- sync with the sheet ---- */
  async function sync(full){if(!session)return;if(syncing){pending=true;return}syncing=true;
    try{const r=await call('sync',session.token,full?0:Math.max(0,since-3000));
      if(r.full){Object.keys(store).forEach(k=>delete store[k])}
      applyDocs(r.docs);since=r.now;reapply();if(outbox.length)flush();
      if(r.me&&(r.me.role!==session.role)){session.role=r.me.role;ls.set(TOK,JSON.stringify(session));location.reload();return}
      notify()}
    catch(e){if(String(e&&e.message||e).includes('AUTH')){logoutLocal();return}}
    finally{syncing=false;if(pending){pending=false;setTimeout(()=>sync(false),200)}}}
  let timer=null;function startPolling(){clearInterval(timer);timer=setInterval(()=>{if(document.visibilityState==='visible')sync(false)},40000);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.visibilityState==='visible')sync(false)});setInterval(()=>sync(true),10*60000)}
  /* ---- offline outbox: writes made with no signal are kept on the phone and sent when it's back ---- */
  const OBK=()=>'kaizen_outbox_'+(session&&session.uid||'');let outbox=[];let flushing=false;
  function loadOutbox(){try{outbox=JSON.parse(ls.get(OBK())||'[]')||[]}catch(_){outbox=[]}badge()}
  function saveOutbox(){ls.set(OBK(),JSON.stringify(outbox));badge()}
  function isNet(e){const m=String(e&&e.message||e);return !/AUTH|DENIED|MISSING|BAD_PATH|TOO_LARGE|Only Ameliah|Enter your|PIN/.test(m)}
  function queue(ops){outbox.push(ops);saveOutbox();setTimeout(flush,4000)}
  function reapply(){outbox.forEach(ops=>ops.forEach(o=>{if(o.op==='delete')delete store[o.path];else if(o.op==='update')store[o.path]=merge(store[o.path]||{},o.data);else store[o.path]=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o.data))}))}
  function badge(){let b=document.getElementById('syncBadge');if(!b){b=document.createElement('div');b.id='syncBadge';b.setAttribute('role','status');
      b.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);top:calc(8px + env(safe-area-inset-top));z-index:80;background:#8a6100;color:#fff;font:600 13px/1.2 system-ui,sans-serif;padding:7px 14px;border-radius:999px;box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.18);cursor:pointer';
      b.onclick=()=>flush();document.body.append(b)}
    const n=outbox.length;b.hidden=!n;b.textContent=n?('No signal \u2014 '+n+' change'+(n>1?'s':'')+' saved on this phone, will send automatically'):''}
  async function flush(){if(flushing||!session||!outbox.length)return;flushing=true;
    try{while(outbox.length){const ops=outbox[0];
        try{const r=await call('write',session.token,ops);outbox.shift();saveOutbox();applyDocs(r.docs)}
        catch(e){if(isNet(e))break;outbox.shift();saveOutbox();const m=String(e&&e.message||e).replace(/^Error: /,'');
          try{const t=document.getElementById('toast');if(t){t.textContent='A change made offline couldn\u2019t be saved: '+m;t.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),5000)}}catch(_){}}}
      reapply();notify()}
    finally{flushing=false;if(outbox.length)setTimeout(flush,30000)}}
  window.addEventListener('online',()=>setTimeout(flush,1500));
  async function doWrite(ops){
    const backup={};ops.forEach(o=>backup[o.path]=store[o.path]);
    ops.forEach(o=>{if(o.op==='delete')delete store[o.path];else if(o.op==='update'){if(!store[o.path])throw{code:'invalid_argument',message:'Document does not exist'};store[o.path]=merge(store[o.path],o.data)}else store[o.path]=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o.data))});
    notify();
    if(outbox.length){queue(ops);return}
    try{const r=await call('write',session.token,ops);applyDocs(r.docs);notify()}
    catch(e){if(isNet(e)){queue(ops);return}Object.keys(backup).forEach(p=>{if(backup[p]===undefined)delete store[p];else store[p]=backup[p]});notify();
      const m=String(e&&e.message||e);if(m.includes('AUTH')){logoutLocal();throw{code:'revoked',message:m}}
      throw{code:m.includes('DENIED')||m.includes('MISSING')||m.includes('BAD_PATH')?'invalid_argument':m.includes('TOO_LARGE')?'quota_exceeded':'unavailable',message:m}}}
  function docRef(path){return{id:path.split('/').pop(),path,
    get:async()=>{await ready;return snapDoc(path)},
    set:async d=>{await ready;return doWrite([{op:'set',path,data:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(d))}])},
    update:async d=>{await ready;return doWrite([{op:'update',path,data:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(d))}])},
    delete:async()=>{await ready;return doWrite([{op:'delete',path}])},
    onSnapshot:(fn)=>addListener(()=>snapDoc(path),fn),
    collection:p=>colRef(path+'/'+p)}}
  function colRef(path,f=[],ob=null,lim=0){const self={path,
    where:(fld,op,v)=>colRef(path,f.concat([[fld,op,v]]),ob,lim),orderBy:(fld,dir)=>colRef(path,f,[fld,dir||'asc'],lim),limit:n=>colRef(path,f,ob,n),
    get:async()=>{await ready;return runQuery(path,f,ob,lim)},
    onSnapshot:(fn)=>addListener(()=>runQuery(path,f,ob,lim),fn),
    doc:id=>docRef(path+'/'+(id||('d'+Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)))),
    add:async d=>{const r=self.doc();await r.set(d);return r}};return self}
  const db={doc:docRef,collection:p=>colRef(p)};
  const user={id:async()=>{await ready;return session.uid},isOwner:async()=>{await ready;return session.role==='owner'},canEdit:async()=>{await ready;return session.role==='owner'||session.role==='tl'},
    can:async()=>{await ready;return session.role==='owner'},me:async()=>{await ready;return{id:session.uid,name:session.name,isOwner:session.role==='owner',canEdit:session.role!=='rep',email:null,avatarUrl:'',color:'#424965'}},
    profiles:async ids=>{const o={};(Array.isArray(ids)?ids:[ids]).forEach(i=>o[i]={id:i,name:'',avatarUrl:'',color:'#424965',email:null,isMe:i===(session&&session.uid),guest:false});return o},name:async()=>session.name};
  window.claude={use:async n=>{await ready;return n==='db'?db:n==='user'?user:null}};
  window.kaizenAdmin={list:()=>call('adminAccess',session.token,'list'),act:(a,uid,v)=>call('adminAccess',session.token,a,uid,v)};
  const FD='https://ameliahcrowe-cloud.github.io';function fdPost(m){try{window.top.postMessage(m,FD)}catch(e){}}
  function logoutLocal(){ls.del(TOK);fdPost({kz:'clear'});location.reload()}
  window.kaizenLogout=async()=>{try{if(session)await call('logout',session.token)}catch(e){}logoutLocal()};
  /* ---- login screen ---- */
  async function start(sess){session=sess;ls.set(TOK,JSON.stringify(sess));fdPost({kz:'save',v:JSON.stringify(sess)});loadOutbox();document.getElementById('login').hidden=true;
    await sync(true);sessionReady();startPolling()}
  function showLogin(info){const L=document.getElementById('login');L.hidden=false;const msg=document.getElementById('lgMsg');
    if(info){document.getElementById('lgName').textContent='Orexis Innovations';if(window.OREXIS_LOGO)info.logo=window.OREXIS_LOGO;if(info.logo){const im=document.getElementById('lgLogo');im.src=info.logo;im.hidden=false}
      const sel=document.getElementById('nwTeam');sel.replaceChildren();const o0=document.createElement('option');o0.value='';o0.textContent=info.name||'Orexis Innovations';sel.append(o0);
      (info.teams||[]).forEach(t=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=t.id;o.textContent=t.name;sel.append(o)});
      if(!info.hasOwner)msg.textContent='Setup isn’t finished yet. Ameliah: open the spreadsheet and use the Orexis KPIs menu to set your PIN.'}
    const tabIn=document.getElementById('lgTabIn'),tabNew=document.getElementById('lgTabNew'),fIn=document.getElementById('lgIn'),fNew=document.getElementById('lgNew');
    tabIn.onclick=()=>{tabIn.classList.add('on');tabNew.classList.remove('on');fIn.hidden=false;fNew.hidden=true;msg.textContent=''};
    tabNew.onclick=()=>{tabNew.classList.add('on');tabIn.classList.remove('on');fNew.hidden=false;fIn.hidden=true;msg.textContent=''};
    const busy=(f,b)=>f.querySelectorAll('button,input,select').forEach(x=>x.disabled=b);
    fIn.onsubmit=async e=>{e.preventDefault();msg.textContent='';busy(fIn,true);
      try{start(await call('login',document.getElementById('lgUser').value,document.getElementById('lgPin').value))}catch(err){msg.textContent=String(err&&err.message||err).replace(/^Error: /,'');busy(fIn,false)}};
    fNew.onsubmit=async e=>{e.preventDefault();msg.textContent='';const p1=document.getElementById('nwPin').value,p2=document.getElementById('nwPin2').value;
      if(p1!==p2){msg.textContent='The two PINs don’t match.';return}busy(fNew,true);
      try{start(await call('register',document.getElementById('nwUser').value,p1,document.getElementById('nwTeam').value))}catch(err){msg.textContent=String(err&&err.message||err).replace(/^Error: /,'');busy(fNew,false)}}}
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{});
  const saved=ls.get(TOK);let sess=null;try{sess=saved?JSON.parse(saved):null}catch(e){}
  function boot(){if(sess&&sess.token){start(sess);return}
    let done=false;const go=()=>{if(done)return;done=true;call('publicInfo').then(showLogin).catch(()=>showLogin(null))};
    window.addEventListener('message',e=>{if(done||e.origin!==FD||!e.data||e.data.kz!=='tok')return;let x=null;try{x=JSON.parse(e.data.v)}catch(_){}if(x&&x.token){done=true;sess=x;start(x)}else go()});
    if(window.top===window){go();return}fdPost({kz:'need'});setTimeout(go,900)}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot);else boot();
})();
</script>

/* ---------- helpers ---------- */
function pad(n){return String(n).padStart(2,'0')}
function toStr(d){return d.getFullYear()+'-'+pad(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+pad(d.getDate())}
function parse(s){const [y,m,d]=s.split('-').map(Number);return new Date(y,m-1,d)}
function todayStr(){return toStr(new Date())}
function addDays(s,n){const d=parse(s);d.setDate(d.getDate()+n);return toStr(d)}
function weekStart(s){const d=parse(s);d.setDate(d.getDate()-(d.getDay()+6)%7);return toStr(d)}
function monthKey(s){return s.slice(0,7)}
function niceDate(s,o){return parse(s).toLocaleDateString('en-AU',o||{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short'})}
function monthName(m){return parse(m+'-01').toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{month:'long',year:'numeric'})}
function num(v){const n=parseFloat(String(v??'').replace(/[$,\s]/g,''));return isFinite(n)?n:0}
function money(n){return '$'+Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-AU')}
function fmt(m,n){return m.money?money(n):String(Math.round(n*10)/10)}
function fmtK(k,n){const m=METRICS.find(x=>x.k===k);return m?fmt(m,n):(k==='rph'?money(n):String(n))}
function pct(a,b){return b>0?Math.round(a/b*100)+'%':'—'}
const STD_DEFAULT={bell:700,gong:1000,stops:20,conv:75,avg:65,ups:30,rph:120,silver:100,gold:120,platinum:160,diamond:200,buyersDay:12,stopsHr:3,buyersHr:2};
function std(k){const v=Number(((S&&S.std)||{})[k]);return isFinite(v)&&v>0?v:STD_DEFAULT[k]}
function isAdminUser(){return S.isOwner&&S.uid==='u_owner'}
function zone(rph){if(rph==null||!isFinite(rph))return null;
  if(rph<std('silver'))return['Red','m-red'];if(rph<std('gold'))return['Silver','m-silver'];
  if(rph<std('platinum'))return['Gold','m-gold'];if(rph<std('diamond'))return['Platinum','m-plat'];return['Diamond','m-dia']}
function h(tag,attrs,...kids){const el=document.createElement(tag);
  if(attrs)for(const k in attrs){const v=attrs[k];if(v==null||v===false)continue;
    if(k==='class')el.className=v;else if(k==='text')el.textContent=v;
    else if(k.startsWith('on'))el.addEventListener(k.slice(2),v);
    else if(k==='style')el.style.cssText=v;else el.setAttribute(k,v===true?'':v)}
  for(const c of kids.flat()){if(c==null||c===false)continue;el.append(c.nodeType?c:document.createTextNode(String(c)))}
  return el}
function zoneEl(rph){const z=zone(rph);return z?h('span',{class:'zone '+z[1],text:z[0]}):h('span',{class:'hint',text:'—'})}
let toastT;function toast(msg,ms){const t=document.getElementById('toast');t.textContent=msg;t.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(toastT);toastT=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),ms||2400)}
function status(msg,cls){const s=document.getElementById('status');if(!msg){s.hidden=true;return}s.className='notice'+(cls?' '+cls:'');s.textContent=msg;s.hidden=false}
function blank(){const o={};for(const f of LOGFIELDS)o[f.k]=0;return o}
function sumInto(acc,e){for(const f of LOGFIELDS)acc[f.k]=(acc[f.k]||0)+num(e[f.k]);return acc}
function leadRole(){return S.isOwner||S.isAdmin}
function leader(){return leadRole()&&!S.viewMe}
function repList(activeOnly){let l=repListAll(activeOnly);const f=S.teamFilter;
  if(leader()&&isAdminUser()){const mine={[S.uid]:1};downOf(S.uid).forEach(r=>mine[r.id]=1);return l.filter(r=>mine[r.id])}
  if(leader()&&f&&f!=='all')l=l.filter(r=>f==='none'?!r.team:(r.team===f||(f===S.uid&&r.id===S.uid)));return l}
function repListAll(activeOnly,incTest){return Object.entries(S.reps).map(([id,r])=>({id,...r}))
  .filter(r=>(!activeOnly||r.active!==false)).sort((a,b)=>(a.order||0)-(b.order||0)||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)))}
function repName(id){const r=S.reps[id];return (r&&r.name)||'Team member'}
function goalOf(id,week){const r=S.reps[id];return (r&&r.goals&&r.goals[week])||null}
async function write(fn,okMsg){
  try{await fn();if(okMsg)toast(okMsg);return true}
  catch(e){
    if(e&&e.code==='invalid_argument')status('Your change wasn’t saved because your access only lets you view this tracker. Ask your team leader to share it with you so you can edit.','warn');
    else if(e&&e.code==='quota_exceeded')status('The tracker is full. Ask your team leader to clear out old data.','warn');
    else toast('Could not save. Check your connection and try again.');
    return false}}

/* ---------- entries ---------- */
function rawSource(uid,kind){
  if(uid===S.uid&&S.mineReady)return Object.values(S.allMine);
  if(kind==='period')return Object.values(Object.assign({},S.allTeam[uid]||{},S.weekEntries[uid]||{}));
  return Object.values(S.weekEntries[uid]||{})}
/* confirmed figures (leader-entered) replace the rep's own numbers for that day */
function sourceAll(uid,kind){const list=rawSource(uid,kind);const c=S.conf[uid];if(!c)return list;
  const m={};list.forEach(e=>m[e.date]=e);for(const d in c){if(c[d])m[d]={...c[d],confirmed:true,self:m[d]||null}}return Object.values(m)}
/* campaigns: every day is tagged with one; the header filter narrows every view */
function campaigns(){let l=(S.campaignsCfg&&S.campaignsCfg.list)||[];if(!l.length)l=[{id:'paralympics',name:'Paralympics',active:true}];if(!l.some(c=>/wound/i.test(c.name||c.id)))l=l.concat([{id:'wounded',name:'Wounded Heroes',active:true}]);const seen={};return l.filter(c=>{const k=String(c.name||c.id).trim().toLowerCase();if(seen[k])return false;seen[k]=1;return true})}
function campStep(id){const c=campaigns().find(x=>x.id===id);const n=String((c&&c.name)||id||'');return /wound/i.test(n)?30:/paraly/i.test(n)?25:0}
function defCampaign(){const c=S.campaignsCfg||{};const l=campaigns();return(c.def&&l.some(x=>x.id===c.def))?c.def:l[0].id}
function campName(id){const c=campaigns().find(x=>x.id===id);return c?c.name:'Other'}
function campOf(e){return(e&&e.campaign)||defCampaign()}
function source(uid,kind){const l=sourceAll(uid,kind);const f=S.campFilter;return f&&f!=='all'?l.filter(e=>campOf(e)===f):l}
function rawEntry(uid,date){return rawSource(uid,'date').find(e=>e.date===date)||null}
function confEntry(uid,date){return(S.conf[uid]||{})[date]||null}
function confirmMode(uid){return leader()&&uid!==S.uid}
function weekAll(uid,wk){const t=blank();sourceAll(uid,'period').filter(e=>e.week===(wk||S.week)).forEach(e=>sumInto(t,e));return t}
function entriesIn(uid,a,b){return source(uid,'period').filter(e=>e.date>=a&&e.date<=b)}
function totalsIn(uids,a,b){const t=blank();uids.forEach(u=>entriesIn(u,a,b).forEach(e=>sumInto(t,e)));return t}
function spanGroup(a,b){const days=(parse(b)-parse(a))/864e5;return days<=14?'day':days<=100?'week':days<=800?'month':'year'}
function periodRange(){const d=parse(S.date),y=d.getFullYear(),m=d.getMonth();
  if(S.period==='day')return{a:S.date,b:S.date,label:niceDate(S.date,{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'}),group:'none'};
  if(S.period==='week')return{a:S.week,b:addDays(S.week,6),label:'Week of '+niceDate(S.week,{day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'}),group:'day'};
  if(S.period==='all'){let first=todayStr();scopeIds().forEach(u=>source(u,'period').forEach(e=>{if(e.date<first)first=e.date}));return{a:first,b:todayStr(),label:'All time',group:spanGroup(first,todayStr())==='year'?'year':'month',noNav:true}}
  if(S.period==='range'){let a=S.rangeA||addDays(todayStr(),-27),b=S.rangeB||todayStr();if(a>b)[a,b]=[b,a];return{a,b,label:niceDate(a,{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'})+' – '+niceDate(b,{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'}),group:spanGroup(a,b),noNav:true,range:true}}
  if(S.period==='quarter'){const q=Math.floor(m/3);return{a:toStr(new Date(y,q*3,1)),b:toStr(new Date(y,q*3+3,0)),label:`Q${q+1} ${y}`,group:'month'}}
  if(S.period==='ytd'||S.period==='year'){const ty=new Date().getFullYear();return{a:y+'-01-01',b:y===ty?todayStr():y+'-12-31',label:y===ty?`Year to date ${y}`:`${y} full year`,group:'month'}}
  return{a:toStr(new Date(y,m,1)),b:toStr(new Date(y,m+1,0)),label:monthName(monthKey(S.date)),group:'week'}}
function shiftPeriod(n){const d=parse(S.date);
  if(S.period==='day'){setDate(addDays(S.date,n));return}if(S.period==='week'){setDate(addDays(S.date,7*n));return}
  if(S.period==='quarter')d.setMonth(d.getMonth()+3*n,1);else if(S.period==='ytd'||S.period==='year')d.setFullYear(d.getFullYear()+n,0,1);else d.setMonth(d.getMonth()+n,1);setDate(toStr(d))}
function entriesFor(uid,kind,key){return source(uid,kind).filter(e=>e[kind]===key)}
function savedEntry(uid,date){return confirmMode(uid)?(confEntry(uid,date)||rawEntry(uid,date)):rawEntry(uid,date)}
function draftKey(uid,date){return uid+'|'+date}
function rowVals(uid,date){const saved=savedEntry(uid,date)||{};const d=S.drafts[draftKey(uid,date)]||{};const o={};
  for(const f of LOGFIELDS)o[f.k]=(f.k in d)?num(d[f.k]):num(saved[f.k]);return o}
function totalsFor(uids,kind,key){const t=blank();uids.forEach(u=>entriesFor(u,kind,key).forEach(e=>sumInto(t,e)));return t}
function daysFor(uids){const days=DAYS.map(()=>blank());uids.forEach(u=>entriesFor(u,'week',S.week).forEach(e=>{
  const i=Math.round((parse(e.date)-parse(S.week))/864e5);if(i>=0&&i<7)sumInto(days[i],e)}));return days}
function goalTotal(uids,week){const g={};METRICS.forEach(m=>g[m.k]=0);let any=false;
  uids.forEach(u=>{const x=goalOf(u,week);if(x){any=true;METRICS.forEach(m=>g[m.k]+=num(x[m.k]))}});return any?g:null}
function scopeIds(){return leader()?repList(false).filter(r=>!r.test).map(r=>r.id):(S.me?[S.uid]:[])}
function isTest(id){return !!(S.reps[id]||{}).test}

/* ---------- personal bests ---------- */
function computePB(list){const day={},week={},month={},weeks={},months={};
  for(const e of list){
    for(const [k] of PB_DAY){const v=k==='rph'?(num(e.hours)>=1?num(e.retail)/num(e.hours):0):num(e[k]);
      if(v>0&&(!day[k]||v>day[k].v))day[k]={v:Math.round(v*100)/100,date:e.date}}
    sumInto(weeks[e.week]=weeks[e.week]||blank(),e);const mk=monthKey(e.date);sumInto(months[mk]=months[mk]||blank(),e)}
  const best=(map,out,key)=>{for(const w in map){const x=map[w];for(const [k] of PB_WEEK){const v=x[k];if(v>0&&(!out[k]||v>out[k].v))out[k]={v,[key]:w}}
    if(x.hours>=1){const r=Math.round(x.retail/x.hours*100)/100;if(!out.rph||r>out.rph.v)out.rph={v:r,[key]:w}}}};
  best(weeks,week,'week');best(months,month,'month');
  return{day,week,month}}
function newRecords(oldPb,pb){const out=[];oldPb=oldPb||{day:{},week:{}};
  for(const [k,l] of PB_DAY){const a=pb.day[k],b=(oldPb.day||{})[k];if(a&&(!b||a.v>b.v)&&b)out.push(`${l} ${fmtK(k,a.v)} (best day)`)}
  for(const [k,l] of PB_WEEK){const a=pb.week[k],b=(oldPb.week||{})[k];if(a&&(!b||a.v>b.v)&&b)out.push(`${l} ${fmtK(k,a.v)} (best week)`)}
  return out}
async function recalcAllPB(){if(S.pbBusy)return;S.pbBusy=true;render();const ids=[...new Set([S.uid].concat(repList(false).map(r=>r.id)))].filter(Boolean);
  for(const id of ids){try{await refreshPB(id,true)}catch(_){}}S.pbBusy=false;toast('PBs recalculated for '+ids.length+' '+(ids.length===1?'person':'people'));render();scheduleTeamPB()}
async function refreshPB(uid,quiet){
  let list;
  if(uid===S.uid&&S.mineReady)list=sourceAll(uid,'period');
  else{try{const [a,b]=await Promise.all([S.db.collection('reps/'+uid+'/entries').limit(1000).get(),S.db.collection('conf/'+uid+'/days').limit(1000).get()]);
    const m={};a.docs.forEach(d=>m[d.id]=d.data());b.docs.forEach(d=>m[d.id]=d.data());list=Object.values(m)}catch(e){return}}
  const rep=S.reps[uid]||{};const pb=computePB(list);
  if(JSON.stringify(pb)===JSON.stringify(rep.pb||null))return;
  const recs=newRecords(rep.pb,pb);
  const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({pb}));
  if(ok){if(S.reps[uid])S.reps[uid]={...S.reps[uid],pb};if(uid===S.uid&&S.me)S.me={...S.me,pb};if(recs.length&&!quiet)toast('New PB! '+recs.join(' · '),4000)}}

function preserveFocus(fn){const a=document.activeElement;const id=a&&a.id;let s=null,e=null;
  try{if(a&&a.tagName==='INPUT'&&a.type==='text'){s=a.selectionStart;e=a.selectionEnd}}catch(_){}
  fn();if(id){const n=document.getElementById(id);if(n&&n!==a){n.focus();try{if(s!=null)n.setSelectionRange(s,e)}catch(_){}}}}

const H_SYNC=['stops','buyers','retail','upsells','upsellRetail','medals'];
async function pullHourlyOne(uid,date){let d;try{const r=await S.db.doc(`reps/${uid}/hourly/${date}`).get();d=r.exists?r.data():null}catch(_){d=null}
  if(!d||!d.slots||!Object.keys(d.slots).length)return 'none';
  const t={};H_SYNC.forEach(k=>t[k]=0);let n=0;for(const k in d.slots){const x=d.slots[k]||{};if(H_SYNC.some(f=>num(x[f])))n++;H_SYNC.forEach(f=>t[f]+=num(x[f]))}
  const prev=rawEntry(uid,date)||{};const body={...prev,...t,date,week:weekStart(date),month:monthKey(date),campaign:prev.campaign||campFor(uid,date,null,prev),hours:num(prev.hours)>0?num(prev.hours):(num(d.targets&&d.targets.shiftHours)||8),fromHourly:true,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
  const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc(`reps/${uid}/entries/${date}`).set(body));if(!ok)return 'fail';
  delete S.drafts[draftKey(uid,date)];refreshPB(uid,true);pushBoard(uid,body.week);try{await S.db.doc(`reps/${uid}/hourly/${date}`).set({...d,syncedAt:body.updatedAt})}catch(_){}return 'ok'}
async function syncFromHourly(){if(S.hSyncBusy)return;S.hSyncBusy=true;render();
  const ids=leader()?repList(true).map(r=>r.id):[S.uid];let ok=0,none=0;
  for(const u of ids){const r=await pullHourlyOne(u,S.date);if(r==='ok')ok++;else if(r==='none')none++}
  S.hSyncBusy=false;scheduleSummary&&scheduleSummary();
  toast(ok?(leader()?'Synced '+ok+' '+(ok===1?'person':'people')+' from Orexis Hourly':'Synced from Orexis Hourly'):'Nothing in Orexis Hourly for this day yet',3500);render()}

    h('button',{'aria-label':'Next day',onclick:()=>setDate(addDays(S.date,1))},'›'))}

  root.querySelectorAll('table').forEach(t=>{const th=[...t.querySelectorAll('thead th')].map(x=>x.textContent.trim());if(th.length<4)return;t.classList.add('cards');
    t.querySelectorAll('tbody tr').forEach(tr=>{let i=0;[...tr.children].forEach(td=>{td.setAttribute('data-label',th[i]||'');i+=Number(td.getAttribute('colspan')||1)})})})}
(function(){let x0=null,y0=null,t0=0;document.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>{if(e.touches.length!==1){x0=null;return}const tg=e.target;if(tg.closest&&tg.closest('input,select,textarea,.scroll,svg,#mnav,#msheet')){x0=null;return}x0=e.touches[0].clientX;y0=e.touches[0].clientY;t0=Date.now()},{passive:true});
  document.addEventListener('touchend',e=>{if(x0==null)return;const dx=e.changedTouches[0].clientX-x0,dy=e.changedTouches[0].clientY-y0;x0=null;
    if(Date.now()-t0<600&&Math.abs(dx)>70&&Math.abs(dy)<45&&(S.tab==='log'||S.tab==='reflect'))setDate(addDays(S.date,dx<0?1:-1))},{passive:true});

function logInput(uid,f,cls){const key=draftKey(uid,S.date);const saved=savedEntry(uid,S.date);const d=S.drafts[key]||{};
  const raw=(f.k in d)?d[f.k]:(saved?String(num(saved[f.k])):'');
  return h('input',{type:'text',inputmode:'decimal',class:'num'+(f.money?' money':'')+((f.k in d)?' dirty':'')+(cls?' '+cls:''),id:`in-${uid}-${f.k}`,'aria-label':`${repName(uid)} ${f.l}`,value:raw,placeholder:f.money?'$0':'0',
    oninput:ev=>{const dd=(S.drafts[key]=S.drafts[key]||{});dd[f.k]=ev.target.value;
      const sv=saved?String(num(saved[f.k])):'';if(ev.target.value===sv){delete dd[f.k];if(!Object.keys(dd).length)delete S.drafts[key]}render();
      if(uid===S.uid&&!leader()){clearTimeout(S.asT);S.asT=setTimeout(()=>{if(S.drafts[key]&&S.date===key.split('|')[1])saveRow(uid)},2500)}},
    onkeydown:ev=>{if(ev.key==='Enter'){ev.preventDefault();saveRow(uid)}}})}
function repDoc(uid){return uid===S.uid?(S.me||S.reps[uid]||{}):(S.reps[uid]||{})}
function plannedCamp(uid,date){const r=repDoc(uid);const p=r.campPlan||{};const ok=id=>id&&campaigns().some(c=>c.id===id);return ok(p[date])?p[date]:ok(r.campaign)?r.campaign:null}
function campFor(uid,date,key,saved){const d=(key&&S.drafts[key])||{};return d.campaign||(saved&&saved.campaign)||plannedCamp(uid,date)||(uid===S.uid&&S.lastCamp)||defCampaign()}
async function setPlan(uid,date,camp){const r=repDoc(uid);const p={...(r.campPlan||{})};if(camp)p[date]=camp;else delete p[date];
  const cut=addDays(todayStr(),-120);for(const k in p)if(k<cut)delete p[k];
  if(S.reps[uid])S.reps[uid]={...S.reps[uid],campPlan:p};if(uid===S.uid&&S.me)S.me={...S.me,campPlan:p};render();
  await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({campPlan:p}))}

  await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({campaign:camp||null}),'Usual campaign saved')}

function tgText(uid,date){const t=rowVals(uid,date);const r=x=>Math.round(x*100)/100;const H='#\uFE0F\u20E3 ';
  const L=[['RETAIL','$'+r(t.retail)],['HOURS',String(r(t.hours))],['RPH',t.hours>0?'$'+Math.round(t.retail/t.hours):'$0'],['STOPS',String(t.stops)],['BUYERS',String(t.buyers)],
    ['AVG',t.buyers>0?'$'+Math.round(t.retail/t.buyers):'$0'],['MEDALS',String(t.medals)],['CONV',t.stops>0?Math.round(t.buyers/t.stops*100)+'%':'0%'],['UPSELLS',String(t.upsells)],
    ['CASH','$'+r(t.cash)],['EFTPOS','$'+r(t.eftpos)],['UPSELL AMOUNT','$'+r(t.upsellRetail)],['TRANSACTION FEE','']];
  return 'FINAL KPIs\n\n'+L.map(([k,v])=>H+k+': '+v).join('\n')}
async function copyTG(uid){const txt=tgText(uid,S.date);let ok=false;
  try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);ok=true}catch(_){}
  if(!ok){const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=txt;ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';document.body.append(ta);ta.select();try{ok=document.execCommand('copy')}catch(_){}ta.remove()}
  if(ok)toast('Copied. Paste it into Final KPI\u2019s on Telegram');else{S.tgShow=txt;render()}}

/* ---------- monthly target, streaks, milestones ---------- */
function monthGoal(uid,mk){const r=uid===S.uid?(S.me||S.reps[uid]):S.reps[uid];return num(r&&r.monthGoals&&r.monthGoals[mk])}
async function setMonthGoal(uid,mk,v){const r=(uid===S.uid?S.me:S.reps[uid])||{};const mg={...(r.monthGoals||{}),[mk]:num(v)};
  const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({monthGoals:mg}),'Monthly target saved');
  if(ok){if(S.reps[uid])S.reps[uid]={...S.reps[uid],monthGoals:mg};if(uid===S.uid&&S.me)S.me={...S.me,monthGoals:mg};S.mgEdit=false;render()}}
function monthPanel(uid){const mk=monthKey(S.date);const list=sourceAll(uid,'period');const t=blank();list.filter(e=>monthKey(e.date)===mk).forEach(e=>sumInto(t,e));
  const goal=monthGoal(uid,mk);const [y,m]=mk.split('-').map(Number);const dim=new Date(y,m,0).getDate();const tk=monthKey(todayStr());
  const dayNo=mk<tk?dim:mk>tk?0:Number(todayStr().slice(8,10));const exp=goal*dayNo/dim;
  const st=!goal?null:t.retail>=goal?'ahead':exp<=0?'on':t.retail>=exp*1.05?'ahead':t.retail>=exp*0.95?'on':'behind';
  const lbl={ahead:t.retail>=goal&&goal?'Goal hit':'Ahead',on:'On track',behind:'Behind'};
  const pctv=goal?Math.min(100,t.retail/goal*100):0;
  const edit=S.mgEdit||!goal;const inp=h('input',{type:'number',min:0,step:100,inputmode:'decimal',value:goal||'',placeholder:'e.g. 25000','aria-label':'Monthly retail target',style:'width:130px'});
  const days=dim-dayNo;const need=goal&&days>0?Math.max(0,goal-t.retail)/days:0;
  return h('div',{class:'panel mprog'},
    h('div',{class:'row'},h('div',null,h('div',{class:'hint',text:monthName(mk)+' retail target'}),h('div',null,h('span',{class:'amt',text:money(t.retail)}),goal?h('span',{class:'hint',text:'  of '+money(goal)}):null)),
      st?h('span',{class:'mstat '+st,text:lbl[st]}):null),
    goal?h('div',{class:'mbar '+(st||''),role:'progressbar','aria-valuenow':Math.round(pctv),'aria-valuemin':0,'aria-valuemax':100},h('i',{style:'width:'+pctv.toFixed(1)+'%'}),exp>0&&exp<goal?h('b',{style:'left:'+(exp/goal*100).toFixed(1)+'%',title:'Where you should be today'}):null):null,
    goal?h('div',{class:'row hint'},h('span',{text:Math.round(t.retail/goal*100)+'% done · should be at '+money(exp)+' by today'}),h('span',{text:t.retail>=goal?'Target smashed':days>0?money(need)+' a day for the last '+days+' day'+(days>1?'s':''):''})):null,
    edit?h('div',{class:'row'},h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center'},inp,h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>setMonthGoal(uid,mk,inp.value)},'Set target'),goal?h('button',{onclick:()=>{S.mgEdit=false;render()}},'Cancel'):null)):
      h('div',null,h('button',{class:'linkbtn small',onclick:()=>{S.mgEdit=true;render()}},'Change target')))}
const MS_DAY=[2000,2500,3000,3500,5000],MS_WEEK=[5000,8000,10000,12000,15000,20000];
function streakPanel(uid){const list=sourceAll(uid,'period').filter(e=>num(e.hours)>0||num(e.stops)>0).sort((a,b)=>a.date<b.date?-1:1);
  const run=test=>{let cur=0,best=0;for(const e of list){if(test(e)){cur++;if(cur>best)best=cur}else cur=0}return[cur,best]};
  const rph=e=>num(e.hours)>0?num(e.retail)/num(e.hours):0;
  const S1=[['🔔 Bell days',run(e=>num(e.retail)>=(e.roadie?1000:700))],['Gold+ RPH days',run(e=>rph(e)>=(e.date<'2025-10-01'?100:120))],['Platinum+ RPH days',run(e=>rph(e)>=160)],['$1k+ retail days',run(e=>num(e.retail)>=1000)],['Medal days',run(e=>num(e.medals)>0)]];
  const wk={};list.forEach(e=>{wk[e.week]=(wk[e.week]||0)+num(e.retail)});const wv=Object.values(wk);
  const kf=x=>'$'+(x/1000)+'k';
  const tier=(label,steps,vals)=>{const best=vals.length?Math.max(...vals):0;const next=steps.find(x=>best<x);
    return h('div',{class:'streak'},h('div',{class:'hint',text:label}),h('div',{class:'n',text:money(best)}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Best so far'}),
      next?h('div',{class:'prog',style:'margin:6px 0 2px'},h('i',{style:'width:'+Math.min(100,best/next*100).toFixed(1)+'%'})):null,
      h('div',{class:'hint',text:next?'Next: '+kf(next)+' ('+money(next-best)+' to go)':'Every milestone hit!'}),
      h('div',{class:'badges',style:'margin-top:4px'},steps.map(x=>{const n=vals.filter(v=>v>=x).length;return h('span',{class:'badge'+(n?' got':''),text:kf(x)+(n?' ×'+n:'')})})))};
  const ms=[tier('Day retail milestones',MS_DAY,list.map(e=>num(e.retail))),tier('Week retail milestones',MS_WEEK,wv)];
  return h('div',null,
    h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Streaks'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Counted over days you worked, in a row.'})),
    h('div',{class:'streaks'},S1.map(([l,[c,b]])=>h('div',{class:'streak'},h('div',{class:'hint',text:l}),h('div',{class:'n',text:c+(c>=3?' 🔥':'')}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Best ever: '+b})))),
    h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Milestones'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'×2 means you\u2019ve hit it twice.'})),h('div',{class:'streaks',style:'grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(260px,1fr))'},ms))}
function step25Bad(camp,t){const st=campStep(camp);if(!st)return null;const b=[['retail','Retail'],['upsellRetail','Upsell retail']].find(([k])=>num(t[k])%st!==0);return b?b[1]:null}
function cashMismatch(t){const c=num(t.cash)+num(t.eftpos);return c>0&&Math.abs(c-num(t.retail))>0.5?c:null}
function noteRow(m,multi){const cur=((repDoc(m.id).coachNotes||{})[S.date]||{}).text||'';const ta=h('textarea',{rows:2,placeholder:'e.g. Loved your stopping today! Now slow down the close','aria-label':'Coaching note for '+m.name,style:'width:100%;min-width:220px;font:inherit;box-sizing:border-box;padding:8px;border-radius:10px'});ta.value=cur;
  return h('tr',null,h('td',{colspan:String(LOGFIELDS.length+(multi?8:7))},h('div',{style:'display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start;max-width:420px;position:sticky;left:0'},ta,h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:async()=>{const txt=ta.value.trim();const r=repDoc(m.id);const cn={...(r.coachNotes||{})};
    if(txt)cn[S.date]={text:txt,by:(S.me&&S.me.name)||'Your leader',at:new Date().toISOString()};else cn[S.date]=null;const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+m.id).update({coachNotes:cn}),txt?'Note sent to '+m.name:'Note removed');
    if(ok){if(S.reps[m.id])S.reps[m.id]={...S.reps[m.id],coachNotes:cn};S.noteFor=null;render()}}},'Send'))))}
function promoBanner(uid){const r=repDoc(uid);const pr=r.promo||{};if(!pr.promotedAt||(r.promoSeen||'')>=pr.promotedAt)return null;const st=STAGES.find(x=>x.k===pr.promotedTo)||STAGES[stageIdx(uid)];
  const dismiss=()=>{const at=pr.promotedAt;if(S.me)S.me={...S.me,promoSeen:at};if(S.reps[uid])S.reps[uid]={...S.reps[uid],promoSeen:at};render();write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({promoSeen:at}))};
  const team=stageIdx(uid)>=2&&!S.teams[uid];
  return h('div',{class:'panel',style:'margin-bottom:12px;border:2px solid var(--accent);text-align:center'},h('div',{style:'font-size:2rem'},'🎉'),h('strong',{style:'font-size:1.15rem;display:block',text:'Congrats! You’re now a '+st.from+'!'}),
    h('div',{class:'hint',style:'margin:4px 0 10px',text:team?'Time to build your team. Give it a name and a logo!':'So proud of you. Check out your next step on your Promotion page.'}),
    h('div',{style:'display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:center;flex-wrap:wrap'},team?h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>{dismiss();go('myteam')}},'Create my team'):h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>{dismiss();go('promo')}},'See my next step'),h('button',{onclick:dismiss},'Thank you!')))}
function coachNoteCard(uid){const r=repDoc(uid);const cn=r.coachNotes||{};const seen=r.coachSeen||'';const ks=Object.keys(cn).filter(k=>cn[k]&&cn[k].text&&k<=todayStr()&&k>addDays(todayStr(),-8)).sort();const k=ks[ks.length-1];if(!k||(cn[k].at||k)<=seen)return null;
  return h('div',{class:'notice'},h('strong',{text:'💬 Note from '+(cn[k].by||'your leader')+' ('+niceDate(k,{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short'})+'): '}),h('span',{text:cn[k].text}),' ',h('button',{class:'linkbtn small',onclick:async()=>{const at=cn[k].at||k;if(S.me)S.me={...S.me,coachSeen:at};if(S.reps[uid])S.reps[uid]={...S.reps[uid],coachSeen:at};render();write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({coachSeen:at}))}},'Got it'))}
function monthCheckCard(uid){const mk=monthKey(todayStr());if(monthKey(S.date)!==mk)return null;const r=repDoc(uid);const goal=monthGoal(uid,mk);const plan=(r.monthPlans||{})[mk];
  if(goal&&plan)return S.mcOpen?null:h('div',{class:'hint',style:'margin:-4px 0 10px'},'This month: '+money(goal)+' · Plan: '+plan+' ',h('button',{class:'linkbtn small',onclick:()=>{S.mcOpen=true;render()}},'change'));
  const mname=new Date(mk+'-01T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{month:'long'});
  const gi=h('input',{type:'number',min:0,step:100,inputmode:'decimal',placeholder:'e.g. 20000','aria-label':'Retail target this month',value:goal||''});
  const pi=h('textarea',{rows:2,placeholder:'What are you going to do differently this month?','aria-label':'Your plan this month',style:'width:100%;font:inherit;box-sizing:border-box;padding:8px;border-radius:10px'});pi.value=plan||'';
  return h('div',{class:'panel',style:'margin-bottom:12px;border-color:var(--accent);padding:14px'},h('strong',{text:mname+' check-in 🎯'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'What’s your target this month, and what are you doing differently to get there?'}),
    h('label',{style:'display:block;margin-top:8px'},'Retail target ',gi),h('div',{style:'margin-top:8px'},pi),
    h('button',{class:'primary',style:'margin-top:8px',onclick:async()=>{const g=num(gi.value),pl=pi.value.trim();if(!g){toast('Set your target first');return}if(!pl){toast('Pop in how you’re going to get there');return}
      const mg={...(r.monthGoals||{}),[mk]:g},mp={...(r.monthPlans||{}),[mk]:pl};const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({monthGoals:mg,monthPlans:mp}),'Month locked in');
      if(ok){const nr={...r,monthGoals:mg,monthPlans:mp};if(S.reps[uid])S.reps[uid]=nr;if(uid===S.uid&&S.me)S.me={...S.me,monthGoals:mg,monthPlans:mp};S.mcOpen=false;render()}}},'Lock it in 🔒'))}
function renderLog(v){
  const title=niceDate(S.date,{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long'});
  if(!leader()){ // rep form
    const uid=S.uid,key=draftKey(uid,S.date),saved=savedEntry(uid,S.date),dirty=!!S.drafts[key],t=rowVals(uid,S.date);
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:title}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Log your numbers for the day. Hours drive your RPH zone.'}))));
    {const pb=promoBanner(uid);if(pb)v.append(pb)}{const cn=coachNoteCard(uid);if(cn)v.append(cn)}{const mc=monthCheckCard(uid);if(mc)v.append(mc)}
    const cf=confEntry(uid,S.date);
    if(cf)v.append(h('div',{class:'notice'},h('strong',{text:'Your leader confirmed today: '}),
      LOGFIELDS.map(f=>`${f.l} ${fmt(f,num(cf[f.k]))}`).join(' · '),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Confirmed numbers are what count in your totals, bonus and PBs. What you log below stays as your own record.'})));
    const r=rateCells(t);
    const cs=campSelect(uid,key,saved);
    v.append(h('div',{class:'panel'},cs?h('div',{class:'field'},h('label',{for:'camp-'+uid,text:'Campaign'}),cs):null,h('div',{class:'form'},LOGFIELDS.map(f=>h('label',{for:`in-${uid}-${f.k}`},f.l,logInput(uid,f)))),
      h('div',{class:'formfoot'},h('div',{class:'chips'},
        h('span',null,'Conv ',h('b',{text:r[0]})),h('span',null,'Upsell ',h('b',{text:r[1]})),
        h('span',null,'$av ',h('b',{text:t.buyers>0?money(t.retail/t.buyers):'—'})),h('span',null,'Medal ',h('b',{text:r[2]})),h('span',null,'RPH ',h('b',{text:r[3]})),zoneEl(t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:null),step25Bad(campFor(uid,S.date,key,saved),t)?h('span',{class:'cashwarn',text:'⚠ '+step25Bad(campFor(uid,S.date,key,saved),t)+' must be in $'+campStep(campFor(uid,S.date,key,saved))+' steps'}):null,cashMismatch(t)!=null?h('span',{class:'cashwarn',text:'⚠ Cash + EFTPOS = '+money(cashMismatch(t))+', retail is '+money(t.retail)}):null),
        h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center'},saved||dirty?h('button',{id:'copyTG',onclick:()=>copyTG(uid)},'Copy for Telegram'):null,dirty?h('button',{class:'primary',id:'saveMine',onclick:()=>saveRow(uid)},saved?'Save changes':'Save day'):h('span',{class:saved?'saved':'hint',text:saved?'Saved':'Not logged yet'})))));
    const tb=tgBox();if(tb)v.append(tb);
    revNudge(v);v.append(h('div',{class:'bar',id:'reflAnchor'},h('div',null,h('h3',{text:'Daily reflection'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Do this at the end of each shift. Only you and your leader see it.'}))));reflForm(v,uid);
    v.append(monthPanel(uid));v.append(streakPanel(uid));
    v.append(legend());return}
  const ros=repList(true);
  const dirtyIds=Object.keys(S.drafts).filter(k=>k.endsWith('|'+S.date));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:title}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Reps log their own numbers. Anything you save here is stored as confirmed figures: it never overwrites what they logged, and it\u2019s what counts.'})),
    ros.length?h('button',{class:'primary',id:'saveAll',disabled:!dirtyIds.length,onclick:saveAll},dirtyIds.length?`Save ${dirtyIds.length} change${dirtyIds.length>1?'s':''}`:'All saved'):null));
  if(!ros.length){v.append(noReps());return}
  const multi=campaigns().length>1;
  const head=h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Name'}),multi?h('th',{text:'Campaign'}):null,LOGFIELDS.map(f=>h('th',{text:f.l})),['Conv %','Upsell %','Medal %','RPH','RPH zone',''].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})));
  const body=h('tbody');const tot=blank();
  for(const m of ros){const key=draftKey(m.id,S.date);const saved=savedEntry(m.id,S.date);const t=rowVals(m.id,S.date);if(!m.test)sumInto(tot,t);
    const own=rawEntry(m.id,S.date),cf=confirmMode(m.id)?confEntry(m.id,S.date):null;
    body.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:m.name}),multi?h('td',null,campSelect(m.id,key,saved)):null,LOGFIELDS.map(f=>{const diff=cf&&own&&num(own[f.k])!==num(cf[f.k]);
        const ed=S.editRow===m.id||!!S.drafts[key];return h('td',null,ed?logInput(m.id,f):h('span',{text:(own||cf||num(t[f.k]))?fmt(f,num(t[f.k])):'—'}),diff?h('div',{class:'hint small diff',text:'logged '+fmt(f,num(own[f.k]))}):null)}),
      rateCells(t).map(x=>h('td',{class:'derived',text:x})),h('td',null,zoneEl(t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:null)),
      h('td',null,m.id===S.uid?h('button',{class:'linkbtn small',onclick:()=>{S.viewMe=true;try{localStorage.setItem('kz_view','me')}catch(x){}onWeekChange();render()}},'Edit in 👤 Personal'):S.drafts[key]?h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px'},h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:async()=>{await saveRow(m.id);S.editRow=null;render()}},cf||!confirmMode(m.id)?'Save':'Confirm'),h('button',{onclick:()=>{delete S.drafts[key];S.editRow=null;render()}},'Cancel')):S.editRow===m.id?h('button',{onclick:()=>{S.editRow=null;render()}},'Done'):h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap'},h('button',{class:'small',onclick:()=>{S.editRow=m.id;render()}},'✏️ Edit'),
        cf?h('span',{class:'saved'},'✓ Confirmed ',h('button',{class:'linkbtn small',onclick:()=>unconfirm(m.id)},'undo')):
        own?h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Rep logged'}):h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Not logged'}),
        (own||cf)?h('button',{class:'linkbtn small',onclick:()=>copyTG(m.id)},'Copy for Telegram'):null,
        h('button',{class:'linkbtn small',onclick:()=>{S.noteFor=S.noteFor===m.id?null:m.id;render()}},(((repDoc(m.id).coachNotes||{})[S.date]||{}).text?'💬 Edit note':'💬 Note'))))));if(S.noteFor===m.id)body.append(noteRow(m,multi))}
  body.append(h('tr',{class:'total'},h('td',{text:'Team total'}),multi?h('td'):null,LOGFIELDS.map(f=>h('td',{text:fmt(f,tot[f.k])})),
    rateCells(tot).map(x=>h('td',{text:x})),h('td',null,zoneEl(tot.hours>0?tot.retail/tot.hours:null)),h('td')));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,head),body)));const tb=tgBox();if(tb)v.append(tb);
  v.append(legend())}
function noReps(){return h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('strong',{text:'No one has joined yet'}),
  h('span',{text:'Share this tracker with your team. Each person opens the link, enters their name and starts logging.'}),
  h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>go('team')},'How to add your team'))}
function legend(){return h('div',{class:'legend'},h('span',{text:'RPH zones:'}),
  [['Red','m-red','under $100'],['Silver','m-silver','$100–119'],['Gold','m-gold','$120–159'],['Platinum','m-plat','$160–199'],['Diamond','m-dia','$200+']].map(([n,c,r])=>h('span',null,h('span',{class:'zone '+c,text:n}),' '+r)),
  h('span',{text:'Conv % = buyers ÷ stops · Upsell % = upsells ÷ buyers · Medal % = medals ÷ buyers'}))}
async function saveRow(uid){const key=draftKey(uid,S.date);if(!S.drafts[key])return;
  const vals=rowVals(uid,S.date);{const cp=campFor(uid,S.date,key,savedEntry(uid,S.date));const bad=step25Bad(cp,vals);if(bad){{const st=campStep(cp);toast(bad+' must be in $'+st+' steps on '+campName(cp)+' (e.g. $'+st+', $'+st*2+', $'+st*3+'). Fix it and save again.',5000)};return}}const mm=cashMismatch(vals);if(mm!=null&&S.cashOk!==key+mm){S.cashOk=key+mm;toast('Cash + EFTPOS ('+money(mm)+') doesn\u2019t match retail ('+money(vals.retail)+'). Fix it, or tap Save again to save anyway.',5000);return}const sv=savedEntry(uid,S.date)||rawEntry(uid,S.date);const campaign=campFor(uid,S.date,key,sv);
  const body={date:S.date,week:weekStart(S.date),month:monthKey(S.date),campaign,...vals,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
  if(confirmMode(uid)){body.confirmedAt=body.updatedAt;
    const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc(`conf/${uid}/days/${S.date}`).set(body),repName(uid)+' confirmed');
    if(ok){(S.conf[uid]=S.conf[uid]||{})[S.date]=body;delete S.drafts[key];render();refreshPB(uid);pushBoard(uid,body.week);scheduleSummary();scheduleTeamPB()}return}
  const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc(`reps/${uid}/entries/${S.date}`).set(body),'Saved');
  if(ok){if(uid===S.uid&&S.mineReady)S.allMine[S.date]=body;else (S.weekEntries[uid]=S.weekEntries[uid]||{})[S.date]=body;
    if(S.allTeamReady&&uid!==S.uid)(S.allTeam[uid]=S.allTeam[uid]||{})[S.date]=body;
    delete S.drafts[key];render();refreshPB(uid);pushBoard(uid,body.week);scheduleTeamPB()}}
async function unconfirm(uid){const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc(`conf/${uid}/days/${S.date}`).delete(),'Back to '+repName(uid)+'\u2019s own numbers');
  if(ok){if(S.conf[uid])delete S.conf[uid][S.date];render();refreshPB(uid);pushBoard(uid,weekStart(S.date));scheduleSummary();scheduleTeamPB()}}
async function saveAll(){const ks=Object.keys(S.drafts).filter(k=>k.endsWith('|'+S.date));for(const k of ks)await saveRow(k.split('|')[0])}

/* ---------- WEEK ---------- */
function tilesFor(t,g){return h('div',{class:'tiles'},HMETRICS.map(m=>{const goal=g?g[m.k]:0;const a=t[m.k];
  return h('div',{class:'panel tile'},h('div',{class:'lbl',text:m.l}),h('div',{class:'big',text:fmt(m,a)}),
    goal>0?h('div',{class:'prog'+(a>=goal?' hit':'')},h('i',{style:`width:${Math.min(100,a/goal*100)}%`})):null,
    goal>0&&a>=goal?h('div',{class:'achieved',text:'✓ Goal achieved · '+pct(a,goal)}):(g===undefined||goal>0)?h('div',{class:'of',text:goal>0?`of ${fmt(m,goal)} goal · ${pct(a,goal)}`:'No goal set'}):null)}))}
function goalsHit(t,g){if(!g)return null;t={...t,rph:t.hours?t.retail/t.hours:0};let set=0,hit=0;GMETRICS.forEach(m=>{const x=num(g[m.k]);if(x>0){set++;if(t[m.k]>=x)hit++}});return set?{set,hit}:null}
function hitEl(gh){if(!gh)return h('span',{class:'hint',text:'—'});return h('span',{class:gh.hit===gh.set?'achieved':null,text:`${gh.hit===gh.set?'✓ ':''}${gh.hit} of ${gh.set}`})}
function ratesFor(t,label){const rph=t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:null;return h('div',{class:'panel rates'},
  rate('Conversion',pct(t.buyers,t.stops),'buyers ÷ stops'),rate('Upsell rate',pct(t.upsells,t.buyers),'upsells ÷ buyers'),
  rate('Medal ratio',pct(t.medals,t.buyers),'medals ÷ buyers'),rate('Avg sale',t.buyers>0?money(t.retail/t.buyers):'—','retail ÷ buyers'),
  rate(label||'RPH',rph!=null?money(rph):'—',null,zoneEl(rph)))}
function rate(l,val,f,extra){return h('div',{class:'rate'},h('div',{class:'lbl',text:l}),h('div',{class:'v',text:val}),f?h('div',{class:'f',text:f}):null,extra||null)}

/* ---------- weekly wrap for Telegram ---------- */
function docStreak(d){if(!d||!d.slots)return 0;let run=0,best=0;const won=x=>x&&((num(x.stops)>=3)+(num(x.buyers)>=2)+(num(x.retail)>=150))>=2;
  const last=Math.max(0,...Object.keys(d.slots).map(Number));for(let i=1;i<=last;i++){const x=d.slots[String(i)];if(won(x)){run++;best=Math.max(best,run)}else run=0}return best}
function weeklyWrapText(){const wk=S.week,end=addDays(wk,6);const ids=repList(false).filter(r=>!r.test).map(r=>r.id);const t=totalsIn(ids,wk,end);
  const rows=ids.map(u=>({u,t:totalsIn([u],wk,end)})).filter(x=>x.t.retail>0).sort((a,b)=>b.t.retail-a.t.retail);
  let bestDay=null;ids.forEach(u=>entriesIn(u,wk,end).forEach(e=>{if(!bestDay||num(e.retail)>bestDay.v)bestDay={u,v:num(e.retail),d:e.date}}));
  let bestRph=null;rows.forEach(x=>{if(x.t.hours>=4){const r=x.t.retail/x.t.hours;if(!bestRph||r>bestRph.v)bestRph={u:x.u,v:r}}});
  let streak=null;const all=[];ids.forEach(u=>(S.hAll&&S.hAll[u]||[]).forEach(d=>{if(d.date>=wk&&d.date<=end){all.push(d);const b=docStreak(d);if(b>=2&&(!streak||b>streak.v))streak={u,v:b,d:d.date}}}));
  const by=hourBuckets(all,wk,end);const hk=Object.keys(by).map(Number);const bh=hk.length?hk.reduce((p,k)=>by[k].retail/by[k].n>by[p].retail/by[p].n?k:p,hk[0]):null;
  const md=['🥇','🥈','🥉'];const L=['WEEKLY WRAP','WC '+niceDate(wk,{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'}),'',
    '💰 TEAM RETAIL: '+money(t.retail),'⏱ TEAM RPH: '+(t.hours?money(t.retail/t.hours):'—'),'🛑 STOPS: '+t.stops+'  ·  🛍 BUYERS: '+t.buyers+'  ·  CONV: '+(t.stops?Math.round(t.buyers/t.stops*100)+'%':'—'),'🏅 MEDALS: '+t.medals,''];
  if(rows.length){L.push('TOP RETAIL');rows.slice(0,3).forEach((x,i)=>L.push(md[i]+' '+repName(x.u)+' — '+money(x.t.retail)));L.push('')}
  if(bestDay)L.push('🚀 BEST DAY: '+repName(bestDay.u)+' — '+money(bestDay.v)+' ('+niceDate(bestDay.d,{weekday:'short'})+')');
  if(bestRph)L.push('⚡ BEST RPH: '+repName(bestRph.u)+' — '+money(bestRph.v));
  if(streak)L.push('🔥 LONGEST STREAK: '+repName(streak.u)+' — '+streak.v+' hours won in a row');
  if(bh!=null)L.push('🕐 BEST HOUR: '+hlab(bh)+'–'+hlab((bh+1)%24)+' ('+money(by[bh].retail/by[bh].n)+' avg)');
  return L.join('\n').replace(/\n+$/,'')}
async function copyWrap(){loadHourlyAll(repList(false).map(r=>r.id));const txt=weeklyWrapText();let ok=false;try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);ok=true}catch(_){}
  if(!ok){const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=txt;ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';document.body.append(ta);ta.select();try{ok=document.execCommand('copy')}catch(_){}ta.remove()}
  if(ok)toast('Weekly wrap copied. Paste it into Telegram');else{S.tgShow=txt;render()}}
function testPanel(v,kind){if(!leader())return;const T=repListAll(true,true).filter(r=>r.test&&(S.isOwner||r.team===S.uid));if(!T.length)return;
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'🧪 Test people · not counted'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:kind==='week'?'Their week so far. Left out of every team total, leaderboard and PB.':'Left out of the team total above, the leaderboard and PBs.'})));
  const tb=h('tbody');T.forEach(m=>{const key=draftKey(m.id,S.date);const t=kind==='week'?totalsFor([m.id],'week',S.week):rowVals(m.id,S.date);const ed=kind==='day'&&(S.editRow===m.id||!!S.drafts[key]);
    tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:m.name}),LOGFIELDS.map(f=>h('td',null,ed?logInput(m.id,f):h('span',{text:fmt(f,num(t[f.k]))}))),h('td',{class:'derived',text:t.hours>0?money(t.retail/t.hours):'—'}),h('td',null,zoneEl(t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:null)),
      kind==='day'?h('td',null,S.drafts[key]?h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px'},h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:async()=>{await saveRow(m.id);S.editRow=null;render()}},'Save'),h('button',{onclick:()=>{delete S.drafts[key];S.editRow=null;render()}},'Cancel')):S.editRow===m.id?h('button',{onclick:()=>{S.editRow=null;render()}},'Done'):h('button',{class:'small',onclick:()=>{S.editRow=m.id;render()}},'✏️ Edit')):null))});
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Name'}),LOGFIELDS.map(f=>h('th',{text:f.l})),h('th',{text:'RPH'}),h('th',{text:'Zone'}),kind==='day'?h('th'):null)),tb)))}
function renderWeek(v){renderWeek0(v)}
function renderWeek0(v){
  if(leader()){loadHourlyAll(repList(false).map(r=>r.id));v.append(h('div',{style:'display:flex;justify-content:flex-end'},h('button',{id:'copyWrap',onclick:copyWrap},'Copy weekly wrap for Telegram')));const tb0=tgBox();if(tb0)v.append(tb0)}
  const ids=scopeIds();const t=totalsFor(ids,'week',S.week);const g=goalTotal(ids,S.week);
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:(leader()?'Team · ':'My week · ')+'week of '+niceDate(S.week,{day:'numeric',month:'long'})}),
    h('div',{class:'hint',text:g?(leader()?'Team outcome against the sum of everyone’s goals.':'Your outcome against your goals.'):'No goals set for this week yet. Add them on the goals tab.'})),
    h('div',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px'},h('button',{onclick:()=>setDate(addDays(S.week,-7))},'‹ Last week'),h('button',{onclick:()=>setDate(addDays(S.week,7))},'Next week ›'))));
  const gh=goalsHit(t,g);
  if(gh)v.append(h('div',{class:'notice'+(gh.hit===gh.set?' win':'')},gh.hit===gh.set?`✓ All ${gh.set} weekly goals achieved.`:`${gh.hit} of ${gh.set} weekly goals achieved so far.`));
  v.append(tilesFor(t,g));v.append(ratesFor(t,leader()?'Team RPH':'My RPH'));
  if(!leader())v.append(feePanel(weekAll(S.uid),'My'));
  const box=h('div',{class:'panel chartbox'});
  box.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('strong',{text:'Daily outcomes'}),h('select',{id:'chartMetric','aria-label':'Metric to chart',onchange:e=>{S.chartMetric=e.target.value;render()}},METRICS.map(m=>h('option',{value:m.k,selected:m.k===S.chartMetric},m.l)))));
  box.append(chart(daysFor(ids),METRICS.find(m=>m.k===S.chartMetric)));v.append(box);
  if(leader()){
    const rows=repList(false).filter(r=>r.active!==false||entriesFor(r.id,'week',S.week).length).map(r=>({r,t:totalsFor([r.id],'week',S.week),g:goalOf(r.id,S.week)})).sort((a,b)=>b.t.retail-a.t.retail);
    const tb=h('tbody');
    rows.forEach((x,i)=>{const tt=x.t;const rr=tt.hours>0?tt.retail/tt.hours:null;
      tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:(i+1)+'. '+x.r.name}),
        HMETRICS.map(m=>{const gg=x.g?num(x.g[m.k]):0;return h('td',null,fmt(m,tt[m.k]),gg>0?h('div',{class:'hint',text:pct(tt[m.k],gg)+' of '+fmt(m,gg)}):null)}),
        h('td',{text:pct(tt.buyers,tt.stops)}),h('td',{text:pct(tt.upsells,tt.buyers)}),h('td',{text:pct(tt.medals,tt.buyers)}),
        h('td',{text:rr!=null?money(rr):'—'}),h('td',null,zoneEl(rr)),h('td',null,hitEl(goalsHit(tt,x.g))),h('td',{text:money(feeFor(weekAll(x.r.id)).fee)})))});
    if(!rows.length)tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{colspan:16,class:'hint',text:'Nothing logged this week yet.'})));
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Leaderboard'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Ranked by retail. Only you can see this.'})));
    v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Name'}),HMETRICS.map(m=>h('th',{text:m.l})),['Conv %','Upsell %','Medal %','Week RPH','RPH zone','Goals hit','Perf. fee'].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})))),tb)));
  }else{
    const myT=S.me&&S.me.team;const tm=blank();let n=0;Object.values(S.board).forEach(b=>{if(!myT||b.team!==myT)return;const w=(b.weeks||{})[S.week];if(w){BOARD_FIELDS.forEach(f=>tm[f]+=num(w[f]));n++}});
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:myT?teamName(myT)+' this week':'Team this week'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:myT?`${n} ${n===1?'person has':'people have'} logged · see the Leaderboard tab for rankings`:'Pick your team on the My team tab to see team totals.'})));
    if(myT)v.append(tilesFor(tm,null));
  }
  v.append(legend());if(leader()){const rp=rosterPanel();if(rp)v.append(rp)}}
function chart(days,m){
  const W=720,H=230,pl=56,pr=12,pt=22,pb=30;const vals=days.map(d=>d[m.k]);const top=niceMax(Math.max(...vals,0));
  const iw=W-pl-pr,ih=H-pt-pb,bw=iw/7;const NS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';const s=document.createElementNS(NS,'svg');
  s.setAttribute('viewBox',`0 0 ${W} ${H}`);s.setAttribute('width','100%');s.setAttribute('role','img');s.setAttribute('aria-label',`${m.l} by day this week`);
  const el=(t,a,txt)=>{const n=document.createElementNS(NS,t);for(const k in a)n.setAttribute(k,a[k]);if(txt!=null)n.textContent=txt;s.append(n);return n};
  for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){const val=top*i/4;const y=pt+ih-ih*i/4;el('line',{x1:pl,x2:W-pr,y1:y,y2:y,stroke:'var(--line)','stroke-width':1});
    el('text',{x:pl-8,y:y+4,'text-anchor':'end'},m.money?money(val):Math.round(val))}
  days.forEach((d,i)=>{const val=d[m.k];const bh=top>0?ih*val/top:0;const x=pl+i*bw+bw*0.18;const w=bw*0.64;
    el('rect',{x,y:pt+ih-bh,width:w,height:Math.max(bh,0),rx:4,fill:'var(--accent)'});
    if(val>0)el('text',{x:x+w/2,y:pt+ih-bh-6,'text-anchor':'middle',class:'val'},fmt(m,val));
    el('text',{x:x+w/2,y:H-10,'text-anchor':'middle'},DAYS[i]+' '+parse(addDays(S.week,i)).getDate())});
  return s}
function niceMax(v){if(v<=0)return 4;const e=Math.pow(10,Math.floor(Math.log10(v)));const f=v/e;return(f<=1?1:f<=2?2:f<=2.5?2.5:f<=5?5:10)*e}

/* ---------- TOTALS (month / quarter / year to date) ---------- */
async function loadAllTeam(force){if(!leader()||S.allTeamLoading||(S.allTeamReady&&!force))return;
  S.allTeamLoading=true;const out={};
  await Promise.all(repListAll(false).map(async r=>{if(r.id===S.uid&&S.mineReady)return;
    try{const snap=await S.db.collection('reps/'+r.id+'/entries').orderBy('date','desc').limit(1000).get();const o={};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data());out[r.id]=o}catch(_){out[r.id]={}}}));
  await Promise.all(repListAll(false).map(async r=>{if(r.id===S.uid)return;try{const snap=await S.db.collection('conf/'+r.id+'/days').orderBy('date','desc').limit(1000).get();
    const o=S.conf[r.id]=S.conf[r.id]||{};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data())}catch(_){}}));
  S.allTeam=out;S.allTeamReady=true;S.allTeamLoading=false;render();scheduleTeamPB()}
function needsTeam(){return leader()&&!S.allTeamReady}
function periodHead(first){return h('tr',null,h('th',{text:first}),HMETRICS.map(mm=>h('th',{text:mm.l})),['Conv %','Upsell %','Medal %','RPH','RPH zone'].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})))}
function periodRow(label,x,cls){return h('tr',cls?{class:cls}:null,h('td',{class:cls?null:'name',text:label}),HMETRICS.map(mm=>h('td',{text:fmt(mm,x[mm.k])})),
  rateCells(x).map(c=>h('td',{text:c})),h('td',null,zoneEl(x.hours>0?x.retail/x.hours:null)))}
const PERIODS=[['day','Day'],['week','Week'],['month','Month'],['quarter','Quarter'],['year','Year'],['all','All time'],['range','Date range']];
function groupKey(g,e){return g==='day'?e.date:g==='week'?e.week:g==='year'?e.date.slice(0,4):monthKey(e.date)}
function groupLabel(g,k){return g==='day'?niceDate(k,{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short'}):g==='week'?'WC '+niceDate(k,{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'2-digit'}):g==='year'?k:parse(k+'-01').toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{month:'long',year:'numeric'})}

/* ---------- insights graphs (totals tab) ---------- */
const SVGNS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
function svgRoot(w,h,label){const s=document.createElementNS(SVGNS,'svg');s.setAttribute('viewBox',`0 0 ${w} ${h}`);s.setAttribute('role','img');if(label)s.setAttribute('aria-label',label);
  s.add=(t,a,txt)=>{const n=document.createElementNS(SVGNS,t);for(const k in a)n.setAttribute(k,a[k]);if(txt!=null)n.textContent=txt;s.append(n);return n};return s}
function arcPath(cx,cy,r,a0,a1){const p=a=>[cx+r*Math.cos(a),cy+r*Math.sin(a)];const [x0,y0]=p(a0),[x1,y1]=p(a1);return `M${x0} ${y0} A${r} ${r} 0 ${a1-a0>Math.PI?1:0} 1 ${x1} ${y1}`}
function gauge(label,val,show,max,bands,sub){const s=svgRoot(200,120,label+' '+show);const cx=100,cy=104,r=78;
  s.add('path',{d:arcPath(cx,cy,r,Math.PI,2*Math.PI),stroke:'var(--line)','stroke-width':22,fill:'none'});
  const f=val==null||!isFinite(val)?0:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,val/max));const col=val==null?'var(--line)':(bands.find(b=>val<b[0])||bands[bands.length-1])[1];
  if(f>0.002)s.add('path',{d:arcPath(cx,cy,r,Math.PI,Math.PI+Math.PI*f),stroke:col,'stroke-width':22,fill:'none'});
  s.add('text',{x:cx,y:cy-6,'text-anchor':'middle',style:'font-size:30px;font-weight:700;fill:var(--ink)'},show);
  return h('div',{class:'panel'},h('h4',{text:label}),s,sub?h('div',{class:'gsub',text:sub}):null)}
function donut(title,segs,centre){const tot=segs.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.v,0);const s=svgRoot(170,170,title);const cx=85,cy=85,r=62;
  if(!tot)s.add('circle',{cx,cy,r,stroke:'var(--line)','stroke-width':26,fill:'none'});
  let a=-Math.PI/2;segs.filter(x=>x.v>0).forEach(x=>{const b=a+2*Math.PI*x.v/tot;
    if(b-a>=2*Math.PI-1e-6)s.add('circle',{cx,cy,r,stroke:x.c,'stroke-width':26,fill:'none'});else s.add('path',{d:arcPath(cx,cy,r,a,b),stroke:x.c,'stroke-width':26,fill:'none'});a=b});
  if(centre)s.add('text',{x:cx,y:cy+7,'text-anchor':'middle',style:'font-size:18px;font-weight:700;fill:var(--ink)'},centre);
  return h('div',{class:'panel'},h('h4',{text:title}),h('div',{class:'donutwrap'},s,h('div',{class:'legend'},segs.map(x=>h('span',null,h('i',{style:'background:'+x.c}),x.l+': '+x.t)))))}
function funnel(stages,shifts,hours){const top=stages[0].v||0;
  return h('div',{class:'panel funnel'},h('h4',{text:'Sales funnel'}),stages.map((x,i)=>{const prev=i?stages[i-1].v:0;const w=top?Math.max(4,x.v/top*100):0;
    return h('div',{class:'fr'},h('div',{class:'fl'},h('b',{text:x.l}),h('span',{text:'Total: '+(x.money?money(x.v):x.v)}),
      h('span',{text:'Per shift: '+(shifts?(x.money?money(x.v/shifts):(Math.round(x.v/shifts*10)/10)):'—')}),h('span',{text:'Per hour: '+(hours?(x.money?money(x.v/hours):(Math.round(x.v/hours*10)/10)):'—')})),
      h('div',{class:'fb'},h('i',{style:'width:'+w.toFixed(1)+'%;opacity:'+(1-i*0.17)}),i&&prev?h('em',{text:Math.round(x.v/prev*100)+'%'}):null))}))}
function trend(rows){const W=640,H=260,pl=78,pr=64,pt=16,pb=36;const s=svgRoot(W,H,'Retail and RPH over time');const iw=W-pl-pr,ih=H-pt-pb;
  const mr=niceMax(Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r.retail),0)),mh=niceMax(Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r.rph||0),0));const n=rows.length;
  const X=i=>pl+(n<2?iw/2:iw*i/(n-1));const Y=(v,m)=>pt+ih-(m?ih*v/m:0);
  for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){const y=pt+ih-ih*i/4;s.add('line',{x1:pl,x2:W-pr,y1:y,y2:y,stroke:'var(--line)'});
    s.add('text',{x:pl-6,y:y+4,'text-anchor':'end',style:'font-size:18px;fill:var(--muted)'},money(mr*i/4));s.add('text',{x:W-pr+6,y:y+4,style:'font-size:18px;fill:var(--muted)'},money(mh*i/4))}
  const step=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(n/7));rows.forEach((r,i)=>{if(i%step===0||i===n-1)s.add('text',{x:X(i),y:H-8,'text-anchor':'middle',style:'font-size:18px;fill:var(--muted)'},r.label)});
  const line=(key,m,col)=>{const pts=rows.map((r,i)=>r[key]==null?null:[X(i),Y(r[key],m)]).filter(Boolean);if(!pts.length)return;
    s.add('polyline',{points:pts.map(p=>p.join(',')).join(' '),fill:'none',stroke:col,'stroke-width':3,'stroke-linejoin':'round','stroke-linecap':'round'});
    pts.forEach(p=>s.add('circle',{cx:p[0],cy:p[1],r:n>20?2.5:5,fill:col}))};
  line('retail',mr,'var(--accent)');line('rph',mh,'#2f8a9e');
  const tr=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.retail,0),th=rows.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.hours,0);
  return h('div',{class:'panel trend wide'},h('h4',{text:'Retail & RPH'}),h('div',{class:'keys'},h('span',null,h('b',{style:'background:var(--accent)'}),'Retail: '+money(tr)),h('span',null,h('b',{style:'background:#2f8a9e'}),'RPH: '+(th>0?money(tr/th):'—'))),s)}

/* ---------- best hours (from the Orexis Hourly app) ---------- */
function loadHourlyAll(ids){S.hAll=S.hAll||{};const need=ids.filter(u=>!(u in S.hAll));if(!need.length)return;need.forEach(u=>S.hAll[u]=undefined);
  need.forEach(u=>{try{S.db.collection(`reps/${u}/hourly`).onSnapshot(q=>{S.hAll[u]=q.docs.map(d=>d.data());if(S.tab==='totals')render()},()=>{S.hAll[u]=[]})}catch(_){S.hAll[u]=[]}})}
function hourBuckets(docs,a,b){const by={};(docs||[]).forEach(d=>{if(!d||!d.date||d.date<a||d.date>b||!d.slots)return;
    const st=/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(d.start||'')?d.start.split(':').map(Number):null;const base=st?st[0]*60:null;
    for(const k in d.slots){const x=d.slots[k]||{};const i=Number(k);let clock;
      if(base!=null)clock=Math.floor((base+(i-1)*60+30)/60)%24;else if(i>=6&&i<=23)clock=i;else continue;
      const o=by[clock]=by[clock]||{n:0,retail:0,stops:0,buyers:0};o.n++;o.retail+=num(x.retail);o.stops+=num(x.stops);o.buyers+=num(x.buyers)}});return by}
const hlab=hh=>(hh%12||12)+(hh<12?'am':'pm');
function bestHoursPanel(ids,R){loadHourlyAll(ids);if(ids.some(u=>S.hAll[u]===undefined))return h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Loading hourly data…'}));
  const all=[];ids.forEach(u=>all.push(...(S.hAll[u]||[])));const by=hourBuckets(all,R.a,R.b);const ks=Object.keys(by).map(Number).sort((x,y)=>x-y);
  if(!ks.length)return h('div',{class:'panel'},h('h4',{text:'Best hours'}),h('div',{class:'hint',style:'text-align:center',text:'No hourly data in this period yet. It fills in once reps use the Orexis Hourly app.'}));
  const avg=k=>by[k].retail/by[k].n;const top=Math.max(...ks.map(avg));const best=ks.reduce((p,k)=>avg(k)>avg(p)?k:p,ks[0]);
  const rows=ks.map(k=>h('div',{class:'fr',style:'grid-template-columns:62px 1fr 90px'},h('b',{text:hlab(k)}),h('div',{class:'fb',style:'height:26px'},h('i',{style:'width:'+(top?Math.max(3,avg(k)/top*100):0).toFixed(1)+'%;opacity:'+(k===best?1:.55)})),
    h('span',{style:'text-align:right;font-size:.85rem'},h('b',{text:money(avg(k))}),h('span',{class:'hmini',text:by[k].n+' hr'+(by[k].n>1?'s':'')+' · '+(by[k].stops?Math.round(by[k].buyers/by[k].stops*100)+'% conv':'')}))));
  const out=h('div',{class:'panel funnel wide'},h('h4',{text:'Best hours · average retail per hour'}),h('div',{class:'hint',style:'text-align:center;margin-bottom:6px',text:'Best: '+hlab(best)+'–'+hlab((best+1)%24)+' at '+money(avg(best))+' an hour'}),rows);
  if(leader()&&ids.length>1){const tb=h('tbody');ids.forEach(u=>{const b2=hourBuckets(S.hAll[u],R.a,R.b);const k2=Object.keys(b2).map(Number);if(!k2.length)return;
      const a2=k=>b2[k].retail/b2[k].n;const bb=k2.reduce((p,k)=>a2(k)>a2(p)?k:p,k2[0]),ww=k2.reduce((p,k)=>a2(k)<a2(p)?k:p,k2[0]);
      tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:repName(u)}),h('td',{text:hlab(bb)+' · '+money(a2(bb))}),h('td',{text:hlab(ww)+' · '+money(a2(ww))}),h('td',{text:String(k2.reduce((p,k)=>p+b2[k].n,0))})))});
    if(tb.children.length)out.append(h('div',{class:'scroll',style:'margin-top:10px'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Rep'}),h('th',{text:'Best hour'}),h('th',{text:'Weakest hour'}),h('th',{text:'Hours tracked'}))),tb)))}
  return out}
function insights(ids,R,t,shifts){const list=[];ids.forEach(u=>entriesIn(u,R.a,R.b).forEach(e=>list.push(e)));if(!list.length)return null;
  const rph=t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:null;const pc=(a,b)=>b>0?a/b*100:null;const fp=v=>v==null?'—':Math.round(v)+'%';
  const wrap=h('div',{class:'ins'});
  if(R.group&&R.group!=='none'){const g={};list.forEach(e=>{const k=groupKey(R.group,e);sumInto(g[k]=g[k]||blank(),e)});const ks=Object.keys(g).sort();
    if(ks.length>1)wrap.append(trend(ks.map(k=>({label:R.group==='day'?niceDate(k,{weekday:'short',day:'numeric'}):R.group==='week'?niceDate(k,{day:'numeric',month:'short'}):R.group==='year'?k:parse(k+'-01').toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{month:'short',year:'2-digit'}),retail:g[k].retail,hours:g[k].hours,rph:g[k].hours>0?g[k].retail/g[k].hours:null}))))}
  const conv=pc(t.buyers,t.stops),up=pc(t.upsells,t.buyers),med=pc(t.medals,t.buyers),avg=t.buyers>0?t.retail/t.buyers:null;
  wrap.append(h('div',{class:'gauges'},
    gauge('RPH',rph,rph==null?'—':money(rph),250,[[100,'var(--m-red)'],[120,'var(--m-silver)'],[160,'var(--m-gold)'],[200,'var(--m-plat)'],[1e9,'var(--m-dia)']],rph==null?'':(zone(rph)||[''])[0]+' zone'),
    gauge('Conversion',conv,fp(conv),100,[[60,'var(--bad)'],[70,'var(--warn)'],[1e9,'var(--ok)']],'buyers ÷ stops'),
    gauge('Avg sale',avg,avg==null?'—':money(avg),120,[[50,'var(--bad)'],[60,'var(--warn)'],[1e9,'var(--ok)']],'retail ÷ buyers'),
    gauge('Upsell rate',up,fp(up),60,[[20,'var(--bad)'],[30,'var(--warn)'],[1e9,'var(--ok)']],'upsells ÷ buyers'),
    gauge('Medal ratio',med,fp(med),15,[[5,'var(--bad)'],[8,'var(--warn)'],[1e9,'var(--ok)']],'medals ÷ buyers'),
    gauge('Retail per shift',shifts?t.retail/shifts:null,shifts?money(t.retail/shifts):'—',1500,[[700,'var(--bad)'],[1000,'var(--warn)'],[1e9,'var(--ok)']],shifts+' shifts')));
  wrap.append(bestHoursPanel(ids,R));
  wrap.append(funnel([{l:'Stops',v:t.stops},{l:'Buyers',v:t.buyers},{l:'Upsells',v:t.upsells},{l:'Medals',v:t.medals}],shifts,t.hours));
  const zc={Red:0,Silver:0,Gold:0,Platinum:0,Diamond:0};let zn=0;list.forEach(e=>{const hh=num(e.hours);if(hh>0){const z=zone(num(e.retail)/hh);if(z){zc[z[0]]++;zn++}}});
  const zcol={Red:'var(--m-red)',Silver:'var(--m-silver)',Gold:'var(--m-gold)',Platinum:'var(--m-plat)',Diamond:'var(--m-dia)'};
  wrap.append(donut('RPH zones by shift',Object.keys(zc).map(k=>({l:k,v:zc[k],c:zcol[k],t:zn?Math.round(zc[k]/zn*100)+'%':'0%'})),zn?zn+' shifts':''));
  if(t.cash+t.eftpos>0)wrap.append(donut('Cash vs EFTPOS',[{l:'Cash',v:t.cash,c:'var(--m-gold)',t:money(t.cash)},{l:'EFTPOS',v:t.eftpos,c:'#2f8a9e',t:money(t.eftpos)}],Math.round(t.eftpos/(t.cash+t.eftpos)*100)+'% card'));
  return wrap}

  if(S.period==='ytd')S.period='year';
  const R=periodRange();
  const seg=h('div',{class:'seg'},PERIODS.map(([k,l])=>h('button',{class:S.period===k?'primary':null,'aria-pressed':String(S.period===k),onclick:()=>{S.period=k;render()}},l)));

      R.noNav?null:h('button',{onclick:()=>shiftPeriod(-1)},'‹ Previous'),R.noNav?null:h('button',{onclick:()=>shiftPeriod(1)},'Next ›'),
      leader()?h('button',{onclick:()=>loadAllTeam(true)},'Refresh'):null)));
  if(needsTeam()){v.append(h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Loading the team\u2019s history\u2026'})));loadAllTeam();return}
  const ids=scopeIds();const t=totalsIn(ids,R.a,R.b);const solo=ids.length===1&&ids[0]===S.uid;
  const days=new Set();ids.forEach(u=>entriesIn(u,R.a,R.b).forEach(e=>days.add(u+e.date)));

  if(S.period==='month'&&solo)v.append(monthPanel(S.uid));
  {const ins=insights(ids,R,t,days.size);if(ins)v.append(ins)}
  v.append(h('div',{class:'hint',text:`${days.size} shift${days.size===1?'':'s'} logged · ${Math.round(t.hours*10)/10} hours`+(days.size?` · ${money(t.retail/days.size)} retail per shift`:'')}));
  if(R.group!=='none'){const groups={};ids.forEach(u=>entriesIn(u,R.a,R.b).forEach(e=>{const g=groupKey(R.group,e);sumInto(groups[g]=groups[g]||blank(),e)}));
    const keys=Object.keys(groups).sort();const tb=h('tbody');
    keys.forEach(g=>tb.append(periodRow(groupLabel(R.group,g),groups[g])));
    if(!keys.length)tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{colspan:14,class:'hint',text:'Nothing logged in this period yet.'})));
    else tb.append(periodRow('Total',t,'total'));
    const nm={day:'Day',week:'Week',month:'Month',year:'Year'}[R.group];

  // by campaign (when viewing all campaigns and more than one exists)
  if((!S.campFilter||S.campFilter==='all')&&campaigns().length>1){const byC={};ids.forEach(u=>entriesIn(u,R.a,R.b).forEach(e=>sumInto(byC[campOf(e)]=byC[campOf(e)]||blank(),e)));
    const ks=Object.keys(byC);if(ks.length){const tb3=h('tbody');ks.sort((a,b)=>byC[b].retail-byC[a].retail).forEach(k=>tb3.append(periodRow(campName(k),byC[k])));

/* ---------- team records ---------- */
function computeTeamPB(ids){const byDay={},byWeek={},byMonth={};
  ids.forEach(u=>sourceAll(u,'period').forEach(e=>{sumInto(byDay[e.date]=byDay[e.date]||blank(),e);sumInto(byWeek[e.week]=byWeek[e.week]||blank(),e);sumInto(byMonth[monthKey(e.date)]=byMonth[monthKey(e.date)]||blank(),e)}));
  const best=(map,withRph)=>{const out={};for(const key in map){const x=map[key];
    for(const [k] of PB_WEEK){const val=x[k];if(val>0&&(!out[k]||val>out[k].v))out[k]={v:val,at:key}}
    if(withRph&&x.hours>=1){const r=Math.round(x.retail/x.hours*100)/100;if(!out.rph||r>out.rph.v)out.rph={v:r,at:key}}}return out};
  return{day:best(byDay,true),week:best(byWeek,true),month:best(byMonth,true)}}
function teamPB(){if(leader())return S.allTeamReady?computeTeamPB(repList(false).filter(r=>!r.test).map(r=>r.id)):null;return S.teamPbDoc}
function scheduleTeamPB(){if(!S.isOwner||!S.allTeamReady)return;clearTimeout(S.teamPbTimer);S.teamPbTimer=setTimeout(async()=>{
  const pb=teamPB();if(!pb)return;const sig=JSON.stringify(pb);if(sig===S.teamPbSent)return;
  const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc('summary/teampb').set({...pb,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()}));if(ok)S.teamPbSent=sig},2500)}
function recordHolders(){const out={day:{},week:{}};repList(false).filter(r=>!r.test).forEach(r=>{const pb=r.pb||{};
  ['day','week'].forEach(kind=>{for(const k in (pb[kind]||{})){const x=pb[kind][k];if(!out[kind][k]||x.v>out[kind][k].v)out[kind][k]={...x,name:r.name}}})});return out}
function teamRecordsTable(pb){const rows=[['retail','Retail',1],['stops','Stops'],['buyers','Buyers'],['upsells','Upsells'],['upsellRetail','Upsell retail',1],['medals','Medals'],['rph','RPH',1]];
  const when=(kind,x)=>!x?'':kind==='day'?niceDate(x.at,{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'}):kind==='week'?'WC '+niceDate(x.at,{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'}):monthName(x.at);
  const cell=(kind,k)=>{const x=(pb[kind]||{})[k];const recent=x&&kind!=='month'&&x.at>=addDays(todayStr(),kind==='day'?-7:-13);
    return h('td',null,x?fmtK(k,x.v):'—',recent?h('span',{class:'pbtag',text:'New'}):null,x?h('div',{class:'hint',text:when(kind,x)}):null)};
  const tb=h('tbody');rows.forEach(([k,l])=>tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:l}),cell('day',k),cell('week',k),cell('month',k))));
  return h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Team record'}),h('th',{text:'Best day'}),h('th',{text:'Best week'}),h('th',{text:'Best month'}))),tb))}

function renderGoals(v){
  const ros=leader()?repList(true):[{id:S.uid,...S.me}];const dirty=Object.keys(S.goalDrafts).filter(k=>k.endsWith('|'+S.week));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:(leader()?'Goals · ':'My goals · ')+'week of '+niceDate(S.week,{day:'numeric',month:'long'})}),
      h('div',{class:'hint',text:leader()?'Set each person’s targets. Each rep sees only their own; the team goal is the total.':'Set your targets for the week. Your leader can see and adjust them.'})),
    h('div',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap'},
      ros.length?h('button',{onclick:copyLastWeek},'Copy last week’s goals'):null,
      ros.length?h('button',{class:'primary',disabled:!dirty.length,onclick:saveGoals},dirty.length?'Save goals':'All saved'):null)));
  if(!ros.length){v.append(noReps());return}
  const tb=h('tbody');const tot={};GMETRICS.forEach(m=>tot[m.k]=0);
  for(const r of ros){const key=r.id+'|'+S.week;const g=goalOf(r.id,S.week);const d=S.goalDrafts[key]||{};
    const act=totalsFor([r.id],'week',S.week);act.rph=act.hours?act.retail/act.hours:0;
    const ged=!leader()||(S.goalEdit===r.id)||Object.keys(d).length>0;
    tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:r.name}),GMETRICS.map(m=>{const raw=(m.k in d)?d[m.k]:(g&&g[m.k]?String(g[m.k]):'');if(!r.test)tot[m.k]+=num(raw);const gv=num(raw);
      return h('td',null,!ged?h('span',{text:gv>0?fmt(m,gv):'—'}):h('input',{type:'text',inputmode:'decimal',class:'num'+(m.money?' money':'')+((m.k in d)?' dirty':''),id:`goal-${r.id}-${m.k}`,'aria-label':`${r.name} ${m.l} goal`,value:raw,placeholder:'—',
        oninput:e=>{(S.goalDrafts[key]=S.goalDrafts[key]||{})[m.k]=e.target.value;render()},onkeydown:e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();saveGoals()}}}),
        gv>0?h('div',{class:act[m.k]>=gv?'achieved small':'hint small',text:act[m.k]>=gv?'✓ '+fmt(m,act[m.k]):fmt(m,act[m.k])+' so far'}):null)}),h('td',null,hitEl(goalsHit(act,g))),
      leader()?h('td',null,Object.keys(d).length?h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px'},h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:async()=>{await saveGoals();S.goalEdit=null;render()}},'Save'),h('button',{onclick:()=>{delete S.goalDrafts[key];S.goalEdit=null;render()}},'Cancel')):S.goalEdit===r.id?h('button',{onclick:()=>{S.goalEdit=null;render()}},'Done'):h('button',{class:'small',onclick:()=>{S.goalEdit=r.id;render()}},'✏️ Edit')):null))}
  if(leader())tb.append(h('tr',{class:'total'},h('td',{text:'Team goal'}),GMETRICS.map(m=>h('td',{text:m.k==='rph'?(tot.hours?fmt(m,tot.retail/tot.hours):'—'):fmt(m,tot[m.k])}))));
  tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'hint',text:'Implied rates'}),h('td'),h('td',{class:'hint',text:pct(tot.buyers,tot.stops)+' conv'}),
    h('td',{class:'hint',text:tot.buyers>0?money(tot.retail/tot.buyers)+' avg':''}),h('td',{class:'hint',text:pct(tot.upsells,tot.buyers)+' upsell'}),h('td'),h('td',{class:'hint',text:pct(tot.medals,tot.buyers)+' medal'})));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Name'}),GMETRICS.map(m=>h('th',{text:m.l})),h('th',{text:'Goals hit'}),leader()?h('th'):null)),tb)));
  v.append(weekGoalsSection(ros))}
