const WG=[['personal','Personal goal'],['learning','Learning goal'],['business','Business goal']];
function wgOf(uid,cat){const r=S.reps[uid]||{};return(((r.weekGoals||{})[S.week])||{})[cat]||{}}
async function setWG(uid,cat,patch){const r=S.reps[uid]||{};const wk=S.week;const all=r.weekGoals||{};const w=all[wk]||{};const cur=w[cat]||{};
  const next={...cur,...patch};S.reps[uid]={...r,weekGoals:{...all,[wk]:{...w,[cat]:next}}};if(uid===S.uid)S.me=S.reps[uid];render();
  await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({weekGoals:{[wk]:{[cat]:patch}}}))}
function wgEditor(uid,cat,label){const g=wgOf(uid,cat);
  const inp=h('input',{type:'text',id:`wg-${uid}-${cat}`,placeholder:label,'aria-label':label,onchange:e=>setWG(uid,cat,{t:e.target.value.trim()})});inp.value=g.t||'';
  return h('div',{class:'wgrow'+(g.done?' on':'')},h('input',{type:'checkbox',id:`wgd-${uid}-${cat}`,'aria-label':label+' achieved',checked:!!g.done,disabled:!g.t,onchange:e=>setWG(uid,cat,{done:e.target.checked})}),inp)}
const PLAN_KPIS=[['stops','Stops'],['buyers','Buyers'],['medals','Medals'],['avgSale','Sale Avg',1],['rph','RPH',1],['hours','Hours']];
function planLastWeek(uid){const lw=addDays(S.week,-7);let t=totalsFor([uid],'week',lw);if(!t.stops&&!t.retail&&!t.hours){t=blank();entriesIn(uid,lw,addDays(lw,6)).forEach(e=>sumInto(t,e))}return{lw,t}}
function planLW(k,t){if(['contacts','presentations','closes'].includes(k))return null;const v=k==='hours'?t.hours:kpiVal(k,t);return v==null||!isFinite(v)||(!v&&k!=='hours')?null:v}
function planShow(k,v){if(v==null||v==='')return'—';if(k==='avgSale'||k==='rph'||k==='retail')return money(+v);return String(Math.round(+v*10)/10)}
async function copyRich(md){const esc=x=>x.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;');const html=esc(md).replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g,'<b>$1</b>').replace(/\n/g,'<br>');
  try{if(window.ClipboardItem){await navigator.clipboard.write([new ClipboardItem({'text/html':new Blob([html],{type:'text/html'}),'text/plain':new Blob([md],{type:'text/plain'})})]);return true}}catch(_){}
  try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(md);return true}catch(_){return false}}
function planText(uid){const g=wgOf(uid,'plan');const G=goalOf(uid,S.week)||{};const {t:LW}=planLastWeek(uid);const lw={stops:LW.stops,buyers:LW.buyers,medals:LW.medals,retail:LW.retail,hours:LW.hours,rph:LW.hours?LW.retail/LW.hours:0};const n=k=>num(G[k])||Math.round((lw[k]||0)*(k==='hours'?10:1))/(k==='hours'?10:1);
  const av=n('buyers')&&n('retail')?money(n('retail')/n('buyers')):'',fl=(PLAN_KPIS.find(x=>x[0]===g.focus)||[,''])[1];
  return ['**Weekly Goals**','','**Key Motivator for the week:**',g.motivator||'','','**KPI Targets**',
    '- **Stops:** '+(n('stops')||''),'- **Buyers:** '+(n('buyers')||''),'- **Medals:** '+(n('medals')||''),'- **Sale Avg:** '+av,'- **RPH:** '+(n('rph')?money(n('rph')):''),'- **Hours:** '+(n('hours')||''),'',
    '**Which KPI Target is the biggest focus for yourself?**',fl,'','**Retail Goal:** '+(n('retail')?money(n('retail')):''),'',
    '**Personal Goal:** '+(wgOf(uid,'personal').t||''),'**Learning Goal:** '+(wgOf(uid,'learning').t||''),'**Business Goal:** '+(wgOf(uid,'business').t||'')].join('\n')}
function weekPlanPanel(uid){const g=wgOf(uid,'plan');const {t}=planLastWeek(uid);const p=h('div',{class:'panel rform'});
  const mo=h('input',{type:'text',id:'wp-motivator',class:'wp-in',placeholder:'e.g. New start','aria-label':'Key motivator',onchange:e=>setWG(uid,'plan',{motivator:e.target.value.trim()})});mo.value=g.motivator||'';

    g.focus?h('span',{class:'hl',text:'Last week: '+planShow(g.focus,planLW(g.focus,t))}):null));

  return p}
function weekGoalsSection(ros){const box=h('div',{class:'stack'});
  box.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'My weekly goals'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Your motivation and the one KPI you’re beating.'})),weekPlanPanel(S.uid));

async function saveGoals(){const ks=Object.keys(S.goalDrafts).filter(k=>k.endsWith('|'+S.week));let n=0;
  for(const k of ks){const uid=k.split('|')[0];const prev=goalOf(uid,S.week)||{};const d=S.goalDrafts[k];const g={};
    for(const m of GMETRICS)g[m.k]=(m.k in d)?num(d[m.k]):num(prev[m.k]);
    const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({goals:{[S.week]:g}}));
    if(ok){const r=S.reps[uid]||{};S.reps[uid]={...r,goals:{...(r.goals||{}),[S.week]:g}};if(uid===S.uid)S.me=S.reps[uid];delete S.goalDrafts[k];n++}}
  if(n)toast('Goals saved');render();scheduleSummary()}
function copyLastWeek(){const prev=addDays(S.week,-7);let n=0;const ros=leader()?repList(true):[{id:S.uid}];
  ros.forEach(r=>{if(goalOf(r.id,S.week))return;const g=goalOf(r.id,prev);if(!g)return;const d={};GMETRICS.forEach(m=>{if(num(g[m.k]))d[m.k]=String(g[m.k])});
    if(Object.keys(d).length){S.goalDrafts[r.id+'|'+S.week]=d;n++}});
  toast(n?`Copied ${n} goal row${n>1?'s':''}. Review and save.`:'No goals found for last week');render()}

/* ---------- GROWTH GOALS (personal / development / learning) ---------- */
const GCATS=[['personal','Personal'],['development','Professional development'],['learning','Learning']];
const GTERMS=[['1w','1 week',7],['2w','2 weeks',14],['1m','1 month',30],['6w','6 weeks',42],['8w','8 weeks',56],['2m','2 months',61],['3m','3 months',91],['6m','6 months',182],['12m','12 months',365],['24m','24 months',730]];
const GTERMS_OLD={short:['Short term · 4–8 weeks',56],mid:['Mid term · 1–3 months',91],long:['Long term · 6–24 months',730]};
function termDays(k){const t=GTERMS.find(x=>x[0]===k);return t?t[2]:(GTERMS_OLD[k]?GTERMS_OLD[k][1]:30)}
function termOpts(k){const o=GTERMS.map(([v,l])=>[v,l]);if(GTERMS_OLD[k])o.unshift([k,GTERMS_OLD[k][0]]);return o}
function dueDate(g){const start=(g.startedAt||g.createdAt||'').slice(0,10)||todayStr();return addDays(start,termDays(g.term))}
function growthOf(uid){return Object.entries(S.growth[uid]||{}).map(([id,g])=>({id,...g})).sort((a,b)=>(a.createdAt||'').localeCompare(b.createdAt||''))}
function subscribeGrowth(uid){if(!uid||uid===S.uid||S.growthFor===uid)return;
  if(S.growthUnsub)try{S.growthUnsub()}catch(_){}S.growthFor=uid;
  S.growthUnsub=S.db.collection('reps/'+uid+'/growth').onSnapshot(snap=>{const o={};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data());S.growth[uid]=o;render()},onErr)}
async function loadGrowthAll(force){if(!leader()||S.growthAllLoading||(S.growthAllLoaded&&!force))return;S.growthAllLoading=true;
  await Promise.all(repList(false).map(async r=>{if(r.id===S.uid)return;try{const snap=await S.db.collection('reps/'+r.id+'/growth').get();const o={};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data());if(r.id!==S.growthFor)S.growth[r.id]=o}catch(_){}}));
  S.growthAllLoaded=true;S.growthAllLoading=false;render()}
function growthTarget(){if(!leader())return S.uid;const ros=repList(true);
  if(S.growthSel&&(S.reps[S.growthSel]))return S.growthSel;return (ros[0]&&ros[0].id)||null}
async function addGrowth(uid,preset){const cat=preset?preset.category:document.getElementById('gNewCat').value;const term=preset?preset.term:document.getElementById('gNewTerm').value;
  const title=preset?preset.title:(document.getElementById('gNewTitle').value||'').trim();if(!title){toast('Write the goal first');return}
  const body={category:cat,term,title,why:'',actions:(preset&&preset.actions)||[],achieved:false,createdAt:new Date().toISOString()};
  const ref=S.db.collection('reps/'+uid+'/growth').doc();
  const ok=await write(()=>ref.set(body),'Goal added');
  if(ok){(S.growth[uid]=S.growth[uid]||{})[ref.id]=body;const t=document.getElementById('gNewTitle');if(t)t.value='';render()}}
async function updGrowth(uid,id,patch){const cur=(S.growth[uid]||{})[id];if(!cur)return;S.growth[uid][id]={...cur,...patch};render();
  await write(()=>S.db.doc(`reps/${uid}/growth/${id}`).update(patch))}
async function delGrowth(uid,id){if(S.confirmDel!==id){S.confirmDel=id;render();setTimeout(()=>{if(S.confirmDel===id){S.confirmDel=null;render()}},4000);return}
  S.confirmDel=null;const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc(`reps/${uid}/growth/${id}`).delete(),'Goal deleted');if(ok){delete S.growth[uid][id];render()}}
function growthCard(uid,g){const acts=g.actions||[];const done=acts.filter(a=>a.done).length;
  const card=h('div',{class:'gcard panel'+(g.achieved?' done':'')});
  card.append(h('div',{class:'gtop'},
    h('span',{class:'tcell'},h('select',{id:`g-${g.id}-term`,'aria-label':'Time period',onchange:e=>updGrowth(uid,g.id,{term:e.target.value})},termOpts(g.term).map(([k,l])=>h('option',{value:k,selected:g.term===k},l))),
      g.achieved?null:(()=>{const d=dueDate(g);const late=d<todayStr();return h('span',{class:late?'gap':'hint',text:(late?'Overdue · ':'Due ')+niceDate(d,{day:'numeric',month:'short'})})})()),
    g.achieved?h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓ Achieved'+(g.achievedAt?' '+niceDate(g.achievedAt.slice(0,10),{day:'numeric',month:'short'}):'')}):null));
  card.append(h('input',{type:'text',class:'gtitle',id:`g-${g.id}-title`,value:g.title||'','aria-label':'Goal',onchange:e=>{const t=e.target.value.trim();if(t)updGrowth(uid,g.id,{title:t})}}));
  const why=h('textarea',{id:`g-${g.id}-why`,rows:2,placeholder:'Why does this matter to you?','aria-label':'Why',onchange:e=>updGrowth(uid,g.id,{why:e.target.value})});why.value=g.why||'';card.append(why);
  const list=h('div',{class:'checklist'});
  acts.forEach((a,i)=>list.append(h('div',{class:'citem'+(a.done?' on':'')},
    h('input',{type:'checkbox',id:`g-${g.id}-a${i}`,checked:!!a.done,onchange:e=>{const n=acts.map((x,j)=>j===i?{...x,done:e.target.checked}:x);updGrowth(uid,g.id,{actions:n})}}),
    h('label',{for:`g-${g.id}-a${i}`,text:a.t}),
    h('button',{class:'icon x','aria-label':'Remove action',onclick:()=>updGrowth(uid,g.id,{actions:acts.filter((_,j)=>j!==i)})},'×'))));
  list.append(h('input',{type:'text',class:'cadd',id:`g-${g.id}-new`,placeholder:'+ Add an action step and press Enter','aria-label':'New action step',
    onkeydown:e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){const t=e.target.value.trim();if(t){e.target.value='';updGrowth(uid,g.id,{actions:acts.concat([{t,done:false}])})}}}}));
  card.append(list);
  card.append(h('div',{class:'gfoot'},h('span',{class:'hint',text:acts.length?`${done} of ${acts.length} actions done`+(done===acts.length&&!g.achieved?' · ready to mark achieved':''):'No action steps yet'}),
    h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px'},
      h('button',{class:g.achieved?null:'primary',onclick:()=>updGrowth(uid,g.id,{achieved:!g.achieved,achievedAt:g.achieved?null:todayStr()})},g.achieved?'Undo achieved':'Mark achieved'),
      h('button',{onclick:()=>delGrowth(uid,g.id)},S.confirmDel===g.id?'Tap again to delete':'Delete'))));
  return card}
function renderGrowth(v){
  const uid=growthTarget();
  if(leader()){
    loadGrowthAll();const ros=repList(true);
    if(!ros.length){v.append(noReps());return}
    subscribeGrowth(uid);
    const tb=h('tbody');ros.forEach(r=>{const gs=growthOf(r.id);const ach=gs.filter(g=>g.achieved).length;const acts=gs.flatMap(g=>g.actions||[]);
      tb.append(h('tr',{class:r.id===uid?'sel':null},h('td',{class:'name'},h('button',{class:'linkbtn',onclick:()=>{S.growthSel=r.id;render()}},r.name)),
        GCATS.map(([k])=>h('td',{text:String(gs.filter(g=>g.category===k&&!g.achieved).length)})),h('td',null,ach?h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓ '+ach}):'0'),
        h('td',{text:acts.length?`${acts.filter(a=>a.done).length} / ${acts.length}`:'—'})))});
    v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Name'}),GCATS.map(([,l])=>h('th',{text:l})),h('th',{text:'Achieved'}),h('th',{text:'Actions done'}))),tb)));
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:repName(uid)+'’s goals'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Tap a name above to switch person. You can add, edit and tick off goals for anyone.'})));
  }
  if(!uid)return;
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel addgoal'},
    h('select',{id:'gNewCat','aria-label':'Goal type'},GCATS.map(([k,l])=>h('option',{value:k},l))),
    h('select',{id:'gNewTerm','aria-label':'Time period'},GTERMS.map(([k,l])=>h('option',{value:k,selected:k==='1m'},l))),
    h('input',{id:'gNewTitle',type:'text',placeholder:'New goal, e.g. Hit Platinum zone for a full month','aria-label':'New goal',onkeydown:e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')addGrowth(uid)}}),
    h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>addGrowth(uid)},'Add goal')));
  const gs=growthOf(uid);
  GCATS.forEach(([k,l])=>{const list=gs.filter(g=>g.category===k).sort((a,b)=>(a.achieved?1:0)-(b.achieved?1:0)||termDays(a.term)-termDays(b.term));
    const ach=list.filter(g=>g.achieved).length;
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:l}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:list.length?`${ach} of ${list.length} achieved`:'No goals yet'})));
    if(list.length)v.append(h('div',{class:'ggrid'},list.map(g=>growthCard(uid,g))))})}

const TOPICS_REP=['DISC','TTP','Rapport / engagement','Upselling','Handling concerns','Landing impulses','Law of averages','How to work solo','NVC / body language','8 successful business habits','Bulletproof attitude','Interpreting KPIs','Stopping','6 week goal planner','How to be a top tier team mate','GRASP theory','Staying in the zone in the field','How to have a successful roadtrip','Nail your first 4 weeks in the industry'];

/* rate calculators shared by coaching and bonuses */
const RATES={
  stopsDay:t=>t.days>0?t.stops/t.days:null, stopBuyer:t=>t.stops>0?t.buyers/t.stops*100:null,
  closeRate:t=>t.stops>0?t.closes/t.stops*100:null, closeToBuy:t=>t.closes>0?t.buyers/t.closes*100:null,
  upsellRate:t=>t.buyers>0?t.upsells/t.buyers*100:null, medalRate:t=>t.buyers>0?t.medals/t.buyers*100:null,
  avgSale:t=>t.buyers>0?t.retail/t.buyers:null, rph:t=>t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:null};
/* Knowing your numbers: priority order, Low / Avg / Strong bands */
