function slug(t){return t.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-').replace(/^-|-$/g,'')}
function band(f){const c=(S.coach||{})[f.k]||{};return{low:c.low!=null&&c.low!==''?num(c.low):f.low,strong:c.strong!=null&&c.strong!==''?num(c.strong):f.strong}}
function rateBand(f,v){if(v==null)return null;const b=band(f);return v<b.low?'low':v<b.strong?'avg':'strong'}
function bandPill(st){return st?h('span',{class:'band '+st,text:st==='low'?'Low':st==='avg'?'Average':'Strong'}):h('span',{class:'hint',text:'—'})}
function topicState(uid,t){if(S.isOwner&&uid===S.uid)return{learnt:true,ran:true};const r=S.reps[uid]||{};return ((r.topics||{})[slug(t)])||{}}
function last4(uid){const b=todayStr(),a=addDays(b,-27);const list=entriesIn(uid,a,b);const t=blank();list.forEach(e=>sumInto(t,e));
  t.days=list.filter(e=>num(e.stops)>0||num(e.hours)>0).length;return t}
function focusFor(uid){const t=last4(uid);const enough=t.days>=3||t.stops>=30;
  const rows=FOCUS.map(f=>{const v=RATES[f.k](t);return{f,v,st:rateBand(f,v)}});
  const priority=rows.find(r=>r.st==='low')||rows.find(r=>r.st==='avg')||null;
  return{enough,t,rows,priority,flags:rows.filter(r=>r.st==='low')}}
async function setTopic(uid,t,field,val){const key=slug(t);const r=S.reps[uid]||{};const cur=(r.topics||{})[key]||{};
  S.reps[uid]={...r,topics:{...(r.topics||{}),[key]:{...cur,[field]:val}}};if(uid===S.uid)S.me=S.reps[uid];render();
  await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({topics:{[key]:{[field]:val}}}))}
function topicChecklist(uid,list,title){const tb=h('tbody');let learnt=0,ran=0;
  list.forEach(t=>{const s=topicState(uid,t);if(s.learnt)learnt++;if(s.ran)ran++;const id=slug(t);
    tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:t}),
      h('td',null,h('input',{type:'checkbox',id:`t-${id}-l`,'aria-label':t+' learnt',checked:!!s.learnt,onchange:e=>setTopic(uid,t,'learnt',e.target.checked)})),
      h('td',null,h('input',{type:'checkbox',id:`t-${id}-r`,'aria-label':t+' independently ran',checked:!!s.ran,onchange:e=>setTopic(uid,t,'ran',e.target.checked)}))))});
  return[h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:title}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:`${learnt} learnt · ${ran} run independently · ${list.length} topics`})),
    h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',{class:'topics'},h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Topic'}),h('th',{text:'Learnt'}),h('th',{text:'Ran independently'}))),tb))]}
function guideEl(key,open){const g=GUIDES[key];const d=h('details',{class:'guide',id:'guide-'+key});if(open)d.open=true;
  d.append(h('summary',{text:g.title}));if(g.intro)d.append(h('p',{class:'hint',text:g.intro}));
  g.blocks.forEach(b=>{d.append(h('h4',{text:b.h}));
    if(b.items)d.append(h('ul',null,b.items.map(i=>h('li',{text:i}))));
    if(b.scripts)b.scripts.forEach(([q,a])=>d.append(h('div',{class:'script'},h('div',{class:'q',text:'“'+q+'”'}),h('div',{class:'a',text:a}))))});
  return d}
function numbersTable(uid){const F=focusFor(uid);
  const tb=h('tbody');F.rows.forEach((r,i)=>{const b=band(r.f);
    tb.append(h('tr',{class:F.priority&&F.priority.f===r.f?'sel':null},h('td',{class:'name',text:`${i+1}. ${r.f.l}`}),h('td',{text:r.v!=null?r.f.show(r.v):'—'}),h('td',null,bandPill(r.st)),
      h('td',{class:'hint',text:`Low under ${r.f.show(b.low)} · Strong ${r.f.show(b.strong)}+`})))});
  return h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Priority order'}),h('th',{text:'Last 4 weeks'}),h('th',{text:'Level'}),h('th',{text:'Standard'}))),tb))}
function averagesTable(uid){const b=todayStr(),a=addDays(b,-27);const list=entriesIn(uid,a,b).filter(e=>num(e.stops)>0||num(e.hours)>0||num(e.retail)>0);
  const t=blank();list.forEach(e=>sumInto(t,e));const days=list.length,weeks=new Set(list.map(e=>e.week)).size;
  if(!days)return h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'No days logged in the last 4 weeks.'}));
  const tb=h('tbody');METRICS.forEach(m=>{const tot=t[m.k];
    tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:m.l}),h('td',{text:fmt(m,tot/days)}),h('td',{text:fmt(m,tot/weeks)}),
      h('td',{text:t.hours>0?(m.money?money(tot/t.hours):(Math.round(tot/t.hours*100)/100).toFixed(2)):'—'})))});
  tb.append(h('tr',{class:'total'},h('td',{text:'Hours'}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.hours/days*10)/10)}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.hours/weeks*10)/10)}),h('td',{text:'—'})));
  return h('div',{class:'stack'},h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Averages · last 4 weeks'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:`${days} day${days===1?'':'s'} across ${weeks} week${weeks===1?'':'s'} worked · ${Math.round(t.hours*10)/10} hours`})),
    h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,['KPI','Per day','Per week','Per hour'].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})))),tb)))}
function focusCards(uid){const F=focusFor(uid);
  if(!F.enough)return h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Not enough numbers in the last 4 weeks yet. Coaching appears after about 3 days logged.'}));
  const out=h('div',{class:'stack'});
  if(!F.priority){out.append(h('div',{class:'notice win',text:'✓ Strong on every number over the last 4 weeks. Keep it up, or pick a leader-track topic to stretch.'}));return out}
  const p=F.priority;const todo=p.f.topics.filter(t=>!topicState(uid,t).learnt);
  out.append(h('div',{class:'panel gcard focus'},
    h('div',{class:'gtop'},h('div',null,h('div',{class:'lbl',text:'Priority focus'}),h('strong',{class:'ftitle',text:p.f.l})),h('span',null,h('span',{class:'gap',text:p.f.show(p.v)+' '}),bandPill(p.st))),
    h('div',{class:'hint',text:p.f.why+' Work on this first; the numbers below it follow.'}),
    h('div',{class:'checklist'},h('div',{class:'lbl',text:'Topics that help'}),p.f.topics.map(t=>{const s=leader()?topicState(uid,t):{};return h('div',{class:'citem'+(s.learnt?' on':'')},h('span',{text:s.ran?'✓✓':s.learnt?'✓':'•','aria-hidden':'true'}),h('span',{text:t+(!leader()?'':s.ran?' · ran independently':s.learnt?' · learnt':' · not learnt yet')}))})),
    guideEl(p.f.guide,true),
    h('div',{class:'gfoot'},h('span',{class:'hint',text:`Target: ${p.f.show(band(p.f).strong)}+ for Strong`}),
      h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>addGrowth(uid,{category:'learning',term:'2w',title:`Lift ${p.f.l.toLowerCase()} to ${p.f.show(band(p.f).strong)}`,
        actions:(todo.length?[{t:'Learn '+todo[0]+' with my leader',done:false}]:[]).concat([{t:'Practise the tips from "'+GUIDES[p.f.guide].title+'" every shift for a week',done:false},{t:`Average ${p.f.show(band(p.f).low)}+ for a full week`,done:false},{t:`Average ${p.f.show(band(p.f).strong)}+ for a full week`,done:false}])})},'Add as a learning goal'))));
  const others=F.rows.filter(r=>r!==p&&(r.st==='low'||r.st==='avg'));
  if(others.length){out.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Next up'})));out.append(h('div',{class:'stack'},others.map(r=>{const d=guideEl(r.f.guide,false);
    d.querySelector('summary').textContent=`${r.f.l}: ${r.f.show(r.v)} (${r.st==='low'?'Low':'Average'}) · ${GUIDES[r.f.guide].title}`;return d})))}
  return out}
function renderCoaching(v){
  if(!leader()){
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'My coaching'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Know your numbers. Fix them in priority order: stops first, then stop to buyer, then sale average.'}))));
    v.append(numbersTable(S.uid));v.append(averagesTable(S.uid));v.append(focusCards(S.uid));
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'All guides'})));Object.keys(GUIDES).forEach(k=>v.append(guideEl(k,false)));
    return}
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'Coaching'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Each person’s last 4 weeks against your Knowing Your Numbers standards, in priority order.'})),h('button',{onclick:()=>loadAllTeam(true)},'Refresh')));
  if(needsTeam()){v.append(h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Loading the team’s history…'})));loadAllTeam();return}
  const ros=repList(true);if(!ros.length){v.append(noReps());return}
  // what to train next: each person's top 3 priorities in Knowing Your Numbers order (Low first, then Average)
  const ptb=h('tbody');const p1={};
  ros.forEach(r=>{const F=focusFor(r.id);
    const pri=F.enough?F.rows.filter(x=>x.st==='low').concat(F.rows.filter(x=>x.st==='avg')).slice(0,3):[];
    if(pri[0])(p1[pri[0].f.l]=p1[pri[0].f.l]||[]).push(r.name);
    ptb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name'},h('button',{class:'linkbtn',onclick:()=>{S.coachSel=r.id;render()}},r.name)),
      [0,1,2].map(i=>{const x=pri[i];if(!F.enough)return h('td',{class:'wrapcell hint',text:i===0?'Not enough data yet':''});
        if(!x)return h('td',{class:'wrapcell'},i===0?h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓ All strong'}):'');
        const todo=x.f.topics.filter(t=>!topicState(r.id,t).learnt);
        return h('td',{class:'wrapcell'},h('div',{class:'pcell'},h('strong',{text:x.f.l}),' ',bandPill(x.st)),h('div',{class:'hint small',text:x.f.show(x.v)+' · aim for '+x.f.show(band(x.f).strong)+'+'}),
          h('ul',{class:'tlist'},(todo.length?todo:x.f.topics).map(t=>h('li',{text:t+(todo.length?'':' (learnt, practise it)')}))))})))});
  const common=Object.entries(p1).sort((a,b)=>b[1].length-a[1].length)[0];
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'What to train next'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:common?`Most common priority 1: ${common[0]} (${common[1].length} ${common[1].length===1?'person':'people'})`:'Each person\u2019s top 3, in priority order'})));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',{class:'train'},h('thead',null,h('tr',null,['Name','Priority 1','Priority 2','Priority 3'].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})))),ptb)));
  const tb=h('tbody');
  ros.forEach(r=>{const F=focusFor(r.id);const learnt=TOPICS_REP.filter(t=>topicState(r.id,t).learnt).length;
    tb.append(h('tr',{class:r.id===S.coachSel?'sel':null},h('td',{class:'name'},h('button',{class:'linkbtn',onclick:()=>{S.coachSel=r.id;render()}},r.name)),
      F.rows.map(x=>h('td',null,x.v!=null&&F.enough?x.f.show(x.v):'—',F.enough?h('div',null,bandPill(x.st)):null)),
      (()=>{const wk=new Set(entriesIn(r.id,addDays(todayStr(),-27),todayStr()).filter(e=>num(e.stops)>0||num(e.hours)>0).map(e=>e.week)).size;
        return[h('td',{text:wk?money(F.t.retail/wk):'—'}),h('td',{text:F.t.hours>0?(F.t.stops/F.t.hours).toFixed(1):'—'}),h('td',{text:F.t.hours>0?money(F.t.retail/F.t.hours):'—'})]})(),
      h('td',{class:'wrapcell'},!F.enough?h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Not enough data'}):F.priority?h('strong',{text:F.priority.f.l}):h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓ All strong'})),
      h('td',{text:`${learnt} / ${TOPICS_REP.length}`})))});
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'By person'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Tap a name to see their coaching plan and topics.'})));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Name'}),FOCUS.map(f=>h('th',{text:f.l})),h('th',{text:'Retail / week'}),h('th',{text:'Stops / hour'}),h('th',{text:'Retail / hour'}),h('th',{text:'Priority focus'}),h('th',{text:'Topics learnt'}))),tb)));
  const sel=S.coachSel&&S.reps[S.coachSel]?S.coachSel:null;
  if(sel){v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:repName(sel)+'’s coaching plan'})));v.append(numbersTable(sel));v.append(averagesTable(sel));v.append(focusCards(sel))}
  // topics learnt checklist with a person picker (team leaders and up only)
  const tros=ros.filter(r=>!(S.isOwner&&r.id===S.uid));const tWho=S.topicSel&&S.reps[S.topicSel]&&!(S.isOwner&&S.topicSel===S.uid)?S.topicSel:((sel&&!(S.isOwner&&sel===S.uid))?sel:(tros[0]&&tros[0].id));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel'},h('div',{class:'field'},h('label',{for:'topicWho',text:'Topics learnt'}),
    h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Only team leaders and above see this. Tick topics off as each person learns them, then again once they\u2019ve run them independently.'}),
    h('select',{id:'topicWho','aria-label':'Person',onchange:e=>{S.topicSel=e.target.value;render()}},tros.map(r=>h('option',{value:r.id,selected:r.id===tWho},r.name+(r.id===S.uid?' (me)':'')))))));
  if(tWho){topicChecklist(tWho,TOPICS_REP,repName(tWho)+' · topics to learn: SA + SC').forEach(x=>v.append(x));topicChecklist(tWho,TOPICS_LEAD,repName(tWho)+' · leader track').forEach(x=>v.append(x))}
  if(!S.isOwner)return;
  const p=h('div',{class:'panel'});
  p.append(h('div',{class:'field'},h('label',{text:'Standards'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'From your Knowing Your Numbers guide. Below Low gets coached first; between Low and Strong is Average.'})));
  const saveStd=(k,field,val)=>{const c={...(S.coach||{})};c[k]={...(c[k]||{}),[field]:val};S.coach=c;write(()=>S.db.doc('settings/coaching').set(c),'Standard saved');render()};
  p.append(h('div',{class:'rules'},FOCUS.map(f=>{const b=band(f);return h('div',{class:'rule'},h('strong',{style:'min-width:150px',text:f.l}),
    h('label',{class:'hint',for:`std-${f.k}-low`,text:'Low under'}),h('input',{type:'text',inputmode:'decimal',class:'num',id:`std-${f.k}-low`,value:String(b.low),onchange:e=>saveStd(f.k,'low',num(e.target.value))}),
    h('label',{class:'hint',for:`std-${f.k}-strong`,text:'Strong from'}),h('input',{type:'text',inputmode:'decimal',class:'num',id:`std-${f.k}-strong`,value:String(b.strong),onchange:e=>saveStd(f.k,'strong',num(e.target.value))}),
    h('span',{class:'hint',text:f.unit==='%'?'%':f.unit==='$'?'dollars':'stops'}))})));
  v.append(p)}

/* ---------- WEEKLY PERFORMANCE FEE ---------- */
const DEFAULT_TIERS=[{rph:100,pct:2},{rph:120,pct:4},{rph:160,pct:7},{rph:200,pct:11}];
function feeCfg(){const b=S.bonus||{};return{minRetail:b.minRetail!=null&&b.minRetail!==''?num(b.minRetail):2000,
  tiers:((b.tiers&&b.tiers.length)?b.tiers:DEFAULT_TIERS).map(x=>({rph:num(x.rph),pct:num(x.pct)})).filter(x=>x.rph>0).sort((a,c)=>a.rph-c.rph)}}
function feeFor(t){const c=feeCfg();const rph=t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:0;let tier=null;c.tiers.forEach(x=>{if(rph>=x.rph)tier=x});
  const unlocked=t.retail>=c.minRetail;const fee=unlocked&&tier?t.retail*tier.pct/100:0;const next=c.tiers.find(x=>rph<x.rph)||null;
  const needRetail=next&&t.hours>0?Math.max(0,Math.ceil(next.rph*t.hours-t.retail)):null;
  return{rph,tier,unlocked,fee,next,needRetail,minGap:Math.max(0,c.minRetail-t.retail),c}}
function feePanel(t,who){const F=feeFor(t);const c=F.c;
  const tips=[];
  if(!t.hours)tips.push('Log hours with your days to work out RPH.');
  else{if(!F.unlocked)tips.push(`${money(F.minGap)} more retail this week unlocks the fee (minimum ${money(c.minRetail)}).`);
    if(F.next)tips.push(`${money(F.needRetail)} more retail at ${Math.round(t.hours*10)/10} hours reaches ${money(F.next.rph)} RPH and ${F.next.pct}%.`);
    else if(F.tier)tips.push('Top tier reached.')}
  return h('div',{class:'panel feebox'},
    h('div',{class:'mstat'},h('div',{class:'lbl',text:who+' performance fee'}),h('div',{class:'big',text:money(F.fee)}),
      h('div',{class:F.fee>0?'achieved':'of',text:F.fee>0?`✓ ${F.tier.pct}% of ${money(t.retail)} retail`:(F.tier&&!F.unlocked?`Tier ${F.tier.pct}% reached, not unlocked yet`:'Not earned yet')})),
    h('div',{class:'mstat'},h('div',{class:'lbl',text:'Minimum retail'}),h('div',{class:'big',text:money(t.retail)}),
      h('div',{class:'prog'+(F.unlocked?' hit':'')},h('i',{style:`width:${Math.min(100,c.minRetail>0?t.retail/c.minRetail*100:100)}%`})),h('div',{class:F.unlocked?'achieved':'of',text:F.unlocked?'✓ Unlocked':`of ${money(c.minRetail)} to unlock`})),
    h('div',{class:'mstat'},h('div',{class:'lbl',text:'Week RPH'}),h('div',{class:'big',text:t.hours?money(F.rph):'—'}),
      h('div',{class:'tiers'},c.tiers.map(x=>h('span',{class:'tierpill'+(F.tier&&x.rph<=F.tier.rph?' on':''),text:`${money(x.rph)} · ${x.pct}%`})))),
    tips.length?h('div',{class:'feetips hint',text:tips.join(' ')}):null)}

/* ---------- BONUS PLANNER: what's needed for the rest of the week ---------- */
function planHours(uid){const r=S.reps[uid]||{};const p=(r.planHours||{})[S.week];return p==null?null:num(p)}
async function setPlanHours(uid,val){const r=S.reps[uid]||{};S.reps[uid]={...r,planHours:{...(r.planHours||{}),[S.week]:val}};if(uid===S.uid)S.me=S.reps[uid];render();
  await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({planHours:{[S.week]:val}}))}
function planFor(uid){const t=weekAll(uid);const c=feeCfg();const left=planHours(uid);const H=t.hours+(left||0);
  const tiers=c.tiers.map(x=>{const target=Math.max(x.rph*H,c.minRetail);const need=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(target-t.retail));
    return{...x,target,need,perHour:left>0?need/left:null,fee:target*x.pct/100,done:need===0&&H>0}});
  return{t,left,H,tiers,cur:feeFor(t)}}
function planCell(x,left){if(x.done)return h('td',null,h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓ On track'}),h('div',{class:'hint small',text:'~'+money(x.fee)}));
  return h('td',null,h('strong',{text:money(x.need)+' more'}),h('div',{class:'hint small',text:left>0?money(x.perHour)+'/hr over '+left+' hrs · ~'+money(x.fee):'add hours left'}))}
function hoursInput(uid){const v=planHours(uid);return h('input',{type:'text',inputmode:'decimal',class:'num',id:'plan-'+uid,value:v==null?'':String(v),placeholder:'0','aria-label':'Hours left this week',
  onchange:e=>setPlanHours(uid,num(e.target.value))})}
function plannerTable(ros){const c=feeCfg();const tb=h('tbody');
  ros.forEach(r=>{const P=planFor(r.id);tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:r.name}),h('td',{text:money(P.t.retail)}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(P.t.hours*10)/10)}),
    h('td',null,P.t.hours?money(P.cur.rph):'—',P.t.hours?h('div',null,zoneEl(P.cur.rph)):null),h('td',null,hoursInput(r.id)),P.tiers.map(x=>planCell(x,P.left))))});
  return h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,['Name','Retail so far','Hours so far','RPH now','Hours left'].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})),c.tiers.map(x=>h('th',{text:`${money(x.rph)} RPH · ${x.pct}%`})))),tb))}
function plannerCard(uid){const P=planFor(uid);const c=feeCfg();
  const box=h('div',{class:'panel rform'});
  box.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h4',{text:'What I need for the rest of the week'}),h('label',{class:'hint',for:'plan-'+uid},'Hours left this week ',hoursInput(uid))));
  if(P.left==null)box.append(h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Enter how many hours you’re working for the rest of the week to see what each tier needs.'}));
  box.append(h('div',{class:'checklist'},P.tiers.map(x=>h('div',{class:'citem'},h('span',{class:'tierpill'+(x.done?' on':''),text:`${money(x.rph)} · ${x.pct}%`}),
    h('span',{text:x.done?`On track at ${Math.round(P.H*10)/10} hrs`:`${money(x.need)} more retail`+(P.left>0?` · ${money(x.perHour)} per hour for ${P.left} hrs`:'')}),
    h('span',{class:'hint',text:'earns ~'+money(x.fee)})))));
  box.append(h('div',{class:'hint',text:`Based on ${money(P.t.retail)} so far over ${Math.round(P.t.hours*10)/10} hrs plus ${P.left||0} hrs left. Minimum ${money(c.minRetail)} retail applies to every tier.`}));
  return box}

/* ---------- BONUSES (leader defines rules, everyone sees progress) ---------- */
const BONUS_METRICS=METRICS.map(m=>[m.k,m.l,m.money?'money':'num']).concat([['rph','RPH','money'],['stopBuyer','Stop to buyer %','pct'],['closeRate','Close rate %','pct'],['closeToBuy','Close to buy %','pct'],['upsellRate','Upsell rate %','pct'],['medalRate','Medal ratio %','pct'],['avgSale','Average sale','money'],['allGoals','All weekly goals hit','flag']]);
const BPERIODS=[['week','Week'],['month','Month'],['quarter','Quarter']];
function bonusRules(){return((S.bonus&&S.bonus.rules)||[])}
function bRange(p){const d=parse(S.date),y=d.getFullYear(),m=d.getMonth();
  if(p==='week')return{a:S.week,b:addDays(S.week,6),label:'week of '+niceDate(S.week,{day:'numeric',month:'short'})};
  if(p==='quarter'){const q=Math.floor(m/3);return{a:toStr(new Date(y,q*3,1)),b:toStr(new Date(y,q*3+3,0)),label:`Q${q+1} ${y}`}}
  return{a:toStr(new Date(y,m,1)),b:toStr(new Date(y,m+1,0)),label:monthName(monthKey(S.date))}}
function bShow(kind,v){return kind==='money'?money(v):kind==='pct'?Math.round(v)+'%':kind==='flag'?(v?'Yes':'No'):String(Math.round(v*10)/10)}
function bonusStatus(rule,uid){const R=bRange(rule.period||'week');const t=totalsIn([uid],R.a,R.b);const meta=BONUS_METRICS.find(x=>x[0]===rule.metric)||BONUS_METRICS[0];
  let v;if(rule.metric==='allGoals'){const gh=goalsHit(totalsFor([uid],'week',S.week),goalOf(uid,S.week));v=gh&&gh.hit===gh.set?1:0}
  else if(METRICS.some(m=>m.k===rule.metric))v=t[rule.metric];
  else{const f=RATES[rule.metric];v=f?f(t):null}
  const min=rule.metric==='allGoals'?1:num(rule.min);const achieved=v!=null&&min>0&&v>=min;
  return{v:v||0,min,achieved,pct:min>0?Math.min(100,(v||0)/min*100):0,kind:meta[2],label:R.label}}
function saveRules(rules){S.bonus={...(S.bonus||{}),rules};render();return write(()=>S.db.doc('settings/bonus').set({rules}))}
function bonusRow(rule,uid){const s=bonusStatus(rule,uid);
  return h('div',{class:'brow'+(s.achieved?' done':'')},h('div',{class:'bmain'},h('strong',{text:rule.name||'Bonus'}),
      h('span',{class:'hint',text:` · ${(BONUS_METRICS.find(x=>x[0]===rule.metric)||[,''])[1]}${rule.metric==='allGoals'?'':' ≥ '+bShow(s.kind,s.min)} · ${s.label}`})),
    h('div',{class:'bside'},rule.reward?h('span',{class:'reward',text:rule.reward}):null,
      s.achieved?h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓ Achieved'}):h('span',{class:'hint',text:rule.metric==='allGoals'?'Not yet':`${bShow(s.kind,s.v)} of ${bShow(s.kind,s.min)}`})),
    rule.metric==='allGoals'?null:h('div',{class:'prog'+(s.achieved?' hit':'')},h('i',{style:`width:${s.pct}%`})))}
function weekNav(){return h('div',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px'},h('button',{onclick:()=>setDate(addDays(S.week,-7))},'‹ Last week'),h('button',{onclick:()=>setDate(addDays(S.week,7))},'Next week ›'))}
function bonusOn(uid){return stageIdx(uid)>=1||(S.isOwner&&uid===S.uid)}
function bonusLock(uid){const sp=salesProgress(uid,STAGES[0]);return h('div',{class:'panel',style:'text-align:center;padding:22px'},h('div',{style:'font-size:2rem',text:'🔒'}),h('h3',{text:'Bonus unlocks at Sales Consultant'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Hit '+STAGES[0].sales.label+' and get ticked off as SC by your leader to unlock your weekly performance fee.'}),sp?h('div',{style:'margin-top:10px'},bar(sp.pct,sp.done),h('div',{class:'hint',text:sp.line})):null)}
function renderBonus(v){const rules=bonusRules();const c=feeCfg();
  if(!leader()){
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'My bonus · week of '+niceDate(S.week,{day:'numeric',month:'long'})}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Your weekly performance fee is worked out from your week’s retail and RPH. This is an estimate; your leader confirms the final amount.'})),weekNav()));
    if(!bonusOn(S.uid)){v.append(bonusLock(S.uid));return}
    v.append(feePanel(weekAll(S.uid),'My'));v.append(plannerCard(S.uid));
    if(rules.length){const won=rules.filter(r=>bonusStatus(r,S.uid).achieved).length;
      v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Extra incentives'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:`${won} of ${rules.length} achieved`})));
      v.append(h('div',{class:'panel blist'},rules.map(r=>bonusRow(r,S.uid))))}
    return}
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'Bonus · week of '+niceDate(S.week,{day:'numeric',month:'long'})}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Weekly performance fee for each person. Reps see only their own.'})),weekNav()));
  // team fee table
  const ros=repList(false).filter(r=>r.active!==false||entriesFor(r.id,'week',S.week).length);
  const ftb=h('tbody');let totalFee=0;
  ros.map(r=>({r,t:weekAll(r.id)})).sort((a,b)=>bonusOn(b.r.id)-bonusOn(a.r.id)||feeFor(b.t).fee-feeFor(a.t).fee||b.t.retail-a.t.retail).forEach(({r,t})=>{if(!bonusOn(r.id)){ftb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:r.name}),h('td',{text:money(t.retail)}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.hours*10)/10)}),h('td',{text:t.hours?money(t.retail/t.hours):'—'}),h('td',{colspan:4,class:'hint',text:'🔒 Locked until ticked off as Sales Consultant'})));return}const F=feeFor(t);if(!r.test)totalFee+=F.fee;
    ftb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:r.name}),h('td',{text:money(t.retail)}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.hours*10)/10)}),
      h('td',{text:t.hours?money(F.rph):'—'}),h('td',null,F.unlocked?h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓'}):h('span',{class:'hint',text:money(F.minGap)+' to go'})),
      h('td',{text:F.tier?F.tier.pct+'%':'—'}),h('td',null,F.fee>0?h('strong',{text:money(F.fee)}):'$0'),
      h('td',{class:'hint',text:F.next&&t.hours?`${money(F.needRetail)} more → ${F.next.pct}%`:(F.tier?'Top tier':'')})))});
  if(!ros.length)ftb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{colspan:8,class:'hint',text:'No one has joined yet.'})));
  else ftb.append(h('tr',{class:'total'},h('td',{text:'Total fees'}),h('td'),h('td'),h('td'),h('td'),h('td'),h('td',{text:money(totalFee)}),h('td')));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,['Name','Retail','Hours','Week RPH',`${money(c.minRetail)} min`,'Tier','Fee','Next tier'].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})))),ftb)));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Rest of the week planner'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Enter each person\u2019s hours left this week to see the retail needed for every tier, and per hour.'})));
  v.append(plannerTable(repList(true).filter(r=>bonusOn(r.id))));
  // structure editor
  if(!S.isOwner){v.append(h('div',{class:'hint',text:'The fee structure is set by Ameliah.'}));return}
  const se=h('div',{class:'panel'});
  se.append(h('div',{class:'field'},h('label',{text:'Performance fee structure'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Fee = tier % of personal retail for the week, paid only once the minimum weekly retail is reached. RPH = total retail ÷ total hours.'})));
  const saveFee=patch=>{S.bonus={...(S.bonus||{}),...patch};render();write(()=>S.db.doc('settings/bonus').set(S.bonus),'Saved')};
  se.append(h('div',{class:'rules'},
    h('div',{class:'rule'},h('label',{for:'feeMin',class:'hint',text:'Minimum weekly retail ($)'}),h('input',{type:'text',inputmode:'decimal',class:'num money',id:'feeMin',value:String(c.minRetail),onchange:e=>saveFee({minRetail:num(e.target.value)})})),
    c.tiers.map((x,i)=>h('div',{class:'rule'},h('span',{class:'hint',text:`Tier ${i+1}`}),
      h('label',{class:'hint',for:'tierR'+i,text:'RPH from $'}),h('input',{type:'text',inputmode:'decimal',class:'num',id:'tierR'+i,value:String(x.rph),onchange:e=>{const n=c.tiers.map((y,j)=>j===i?{...y,rph:num(e.target.value)}:y);saveFee({tiers:n})}}),
      h('label',{class:'hint',for:'tierP'+i,text:'pays %'}),h('input',{type:'text',inputmode:'decimal',class:'num',id:'tierP'+i,value:String(x.pct),onchange:e=>{const n=c.tiers.map((y,j)=>j===i?{...y,pct:num(e.target.value)}:y);saveFee({tiers:n})}}),
      h('button',{class:'icon','aria-label':'Remove tier',onclick:()=>saveFee({tiers:c.tiers.filter((_,j)=>j!==i)})},'×')))));
  se.append(h('div',{class:'formfoot'},h('button',{onclick:()=>{const last=c.tiers[c.tiers.length-1]||{rph:0,pct:0};saveFee({tiers:c.tiers.concat([{rph:last.rph+40,pct:last.pct+2}])})}},'+ Add a tier')));
  v.append(se);
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Extra incentives'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Optional bonuses on top of the fee, such as a monthly medal target or a quarterly retail prize.'})));
  if(needsTeam()){v.append(h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Loading the team’s history…'})));loadAllTeam();}
  else if(rules.length){const ros=repList(true);
    const tb=h('tbody');ros.forEach(r=>{let won=0;const cells=rules.map(rule=>{const s=bonusStatus(rule,r.id);if(s.achieved)won++;
        return h('td',null,s.achieved?h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓'}):h('span',{class:'hint',text:rule.metric==='allGoals'?'—':Math.round(s.pct)+'%'}))});
      tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:r.name}),cells,h('td',null,won?h('span',{class:'achieved',text:String(won)}):'0')))});
    if(!ros.length)tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{colspan:rules.length+2,class:'hint',text:'No one has joined yet.'})));
    v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Name'}),rules.map(r=>h('th',{text:(r.name||'Bonus')+' · '+((BPERIODS.find(p=>p[0]===r.period)||[,'Week'])[1])})),h('th',{text:'Achieved'}))),tb)));
    v.append(h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Percentages show progress toward the target for the period around the selected date.'}))}
  const ed=h('div',{class:'panel'});ed.append(h('div',{class:'field'},h('label',{text:'Incentive rules'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'One row per incentive. For tiers, add a row for each tier with rising targets.'})));
  const list=h('div',{class:'rules'});
  rules.forEach((r,i)=>{const upd=patch=>{const n=rules.map((x,j)=>j===i?{...x,...patch}:x);saveRules(n)};
    list.append(h('div',{class:'rule'},
      h('input',{type:'text',id:`b-${r.id}-name`,value:r.name||'',placeholder:'Bonus name','aria-label':'Bonus name',onchange:e=>upd({name:e.target.value.trim()})}),
      h('select',{id:`b-${r.id}-metric`,'aria-label':'Based on',onchange:e=>upd({metric:e.target.value})},BONUS_METRICS.map(([k,l])=>h('option',{value:k,selected:r.metric===k},l))),
      h('select',{id:`b-${r.id}-period`,'aria-label':'Period',onchange:e=>upd({period:e.target.value})},BPERIODS.map(([k,l])=>h('option',{value:k,selected:r.period===k},l))),
      r.metric==='allGoals'?h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Weekly only'}):h('input',{type:'text',inputmode:'decimal',class:'num',id:`b-${r.id}-min`,value:r.min!=null?String(r.min):'',placeholder:'Target','aria-label':'Target',onchange:e=>upd({min:num(e.target.value)})}),
      h('input',{type:'text',id:`b-${r.id}-reward`,value:r.reward||'',placeholder:'Reward, e.g. $100','aria-label':'Reward',onchange:e=>upd({reward:e.target.value.trim()})}),
      h('button',{class:'icon','aria-label':'Remove bonus',onclick:()=>{if(S.confirmDel!=='b'+r.id){S.confirmDel='b'+r.id;render();return}S.confirmDel=null;saveRules(rules.filter((_,j)=>j!==i))}},S.confirmDel==='b'+r.id?'Remove?':'×')))});
  ed.append(list);
  ed.append(h('div',{class:'formfoot'},h('button',{onclick:()=>saveRules(rules.concat([{id:Date.now().toString(36),name:'',metric:'retail',period:'week',min:'',reward:''}]))},'+ Add an incentive')));
  v.append(ed)}

/* ---------- REFLECT: daily reflection + weekly review ---------- */
const STEP_DEFAULTS=['Have a world class attitude','Maintain this attitude','Be on time','Be prepared','Take small breaks','Utilise your KPI tracker','Work your territory / site','Take control','Apply discretionary effort'];
const STEP_MAX=[20,10,10,10,10,10,10,10,10];
function stepNames(){const n=(S.steps&&S.steps.names)||[];return STEP_DEFAULTS.map((d,i)=>(n[i]&&String(n[i]).trim())||d)}
function fieldScore(steps){const st=steps||[];if(!st.some(Boolean))return null;return st.reduce((a,x)=>a+(num(x)||0),0)}
const REV_KPIS=[['retail','Retail'],['stops','Stops'],['buyers','Buyers'],['stopBuyer','Stop to buyer %'],['avgSale','Sale average'],['upsells','Upsells'],['upsellRate','Buyers to upsells %'],['medals','Medals'],['rph','RPH'],['hours','Hours']];
function kpiVal(k,t){if(RATES[k])return RATES[k](t);return t[k]}
function kpiShow(k,v){if(v==null)return'—';if(['stopBuyer','upsellRate'].includes(k))return Math.round(v)+'%';if(['retail','avgSale','rph'].includes(k))return money(v);return String(Math.round(v*10)/10)}
async function loadDocs(kind,uids,key){const need=uids.filter(u=>!((u+'|'+key) in S[kind]));if(!need.length)return;
  need.forEach(u=>S[kind][u+'|'+key]=undefined);
  await Promise.all(need.map(async u=>{try{const d=await S.db.doc(`reps/${u}/${kind==='refl'?'reflections':kind==='hrs'?'hourly':'reviews'}/${key}`).get();S[kind][u+'|'+key]=d.exists?d.data():null}catch(_){S[kind][u+'|'+key]=null}}));render()}
function draftOf(kind,uid,key){const id=kind+'|'+uid+'|'+key;if(!S.rDraft[id]){const saved=S[kind][uid+'|'+key];S.rDraft[id]=saved?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(saved)):{}}return S.rDraft[id]}
function txt(id,obj,field,ph,area){const el=h(area?'textarea':'input',area?{id,rows:2,placeholder:ph,'aria-label':ph,oninput:e=>{obj[field]=e.target.value;markDirty()}}:{id,type:'text',placeholder:ph,'aria-label':ph,oninput:e=>{obj[field]=e.target.value;markDirty()}});el.value=obj[field]||'';return el}
function markDirty(){const b=document.getElementById('rSave');if(b){b.disabled=false;b.textContent='Save'}}
function scale(id,val,onpick,max){return h('div',{class:'scale',role:'radiogroup','aria-label':id},Array.from({length:max||10},(_,i)=>i+1).map(n=>h('button',{type:'button',id:`${id}-${n}`,class:val===n?'on':null,'aria-checked':String(val===n),role:'radio',onclick:()=>onpick(n)},String(n))))}
async function saveDoc(kind,uid,key){const d=draftOf(kind,uid,key);const body={...d,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
  const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc(`reps/${uid}/${kind==='refl'?'reflections':'reviews'}/${key}`).set(body),kind==='refl'?'Reflection saved':'Weekly review saved');
  if(ok){S[kind][uid+'|'+key]=body;render()}}
function reflForm(v,uid){const key=S.date;const loaded=(uid+'|'+key) in S.refl&&S.refl[uid+'|'+key]!==undefined;
  if(!loaded){loadDocs('refl',[uid],key);v.append(h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Loading…'})));return}
  const d=draftOf('refl',uid,key);d.well=d.well||['','',''];d.learn=d.learn||['',''];d.steps=d.steps||[];const saved=S.refl[uid+'|'+key];
  const t=rowVals(uid,S.date);
  v.append(h('div',{class:'notice'},`Today: ${t.stops} stops · ${t.buyers} buyers · ${money(t.retail)} retail · ${t.upsells} upsells · ${t.medals} medals`+(t.hours>0?` · ${money(t.retail/t.hours)} RPH`:'')));
  const f=h('div',{class:'panel rform'});if(saved&&saved.fromHourly)f.append(h('div',{class:'hint',style:'margin-bottom:6px',text:'✓ Wins and learnings came through from your Hourly end of day. Add anything else here.'}));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'3 wins'}),...d.well.map((_,i)=>txt('rf-well'+i,d.well,i,`Win ${i+1}`)));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'2 learnings'}),...d.learn.map((_,i)=>txt('rf-learn'+i,d.learn,i,`Learning ${i+1}`)));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'1 focus for tomorrow'}),txt('rf-focus',d,'focus','My focus'));
  const fs=fieldScore(d.steps);
  f.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h4',{text:'9 steps · field performance'}),h('strong',{class:'fscore',text:(fs!=null?fs:'—')+' / 100'})));
  stepNames().forEach((n,i)=>f.append(h('div',{class:'steprow'},h('span',{text:`${i+1}. ${n} (/${STEP_MAX[i]})`}),scale('rf-step'+i,d.steps[i]||null,val=>{d.steps[i]=val;render();markDirty()},STEP_MAX[i]))));
  f.append(h('div',{class:'formfoot'},h('span',{class:'hint',text:saved?'Saved '+new Date(saved.updatedAt).toLocaleString('en-AU',{weekday:'short',hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'}):'Not saved yet'}),
    h('button',{class:'primary',id:'rSave',onclick:()=>saveDoc('refl',uid,key)},saved?'Saved':'Save reflection')));
  v.append(f)}
function revWknd(){const g=new Date(todayStr()+'T12:00:00').getDay();return g===5||g===6||g===0}
function rwk(){const cw=weekStart(todayStr());if(S.week===cw)return revWknd()?cw:addDays(cw,-7);return S.week}
function revKey(){return addDays(rwk(),7)}
function revNudge(v){if(!S.uid||!revWknd()||S.week!==weekStart(todayStr()))return;const k=S.uid+'|'+revKey();if(!(k in S.rev)){loadDocs('rev',[S.uid],revKey());return}if(S.rev[k]!==null)return;
  v.append(h('div',{class:'notice',style:'display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap'},h('span',{text:'📝 Weekly review is open for WC '+niceDate(rwk(),{day:'numeric',month:'short'})+' — takes 2 minutes. Due before Monday.'}),h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>{S.tab='reflect';S.reflView='weekly';if(leadRole()&&!S.viewMe){S.viewMe=true;onWeekChange()}render();scrollTo(0,0)}},'Do it now')))}
function lastWeekInfo(uid){const lw=rwk();const t=totalsFor([uid],'week',lw);
  if(!t.stops&&!t.retail){const x=blank();entriesIn(uid,lw,addDays(lw,6)).forEach(e=>sumInto(x,e));return{lw,t:x}}return{lw,t}}
function reviewForm(v,uid){const key=revKey();const loaded=(uid+'|'+key) in S.rev&&S.rev[uid+'|'+key]!==undefined;
  if(!loaded){loadDocs('rev',[uid],key);v.append(h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Loading…'})));return}
  const d=draftOf('rev',uid,key);const saved=S.rev[uid+'|'+key];const {lw,t}=lastWeekInfo(uid);const rph=t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:null;const z=zone(rph);
  const f=h('div',{class:'panel rform'});
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'What were your KPIs for WC '+niceDate(lw,{day:'numeric',month:'short'})+'?'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Filled in from your logged days'}),
    h('div',{class:'chips'},REV_KPIS.map(([k,l])=>h('span',null,l+' ',h('b',{text:kpiShow(k,kpiVal(k,t))})))),txt('rv-kpinotes',d,'kpiNotes','Anything to add about last week?',true));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'What zone did you achieve for WC '+niceDate(lw,{day:'numeric',month:'short'})+'?'}),h('div',null,z?h('span',{class:'zone '+z[1],text:z[0]+' · '+money(rph)+' RPH'}):h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Log hours to see your zone'})));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'Which KPI from WC '+niceDate(lw,{day:'numeric',month:'short'})+' are you trying to beat this week?'}),
    h('div',{class:'rule'},h('select',{id:'rv-kpi','aria-label':'KPI to beat',onchange:e=>{d.beatKpi=e.target.value;render();markDirty()}},h('option',{value:''},'Choose a KPI'),REV_KPIS.map(([k,l])=>h('option',{value:k,selected:d.beatKpi===k},l))),
      d.beatKpi?h('span',{class:'hint',text:`WC ${niceDate(lw,{day:'numeric',month:'short'})}: ${kpiShow(d.beatKpi,kpiVal(d.beatKpi,t))}. Next week so far: ${kpiShow(d.beatKpi,kpiVal(d.beatKpi,totalsFor([uid],'week',revKey())))}`}):null));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'What went well?'}),txt('rv-w1',d,'well1','1.'),txt('rv-w2',d,'well2','2.'),txt('rv-w3',d,'well3','3.'));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'What education do you need?'}),txt('rv-edu',d,'education','Training I need',true));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'What will you be doing to make sure you beat that KPI?'}),txt('rv-plan',d,'plan','My plan',true));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'Rate your clarity / motivation out of 10'}),scale('rv-motiv',d.motivation||null,val=>{d.motivation=val;render();markDirty()}));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'What is motivating you at the moment?'}),txt('rv-why',d,'motivating','What’s motivating me',true));
  f.append(h('h4',{text:'What would you like your mentor to do more of to support you?'}),txt('rv-mentor',d,'mentor','From my mentor I’d like…',true));
  f.append(h('div',{class:'formfoot'},h('span',{class:'hint',text:saved?'Saved '+new Date(saved.updatedAt).toLocaleString('en-AU',{weekday:'short',hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'}):'Not saved yet'}),
    h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:8px'},h('button',{onclick:async()=>{let ok=false;ok=await copyRich(reviewText(uid,d));toast(ok?'Copied — paste into Telegram':'Couldn’t copy on this device')}},'📋 Copy to Telegram'),
    h('button',{class:'primary',id:'rSave',onclick:()=>saveDoc('rev',uid,key)},saved?'Saved':'Save weekly review'))));
  v.append(f)}
function reviewText(uid,d){const {lw,t}=lastWeekInfo(uid);const dw=new Set(entriesIn(uid,lw,addDays(lw,6)).filter(e=>num(e.stops)||num(e.retail)||num(e.hours)).map(e=>e.date)).size;const hr=t.hours||0,rph=hr?t.retail/hr:null,z=zone(rph);const r1=x=>Math.round(x*10)/10;
  const kl=(REV_KPIS.find(x=>x[0]===d.beatKpi)||[,''])[1];
  const L=['**Weekly Review**','','**Review - WC: '+lw.slice(8,10).replace(/^0/,'')+'/'+lw.slice(5,7).replace(/^0/,'')+'**','','**KPIs:**',
    '- **Stops p/h:** '+(hr?r1(t.stops/hr):'—'),'- **Stops p/d:** '+(dw?r1(t.stops/dw):'—'),'- **Buyers p/h:** '+(hr?r1(t.buyers/hr):'—'),'- **Buyers p/d:** '+(dw?r1(t.buyers/dw):'—'),
    '- **$AV:** '+(t.buyers?money(t.retail/t.buyers):'—'),'- **Retail $:** '+money(t.retail||0),'- **RPH:** '+(rph!=null?r1(rph)+(z?' ('+z[0].toLowerCase()+')':''):'—'),
    '- **HRS:** '+(hr?r1(hr):'—'),'- **Upsell%:** '+(t.buyers?Math.round(100*(t.upsells||0)/t.buyers)+'%':'—'),'',
    '**What went well?**','1. '+(d.well1||''),'2. '+(d.well2||''),'3. '+(d.well3||''),'','**What education do you need?**',d.education||'',
    '','**Which KPI are you trying to beat this week?**',kl?kl+' (last week '+kpiShow(d.beatKpi,kpiVal(d.beatKpi,t))+')':'',
    '','**What will you be doing to make sure you beat that KPI?**',d.plan||'',
    '','**Rate your clarity / motivation out of 10**',d.motivation?d.motivation+'/10':'',
    '','**What is motivating you at the moment?**',d.motivating||'',
    '','**What would you like your mentor to do more of to support you?**',d.mentor||''];
  return L.join('\n')}
function reflView(r){if(!r)return h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Not submitted.'});const names=stepNames();
  return h('div',{class:'rview'},
    h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Field performance: '}),fieldScore(r.steps)!=null?fieldScore(r.steps)+' / 100':'—'),
    h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Wins: '}),(r.well||[]).filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'—'),
    h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Learnings: '}),(r.learn||[]).filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'—'),
    h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Focus: '}),r.focus||'—'),
    h('div',{class:'chips'},names.map((n,i)=>h('span',null,n+' ',h('b',{text:(r.steps||[])[i]?r.steps[i]+'/'+STEP_MAX[i]:'—'})))))}
function reviewView(r,uid){if(!r)return h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Not submitted.'});const {t}=lastWeekInfo(uid);
  const kl=(REV_KPIS.find(x=>x[0]===r.beatKpi)||[,''])[1];
  return h('div',{class:'rview'},
    r.kpiNotes?h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Last week: '}),r.kpiNotes):null,
    h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Beating: '}),kl?`${kl} (WC ${niceDate(rwk(),{day:'numeric',month:'short'})}: ${kpiShow(r.beatKpi,kpiVal(r.beatKpi,t))})`:'—'),
    (r.well1||r.well2||r.well3)?h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Went well: '}),[r.well1,r.well2,r.well3].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')):null,
    r.education?h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Education needed: '}),r.education):null,
    h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Plan: '}),r.plan||'—'),
    h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Clarity / motivation: '}),r.motivation?r.motivation+'/10':'—'),
    h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'Motivated by: '}),r.motivating||'—'),
    h('div',null,h('strong',{text:'From mentor: '}),r.mentor||'—'))}
function renderReflect(v){
  v.append(h('div',{class:'seg'},[['daily','Daily reflection'],['weekly','Weekly review']].map(([k,l])=>h('button',{class:S.reflView===k?'primary':null,'aria-pressed':String(S.reflView===k),onclick:()=>{S.reflView=k;render()}},l))));
  const daily=S.reflView!=='weekly';
  if(false){v.append(h('div',{class:'seg'},[['team','Team'],['me','Mine']].map(([k,l])=>h('button',{class:S.reflWho===k?'primary':null,'aria-pressed':String(S.reflWho===k),onclick:()=>{S.reflWho=k;render()}},l))))}
  if(!leader()){
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:daily?'Reflection · '+niceDate(S.date,{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long'}):'Weekly review · WC '+niceDate(rwk(),{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'})}),
      h('div',{class:'hint',text:daily?(leader()?'Your own end-of-day reflection. Only you can see it.':'Do this at the end of each shift. Only you and your leader see it.'):'Reviewing the week commencing '+niceDate(rwk(),{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long'})+'. '+(revWknd()?'Weekly review is open Fri–Sun — do it before Monday.':'Best done Fri–Sun. Use the arrows at the top to pick another week.')}))));
    daily?reflForm(v,S.uid):reviewForm(v,S.uid);return}
  const ros=repList(true);
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:daily?'Reflections · '+niceDate(S.date,{weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long'}):'Weekly reviews · WC '+niceDate(rwk(),{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'})}),
    h('div',{class:'hint',text:daily?'Who has reflected today, and how they rate themselves on the 9 steps. Use the date at the top to look at other days.':'Each person’s review of the week commencing '+niceDate(rwk(),{day:'numeric',month:'long'})+'. Use the date at the top to change week.'})),
    h('button',{onclick:()=>{const k=daily?S.date:revKey();Object.keys(S[daily?'refl':'rev']).forEach(x=>{if(x.endsWith('|'+k))delete S[daily?'refl':'rev'][x]});render()}},'Refresh')));
  if(!ros.length){v.append(noReps());return}
  const key=daily?S.date:revKey();const kind=daily?'refl':'rev';loadDocs(kind,ros.map(r=>r.id),key);
  if(daily){const names=stepNames();const sums=Array(9).fill(0),cnt=Array(9).fill(0);
    const tb=h('tbody');ros.forEach(r=>{const d=S.refl[r.id+'|'+key];const st=(d&&d.steps)||[];const rated=st.map((x,i)=>[x,i]).filter(x=>x[0]);
      rated.forEach(([x,i])=>{sums[i]+=x/STEP_MAX[i]*10;cnt[i]++});const avg=fieldScore(st);const low=rated.length?rated.reduce((a,x)=>x[0]/STEP_MAX[x[1]]<a[0]/STEP_MAX[a[1]]?x:a):null;
      tb.append(h('tr',{class:S.reflSel===r.id?'sel':null},h('td',{class:'name'},h('button',{class:'linkbtn',onclick:()=>{S.reflSel=r.id;render()}},r.name)),
        h('td',null,d===undefined?'…':d?h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓'}):h('span',{class:'hint',text:'—'})),h('td',{text:avg!=null?avg+' / 100':'—'}),
        h('td',{class:'wrapcell',text:low?`${names[low[1]]} (${low[0]}/${STEP_MAX[low[1]]})`:'—'}),h('td',{class:'wrapcell',text:(d&&d.focus)||''})))});
    const done=ros.filter(r=>S.refl[r.id+'|'+key]).length;
    v.append(h('div',{class:'notice'+(done===ros.length?' win':'')},`${done} of ${ros.length} have reflected.`));
    v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,['Name','Done','Field performance','Lowest step','Focus'].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})))),tb)));
    if(cnt.some(Boolean)){v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Team step ratings today'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Average out of 10 (World Class Attitude scaled from 20). Lowest steps are where to train.'})));
      const avgs=names.map((n,i)=>({n,a:cnt[i]?sums[i]/cnt[i]:null})).filter(x=>x.a!=null);const minA=Math.min(...avgs.map(x=>x.a));
      v.append(h('div',{class:'panel stepbars'},avgs.map(x=>h('div',{class:'sbar'},h('span',{text:x.n}),h('div',{class:'prog'+(x.a===minA?' lowbar':'')},h('i',{style:`width:${x.a*10}%`})),h('b',{text:x.a.toFixed(1)})))))}
    if(S.reflSel&&S.reps[S.reflSel]){v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:repName(S.reflSel)+'’s reflection'})));v.append(h('div',{class:'panel'},reflView(S.refl[S.reflSel+'|'+key])))}
    // step names editor
    if(S.isOwner){const ed=h('details',{class:'guide'});ed.append(h('summary',{text:'Edit the 9 steps'}));
    ed.append(h('div',{class:'rules'},stepNames().map((n,i)=>h('div',{class:'rule'},h('span',{class:'hint',text:String(i+1)}),h('input',{type:'text',id:'stepname'+i,value:n,'aria-label':'Step '+(i+1)+' name',
      onchange:e=>{const arr=stepNames().slice();arr[i]=e.target.value.trim()||STEP_DEFAULTS[i];S.steps={names:arr};write(()=>S.db.doc('settings/steps').set({names:arr}),'Step renamed');render()}})))));
    v.append(ed)}
  }else{
    const tb=h('tbody');ros.forEach(r=>{const d=S.rev[r.id+'|'+key];const {t}=lastWeekInfo(r.id);const z=zone(t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:null);
      tb.append(h('tr',{class:S.reflSel===r.id?'sel':null},h('td',{class:'name'},h('button',{class:'linkbtn',onclick:()=>{S.reflSel=r.id;render()}},r.name)),
        h('td',null,d===undefined?'…':d?h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓'}):h('span',{class:'hint',text:'—'})),
        h('td',null,z?h('span',{class:'zone '+z[1],text:z[0]}):'—'),h('td',{text:d&&d.beatKpi?(REV_KPIS.find(x=>x[0]===d.beatKpi)||[,''])[1]:'—'}),
        h('td',null,d&&d.motivation?h('span',{class:d.motivation<=5?'gap':null,text:d.motivation+'/10'}):'—'),h('td',{class:'wrapcell',text:(d&&d.mentor)||''})))});
    const done=ros.filter(r=>S.rev[r.id+'|'+key]).length;
    v.append(h('div',{class:'notice'+(done===ros.length?' win':'')},`${done} of ${ros.length} have done their weekly review.`));
    v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,['Name','Done','Zone (WC '+niceDate(rwk(),{day:'numeric',month:'short'})+')','Beating this week','Clarity / motivation','Wants from mentor'].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})))),tb)));
    if(S.reflSel&&S.reps[S.reflSel]){v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:repName(S.reflSel)+'’s weekly review'})));v.append(h('div',{class:'panel'},reviewView(S.rev[S.reflSel+'|'+key],S.reflSel)))}
  }}

/* ---------- PROMOTION TRACKER: sales progress + team members progress ---------- */
function hist(uid){return sourceAll(uid,'period').filter(e=>num(e.stops)>0||num(e.retail)>0||num(e.hours)>0).sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date))}
function bestRolling(uid,n){const L=hist(uid);let best=0,sum=0;for(let i=0;i<L.length;i++){sum+=num(L[i].retail);if(i>=n)sum-=num(L[i-n].retail);if(i>=n-1||L.length<n)best=Math.max(best,sum)}return best}
function weekMap(uids){const w={};uids.forEach(u=>hist(u).forEach(e=>w[e.week]=(w[e.week]||0)+num(e.retail)));return w}
/* best back-to-back: the highest "lower of two consecutive weeks" */
function bestB2B(uids){const w=weekMap(uids);let best=0;Object.keys(w).forEach(k=>{const prev=w[addDays(k,-7)]||0;best=Math.max(best,Math.min(w[k],prev))});return best}
function teamMembers(uid){return repListAll(false).filter(r=>r.id===uid||r.team===uid).map(r=>r.id)}
/* the path: each step has a sales target and (for leadership) a team target */
const STAGES=[
  {k:'sa',from:'Sales Associate',to:'Sales Consultant',sales:{kind:'10days',amt:5000,label:'$5,000 retail in 10 days'},extras:[{k:'video',l:'Video pitch sent to journey chat',optional:true}]},
  {k:'sc',from:'Sales Consultant',to:'Sales Leader',sales:{kind:'b2b',amt:3000,label:'$3,000 back-to-back weeks',alt:'One week was a roadie and you hit $5,000'},need:2,extras:[{k:'site',l:'Run a successful site ($1,200 retail)'},{k:'bell',l:'Solo bell ($700 retail)'},{k:'gong',l:'City gong ($1,000 retail)'}]},
  {k:'sl',from:'Sales Leader',to:'Team Leader',sales:{kind:'teamb2b',amt:15000,label:'$15,000 back-to-back weeks'},team:{direct:2,total:3,what:'Sales Leaders'}},
  {k:'tl',from:'Team Leader',to:'Team Manager',sales:{kind:'teamb2b',amt:25000,label:'$25,000 back-to-back weeks'},team:{direct:3,total:5,what:'leaders'}},
  {k:'tm',from:'Team Manager',to:'Assistant Director',sales:{kind:'teamb2b',amt:35000,label:'$35,000 back-to-back weeks'},team:{direct:4,total:6,what:'leaders'}},
  {k:'ad',from:'Assistant Director',to:'Director',sales:{kind:'teamb2b',amt:45000,label:'$45,000 back-to-back weeks'},team:{direct:5,total:8,what:'leaders'}},
  {k:'dir',from:'Director',to:'Managing Director',appointed:true},
  {k:'md',from:'Managing Director',top:true}];
function promo(){return STAGES}
function promoOf(uid){return (S.reps[uid]||{}).promo||{}}
function stageIdx(uid){const s=promoOf(uid).stage;const i=STAGES.findIndex(p=>p.k===s);return i<0?0:i}
function roleName(p){return p.from}
function lastN(uid,n){const L=hist(uid).filter(e=>e.date<=todayStr());return L.slice(-n).reduce((a,e)=>a+num(e.retail),0)}
/* sales progress resets each week: the bar is this week's retail; two weeks in a row at target completes it */
function salesProgress(uid,p){if(!p.sales)return null;const s=p.sales;const m=(promoOf(uid).m||{})[p.k+'_sales'];
  if(s.kind==='10days'){const cur=lastN(uid,10),best=bestRolling(uid,10);const ever=best>=s.amt;
    return{v:cur,amt:s.amt,pct:Math.min(100,cur/s.amt*100),done:ever||!!m,manual:!!m&&!ever,label:s.label,alt:s.alt,
      line:`Last 10 days: ${money(cur)} of ${money(s.amt)}`+(ever&&cur<s.amt?` · achieved (best ${money(best)})`:''),short:ever?'✓':`${money(cur)} of ${money(s.amt)}`}}
  const uids=s.kind==='b2b'?[uid]:[uid].concat(downOf(uid).map(r=>r.id));const w=weekMap(uids);const wk=weekStart(todayStr());const cur=w[wk]||0,last=w[addDays(wk,-7)]||0;
  const ever=bestB2B(uids)>=s.amt;const lastHit=last>=s.amt;
  return{v:cur,last,amt:s.amt,pct:Math.min(100,cur/s.amt*100),done:ever||!!m,manual:!!m&&!ever,label:s.label,alt:s.alt,lastHit,
    line:`${s.kind==='teamb2b'?'Team this':'This'} week: ${money(cur)} of ${money(s.amt)} · last week ${money(last)}${lastHit?' ✓':''}`+(ever?' · back-to-back achieved':lastHit?' · hit it again this week to complete':''),
    short:ever?'✓ achieved':`${money(cur)} of ${money(s.amt)} this week`}}
function ownerId(){return S.isOwner?S.uid:'u_owner'}
function parentOf(r){if(!r.team)return r.id===ownerId()?null:ownerId();if(r.team===r.id)return null;return r.team}
function kidsOf(uid,incTest){return repListAll(false,true).filter(r=>r.id!==uid&&(incTest||!r.test)&&r.active!==false&&parentOf(r)===uid)}
function downOf(uid,seen){seen=seen||{[uid]:1};let out=[];kidsOf(uid).forEach(r=>{if(seen[r.id])return;seen[r.id]=1;out.push(r);out=out.concat(downOf(r.id,seen))});return out}
const STAGE_COL={sa:['#ece7e8','#2a1a1f'],sc:['#f6dfb0','#3d2a06'],sl:['#f4c2bf','#4a1418'],tl:['#e58a86','#3a0c10'],tm:['#b83c3c','#fff'],ad:['#424965','#fff'],dir:['#40101b','#fff'],md:['#d4a24c','#2a1a05']};
function stCol(uid){const k=STAGES[stageIdx(uid)].k;const c=STAGE_COL[k]||STAGE_COL.sa;return 'background:'+c[0]+';color:'+c[1]+';border-color:'+c[0]}
function treeLegend(){return h('div',{style:'display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;justify-content:center;margin-top:10px'},STAGES.map(x=>{const c=STAGE_COL[x.k]||STAGE_COL.sa;return h('span',{style:'padding:4px 10px;border-radius:999px;font-size:.75rem;font-weight:700;background:'+c[0]+';color:'+c[1],text:x.from})}))}
function treeNode(r,seen){seen[r.id]=1;const ks=kidsOf(r.id,true).filter(k=>!seen[k.id]);const lds=downOf(r.id).filter(x=>stageIdx(x.id)>=2).length;const st=STAGES[stageIdx(r.id)];
  return h('li',null,h('div',{class:'node',style:stCol(r.id)+(r.test?';opacity:.55;border-style:dashed':'')},h('b',{text:(r.test?'🧪 ':'')+(r.name||'Rep')}),h('small',{text:st.from}),lds?h('small',{text:lds+' leader'+(lds===1?'':'s')+' below'}):null),ks.length?h('ul',null,ks.map(k=>treeNode(k,seen))):null)}
function openTree(uid){const old=document.querySelector('.ftree');if(old)old.remove();if(S.isOwner&&uid===S.uid){let u=uid,g=0;while(g++<20){const r=S.reps[u]||(u===S.uid?S.me:null);const p=r&&parentOf({...r,id:u});if(!p||!S.reps[p]||p===u)break;u=p}uid=u}const me=repDoc(uid);const own=false;
  const root={id:uid,name:own?coName():(me.name||'Me')};const seen={[uid]:1};const ks=kidsOf(uid,true);
  const rootLi=h('li',null,h('div',{class:'node',style:own?'background:#111;color:#fff;border-color:#111':stCol(uid)},h('b',{text:root.name}),h('small',{text:own?'Owner':STAGES[stageIdx(uid)].from})),ks.length?h('ul',null,ks.map(k=>treeNode(k,seen))):null);
  const all=downOf(uid);const lds=all.filter(x=>stageIdx(x.id)>=2).length;
  const ov=h('div',{class:'ftree',role:'dialog','aria-label':'Team tree'},h('div',{class:'fbar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{style:'margin:0',text:(own?coName():root.name)+' team tree'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:all.length+' people · '+lds+' leaders · '+niceDate(todayStr(),{day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'})})),
    h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:8px'},h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>window.print()},'Save as PDF'),h('button',{onclick:()=>ov.remove()},'Close'))),
    h('div',{class:'tree'},h('ul',null,rootLi)),treeLegend(),h('div',{class:'hint',style:'text-align:center;margin-top:6px',text:'🧪 Test people are shown faded and aren’t counted.'}));
  document.body.append(ov)}
function treeCard(uid){const ld=r=>stageIdx(r.id)>=2;const d=kidsOf(uid).filter(ld),all=downOf(uid).filter(ld);const others=downOf(uid).length-all.length;const tests=kidsOf(uid,true).length-kidsOf(uid).length;if(!all.length&&!others&&!tests&&!leader())return null;const ind=all.filter(r=>!d.some(x=>x.id===r.id));
  const nm=r=>r.name+' ('+STAGES[stageIdx(r.id)].from+')';
  return h('div',{class:'panel',style:'padding:14px'},h('div',{class:'row',style:'display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px'},h('strong',{text:'Your leaders'}),h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center'},h('span',{class:'hint',text:d.length+' direct · '+all.length+' total'}),h('button',{class:'icon','aria-label':'Team tree',title:'Team tree',style:'font-size:1.6rem;line-height:1;padding:6px 10px;background:none;border:none',onclick:()=>openTree(uid)},'🌳'))),
    h('div',{class:'hint',style:'margin-top:6px'},h('b',{text:'Direct: '}),d.map(nm).join(', ')||'—'),ind.length?h('div',{class:'hint',style:'margin-top:4px'},h('b',{text:'Indirect: '}),ind.map(r=>nm(r)+' via '+repName(r.team)).join(', ')):null,
    others?h('div',{class:'hint',style:'margin-top:4px',text:'+ '+others+' team member'+(others===1?'':'s')+' below Sales Leader (not counted)'}):null,tests?h('div',{class:'hint',style:'margin-top:4px',text:'🧪 '+tests+' test '+(tests===1?'person':'people')+' not counted yet. Untick 🧪 Test on the Team tab when they’re real.'}):null)}
function b2bEnds(uids,amt){const w=weekMap(uids);return Object.keys(w).filter(k=>w[k]>=amt&&(w[addDays(k,-7)]||0)>=amt).sort().map(k=>addDays(k,6))}
function teamProgress(uid,p){if(!p.team)return null;const t=p.team;const ld=r=>stageIdx(r.id)>=2;
  const cnt=end=>{const ok=r=>ld(r)&&(!end||!(r.promo&&r.promo.slAt)||r.promo.slAt<=end);const d=kidsOf(uid).filter(ok).length,tot=downOf(uid).filter(ok).length;return[d,tot]};
  let [d,tot]=cnt(null),done=d>=t.direct&&tot>=t.total,win=null;
  if(p.sales&&p.sales.kind==='teamb2b'){const ends=b2bEnds([uid].concat(downOf(uid).map(r=>r.id)),p.sales.amt);done=false;
    for(const e of ends){const [a,b]=cnt(e);if(a>=t.direct&&b>=t.total){done=true;win=e;d=a;tot=b;break}}}
  const ind=tot-d;
  return{direct:d,indirect:ind,total:tot,needDirect:t.direct,needTotal:t.total,what:t.what,win,
    pctDirect:Math.min(100,d/t.direct*100),pctTotal:Math.min(100,tot/t.total*100),done}}
function extrasDone(uid,p){const m=promoOf(uid).m||{};const req=(p.extras||[]).filter(x=>!x.optional);const n=req.filter(x=>!!m[p.k+'_'+x.k]).length;return n>=(p.need||req.length)}
function stageDone(uid,p){if(p.appointed||p.top)return false;const s=salesProgress(uid,p),t=teamProgress(uid,p);return(!s||s.done)&&(!t||t.done)&&extrasDone(uid,p)}
async function setPromo(uid,patch){const r=S.reps[uid]||{};const cur=r.promo||{};if(patch.stage&&STAGES.findIndex(x=>x.k===patch.stage)>=2&&!cur.slAt)patch={...patch,slAt:todayStr()};const next={...cur,...patch,m:{...(cur.m||{}),...(patch.m||{})}};
  S.reps[uid]={...r,promo:next};if(uid===S.uid)S.me=S.reps[uid];render();await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({promo:patch}))}
function bar(pct,done){return h('div',{class:'prog'+(done?' hit':'')},h('i',{style:`width:${Math.max(0,Math.min(100,pct))}%`}))}
function counter(uid,field,label){const v=num(promoOf(uid)[field]);const can=leader()&&uid!==S.uid||S.isOwner;
  return h('span',{class:'stepper'},h('span',{class:'hint',text:label}),can?h('button',{class:'icon','aria-label':'Less '+label,onclick:()=>setPromo(uid,{[field]:Math.max(0,v-1)})},'−'):null,h('b',{text:String(v)}),can?h('button',{class:'icon','aria-label':'More '+label,onclick:()=>setPromo(uid,{[field]:v+1})},'+'):null)}
/* the current step, shown big */
function currentCard(uid){const i=stageIdx(uid);const p=STAGES[i];const m=promoOf(uid).m||{};
  const card=h('div',{class:'panel gcard focus'});
  if(p.top||p.appointed){card.append(h('strong',{class:'ftitle',text:p.from}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:p.appointed?'Managing Director is an appointment rather than a checklist.':'Top of the path.'}));return card}
  card.append(h('div',{class:'gtop'},h('div',null,h('div',{class:'lbl',text:'Working towards'}),h('strong',{class:'ftitle',text:p.to})),
    stageDone(uid,p)?h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓ Ready to promote'}):h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Currently '+p.from})));
  const sp=salesProgress(uid,p);
  if(sp)card.append(h('div',{class:'psec'},h('div',{class:'lbl',text:'Sales progress'}),h('div',{class:'prow'},h('span',{text:sp.label}),h('b',{class:sp.done?'achieved':null,text:sp.done?'✓ Achieved':Math.round(sp.pct)+'% this week'})),bar(sp.done?100:sp.pct,sp.done),
    h('div',{class:'hint small',text:sp.line+(sp.manual?' · ticked by leader':'')}),
    sp.alt&&leader()?h('label',{class:'hint small'},h('input',{type:'checkbox',id:`alt-${p.k}-${uid}`,checked:!!m[p.k+'_sales'],onchange:e=>setPromo(uid,{m:{[p.k+'_sales']:e.target.checked}})}),' '+sp.alt):null,
    p.sales.kind==='teamb2b'&&leader()?h('label',{class:'hint small'},h('input',{type:'checkbox',id:`org-${p.k}-${uid}`,checked:!!m[p.k+'_sales'],onchange:e=>setPromo(uid,{m:{[p.k+'_sales']:e.target.checked}})}),' Whole organisation hit it (includes sub-teams)'):null));
  const tp=teamProgress(uid,p);
  if(tp)card.append(h('div',{class:'psec'},h('div',{class:'lbl',text:'Team members progress'}),
    h('div',{class:'prow'},h('span',{text:`${tp.needDirect} direct ${tp.what}`}),h('b',{class:tp.direct>=tp.needDirect?'achieved':null,text:`${tp.direct} of ${tp.needDirect}`})),bar(tp.pctDirect,tp.direct>=tp.needDirect),
    h('div',{class:'prow'},h('span',{text:`${tp.needTotal} ${tp.what} in total (direct + indirect)`}),h('b',{class:tp.total>=tp.needTotal?'achieved':null,text:`${tp.total} of ${tp.needTotal}`})),bar(tp.pctTotal,tp.total>=tp.needTotal),
    h('div',{class:'hint small',text:'Counted automatically from who\u2019s on whose team (Sales Leaders and up).'+(p.sales&&p.sales.kind==='teamb2b'?' Leaders count if they were promoted by the end of the two qualifying weeks.':'')+(tp.win?' Qualified in the two weeks ending '+niceDate(tp.win,{day:'numeric',month:'short'})+'.':'')})));
  if((p.extras||[]).length)card.append(h('div',{class:'psec'},h('div',{class:'lbl',text:p.need?('Also needed: any '+p.need+' of '+p.extras.length+' \u00b7 '+p.extras.filter(x=>m[p.k+'_'+x.k]).length+' ticked'+(extrasDone(uid,p)?' \u2713':'')):'Also needed'}),...(p.extras.map(x=>{const id=`ex-${p.k}-${x.k}-${uid}`;
    return h('div',{class:'citem'+(m[p.k+'_'+x.k]?' on':'')},h('input',{type:'checkbox',id,checked:!!m[p.k+'_'+x.k],onchange:e=>setPromo(uid,{m:{[p.k+'_'+x.k]:e.target.checked}})}),h('label',{for:id,text:x.l+(x.optional?' (optional)':'')}))}))));
  if(leader()&&uid!==S.uid&&stageDone(uid,p))card.append(h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>setPromo(uid,{stage:STAGES[i+1].k})},'Promote to '+p.to));
  return card}
/* the whole path with progress for every step, starting from Sales Associate */
function pathTable(uid){const cur=uid?stageIdx(uid):-1;const tb=h('tbody');
  STAGES.filter(p=>!p.top).forEach((p,j)=>{const sp=uid&&!p.appointed?salesProgress(uid,p):null,tp=uid&&!p.appointed?teamProgress(uid,p):null;const past=uid&&j<cur;
    const cell=(prog,txt)=>past?h('td',{class:'pathbar'},bar(100,true),h('span',{class:'achieved small',text:'✓'})):prog?h('td',{class:'pathbar'},bar(prog.pct,prog.done),h('span',{class:prog.done?'achieved small':'hint small',text:txt})):h('td',{class:'pathbar hint',text:'—'});
    tb.append(h('tr',{class:j===cur?'sel':null},h('td',{class:'name',text:p.from+' → '+p.to}),
      h('td',{class:'wrapcell'},p.appointed?'Appointed':(p.sales?p.sales.label:''),p.need?h('div',{class:'hint small',text:'+ any '+p.need+' of: '+p.extras.map(x=>x.l).join(' \u00b7 ')}):(p.extras||[]).filter(x=>!x.optional).map(x=>h('div',{class:'hint small',text:'+ '+x.l}))),
      h('td',{class:'wrapcell',text:p.team?`${p.team.direct} direct · ${p.team.total} in total`:'—'}),
      uid?cell(sp?{pct:sp.done?100:sp.pct,done:sp.done}:null,sp?sp.short:''):null,
      uid?cell(tp?{pct:(Math.min(tp.direct,tp.needDirect)+Math.min(tp.total,tp.needTotal))/(tp.needDirect+tp.needTotal)*100,done:tp.done}:null,tp?`${tp.direct}/${tp.needDirect} direct · ${tp.total}/${tp.needTotal} total`:''):null))});
  return h('div',{class:'stack'},h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Promotion path'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:uid?'Every step from Sales Associate, with '+(uid===S.uid?'your':repName(uid)+'’s')+' progress.':'Every step from Sales Associate. Tap a name above to see their progress.'})),
    h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',{class:'train'},h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Step',style:'text-align:left'}),h('th',{text:'Sales',style:'text-align:left'}),h('th',{text:'Team members',style:'text-align:left'}),uid?h('th',{text:'Sales progress',style:'text-align:left'}):null,uid?h('th',{text:'Team progress',style:'text-align:left'}):null)),tb)))}
function canPromote(uid){return S.isOwner||(uid!==S.uid&&['tm','ad','dir','md'].includes(promoOf(S.uid).stage||(S.me&&S.me.promo&&S.me.promo.stage)))}
function renderPromo(v){
  if(needsTeam()){v.append(h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Loading the team’s history…'})));loadAllTeam();return}
  if(!leader()){
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'My promotion path'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'You’re a '+STAGES[stageIdx(S.uid)].from+'. Sales progress fills from your numbers and resets each Monday; your leader updates your team members.'}))));
    v.append(currentCard(S.uid));v.append(pathTable(S.uid));return}
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'Promotions'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Where each person is on the path. Tap a name to see their progress and update their team members.'})),h('button',{onclick:()=>loadAllTeam(true)},'Refresh')));
  const ros=repList(true);if(!ros.length){v.append(noReps());return}
  const tb=h('tbody');ros.forEach(r=>{const i=stageIdx(r.id);const p=STAGES[i];const sp=!p.top&&!p.appointed?salesProgress(r.id,p):null,tp=!p.top&&!p.appointed?teamProgress(r.id,p):null;
    tb.append(h('tr',{class:S.promoSel===r.id?'sel':null},h('td',{class:'name'},h('button',{class:'linkbtn',onclick:()=>{S.promoSel=r.id;render()}},r.name)),
      h('td',null,!canPromote(r.id)?h('span',{text:p.from}):h('select',{id:'role-'+r.id,'aria-label':r.name+' current role',onchange:e=>{const up=STAGES.findIndex(x=>x.k===e.target.value)>i;setPromo(r.id,up?{stage:e.target.value,promotedAt:new Date().toISOString(),promotedTo:e.target.value}:{stage:e.target.value});toast(r.name+' is now '+e.target.selectedOptions[0].textContent)}},STAGES.map(x=>h('option',{value:x.k,selected:x.k===p.k},x.from)))),
      h('td',{text:p.top?'—':p.to+(p.appointed?' (appointed)':'')}),
      h('td',{class:'pathbar'},sp?[bar(sp.done?100:sp.pct,sp.done),h('span',{class:sp.done?'achieved small':'hint small',text:sp.short+(sp.lastHit&&!sp.done?' · last week ✓':'')})]:'—'),
      h('td',{class:'pathbar'},tp?[bar((Math.min(tp.direct,tp.needDirect)+Math.min(tp.total,tp.needTotal))/(tp.needDirect+tp.needTotal)*100,tp.done),h('span',{class:tp.done?'achieved small':'hint small',text:`${tp.direct}/${tp.needDirect} direct · ${tp.total}/${tp.needTotal} total`})]:'—'),
      h('td',null,stageDone(r.id,p)?h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap'},h('span',{class:'achieved',text:'✓ Ready'}),!p.top&&STAGES[i+1]&&canPromote(r.id)?h('button',{class:'primary small',onclick:()=>{const nx=STAGES[i+1];setPromo(r.id,{stage:nx.k,promotedAt:new Date().toISOString(),promotedTo:nx.k});toast('🎉 '+r.name+' promoted to '+nx.from)}},'Promote to '+STAGES[i+1].from):null):'')))});
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,['Name','Current role','Working towards','Sales progress','Team members progress',''].map(x=>h('th',{text:x,style:'text-align:left'})))),tb)));
  const sel=S.promoSel&&S.reps[S.promoSel]?S.promoSel:null;
  if(sel){v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:repName(sel)+'’s progress'})));v.append(currentCard(sel))}
  v.append(pathTable(sel))}

/* ---------- PBs ---------- */
function renderPBs(v){
  if(!leader()){const pb=(S.me&&S.me.pb)||{day:{},week:{}};
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'My personal bests'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Updated every time you save a day. Beat one and you’ll see a New PB message.'}))));
    const mk=(rows,kind)=>{const tb=h('tbody');rows.forEach(([k,l])=>{const x=pb[kind][k];tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:l}),h('td',{text:x?fmtK(k,x.v):'—'}),
      h('td',{class:'hint',text:x?(kind==='day'?niceDate(x.date,{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'}):kind==='month'?monthName(x.month):'WC '+niceDate(x.week,{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'})):''})))});
      return h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:kind==='day'?'Best day':kind==='month'?'Best month':'Best week'}),h('th',{text:'Record'}),h('th',{text:'When'}))),tb))};
    pb.month=pb.month||{};v.append(mk(PB_DAY,'day'));v.append(mk(PB_WEEK.concat([['rph','RPH',1]]),'week'));v.append(mk(PB_WEEK.concat([['rph','RPH',1]]),'month'));
    const tp=S.teamPbDoc;
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Team records'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'The whole team’s best day, week and month. No names shown.'})));
    v.append(tp?teamRecordsTable(tp):h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Team records appear once your leader has opened the PBs tab.'})));return}
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'Team records'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Best day, week and month for the whole team combined. Reps see these without names.'})),
    h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px'},h('button',{id:'recalcPB',onclick:recalcAllPB},S.pbBusy?'Recalculating\u2026':'Recalculate PBs'),h('button',{onclick:()=>loadAllTeam(true)},'Refresh'))));
  if(needsTeam()){v.append(h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Loading the team’s history…'})));loadAllTeam();}
  else v.append(teamRecordsTable(teamPB()));
  const holders=recordHolders();const hrows=[['day','retail','Best day retail'],['day','stops','Best day stops'],['day','buyers','Best day buyers'],['day','medals','Best day medals'],['day','upsells','Best day upsells'],['day','rph','Best RPH'],['week','retail','Best week retail'],['week','stops','Best week stops'],['week','buyers','Best week buyers'],['week','medals','Best week medals']];
  const htb=h('tbody');hrows.forEach(([kind,k,l])=>{const x=holders[kind][k];htb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:l}),h('td',{text:x?fmtK(k,x.v):'—'}),h('td',{text:x?x.name:'—'}),
    h('td',{class:'hint',text:x?(kind==='day'?niceDate(x.date,{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'}):'WC '+niceDate(x.week,{day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric'})):''})))});
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Individual record holders'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Only you can see this.'})));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Record'}),h('th',{text:'Value'}),h('th',{text:'Held by'}),h('th',{text:'When'}))),htb)));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Personal bests'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Each rep’s best day and best week. They see only their own.'})));
  const ros=repList(true);if(!ros.length){v.append(noReps());return}
  const pick=[['day','retail','Best day retail'],['day','stops','Best day stops'],['day','buyers','Best day buyers'],['day','medals','Best day medals'],['day','upsells','Best day upsells'],['day','rph','Best RPH'],['week','retail','Best week retail'],['week','stops','Best week stops'],['week','buyers','Best week buyers'],['week','medals','Best week medals'],['week','rph','Best week RPH'],['month','retail','Best month retail'],['month','stops','Best month stops'],['month','rph','Best month RPH']];
  const tb=h('tbody');ros.forEach(r=>{const pb=r.pb||{day:{},week:{}};
    tb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{class:'name',text:r.name}),pick.map(([kind,k])=>{const x=(pb[kind]||{})[k];const recent=x&&(kind==='day'?x.date>=addDays(todayStr(),-7):x.week>=addDays(weekStart(todayStr()),-7));
      return h('td',null,x?fmtK(k,x.v):'—',recent?h('span',{class:'pbtag',text:'New'}):null,x?h('div',{class:'hint',text:kind==='day'?niceDate(x.date):kind==='month'?monthName(x.month):'WC '+niceDate(x.week,{day:'numeric',month:'short'})}):null)})))});
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,h('th',{text:'Name'}),pick.map(p=>h('th',{text:p[2]})))),tb)));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'hint',text:'"New" marks a record set in the last 7 days.'}))}
