/* ---------- KAIZEN TEAMS ---------- */
function teamList(){return Object.entries(S.teams).map(([id,t])=>({id,...t})).sort((a,b)=>String(a.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.name||'')))}
function coName(){return('Orexis Innovations').trim()||'Orexis Innovations'}
function teamName(id){if(!id)return coName();const r=S.reps[id];const fn=r&&r.name?String(r.name).trim().split(/\s+/)[0]:'';return fn?fn+'’s team':coName()}
function teamLogo(id,cls){const t=S.teams[id];const src=id?(t&&t.logo):(S.logo&&S.logo.data);return src&&String(src).startsWith('data:image/')?h('img',{class:cls||'tlogo',src,alt:''}):h('span',{class:(cls||'tlogo')+' blank',text:(teamName(id)[0]||'?').toUpperCase(),'aria-hidden':'true'})}
function canCreateTeam(){if(S.isOwner)return true;if(leader())return true;const i=stageIdx(S.uid);return i>=2}
function shrinkImage(file,cb){if(!/^image\//.test(file.type)){toast('Choose an image file');return}
  const rd=new FileReader();rd.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{const max=window.KAIZEN_GOOGLE?160:256;const sc=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.width,img.height));
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.width*sc));c.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.height*sc));c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0,c.width,c.height);
      let data=c.toDataURL('image/png');const lim=window.KAIZEN_GOOGLE?40000:180000;if(data.length>lim)data=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.85);if(data.length>lim)data=c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.6);cb(data)};
    img.onerror=()=>toast('That image couldn’t be read. Try a PNG or JPG.');img.src=rd.result};rd.readAsDataURL(file)}
function uploadLogo(file){shrinkImage(file,async data=>{const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc('settings/logo').set({data,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()}),'Logo saved');if(ok){S.logo={data};render()}})}
function logoPicker(id,current,onData,onRemove,label){const file=h('input',{type:'file',id,accept:'image/*',hidden:true,onchange:e=>{const f=e.target.files&&e.target.files[0];if(f)shrinkImage(f,onData);e.target.value=''}});
  return h('div',{class:'logorow'},current?h('img',{class:'logoprev',src:current,alt:label}):h('div',{class:'logoprev empty',text:'No logo'}),
    h('div',{style:'display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap'},h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>file.click()},current?'Change logo':'Upload logo'),current&&onRemove?h('button',{onclick:onRemove},'Remove'):null),file)}
async function saveTeam(id,patch){const cur=S.teams[id];S.teams[id]={...(cur||{}),...patch};render();
  return write(()=>cur?S.db.doc('teams/'+id).update(patch):S.db.doc('teams/'+id).set({leader:id,createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),...patch}))}
async function setRepTeam(uid,team){const r=S.reps[uid]||{};S.reps[uid]={...r,team};if(uid===S.uid)S.me=S.reps[uid];render();
  const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+uid).update({team}),'Team updated');if(ok&&S.board[uid])write(()=>S.db.doc('board/'+uid).update({team}))}
function createTeamPanel(){const myTeam=S.teams[S.uid];
  if(myTeam){return h('div',{class:'panel'},h('div',{class:'field'},h('label',{text:'My team'}),
    h('input',{type:'text',id:'myTeamName',value:myTeam.name||'','aria-label':'Team name',onchange:e=>{const n=e.target.value.trim();if(n)saveTeam(S.uid,{name:n}).then(ok=>ok&&toast('Team name saved'))}}),
    logoPicker('myTeamLogo',myTeam.logo,data=>saveTeam(S.uid,{logo:data}).then(ok=>ok&&toast('Logo saved')),()=>saveTeam(S.uid,{logo:null}),'Team logo'),
    !leader()?h('span',{class:'hint',text:'You’ll see your members’ numbers, goals and reflections as soon as they’re on your team. Close and reopen the app if they don’t show yet.'}):null))}
  if(!canCreateTeam())return null;
  return h('div',{class:'panel'},h('div',{class:'field'},h('label',{for:'newTeamName',text:'Create your team'}),
    h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Name your team within Orexis. You can add a logo once it’s created, and people pick your team when they join.'}),
    h('div',{class:'rule'},h('input',{type:'text',id:'newTeamName',placeholder:'Team name','aria-label':'New team name'}),
      h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:async()=>{const n=(document.getElementById('newTeamName').value||'').trim();if(!n){toast('Name your team first');return}
        const ok=await saveTeam(S.uid,{name:n});if(ok){toast('Team created');if(S.reps[S.uid]&&S.reps[S.uid].team!==S.uid)setRepTeam(S.uid,S.uid)}}},'Create team'))))}
/* leader view: owner sees everything; team leaders manage their own team */
function renderTeam(v){const OWN=S.isOwner&&(S.admin||!isAdminUser());
  if(OWN){v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'Orexis'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'The teams within Orexis and who is on each.'}))));
  }else v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'My team'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Your team’s name, logo and members.'}))));
  const cp=createTeamPanel();if(cp)v.append(cp);{const tc=treeCard(S.uid);if(tc)v.append(tc)}
  if(OWN){const ts=teamList();
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Teams within Orexis'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:ts.length+' team'+(ts.length===1?'':'s')})));
    const free=repListAll(false,true).filter(r=>r.id!==S.uid&&!S.teams[r.id]);
    if(free.length){const who=h('select',{id:'newTeamLeader','aria-label':'Team leader'},h('option',{value:''},'Choose who leads it'),free.map(r=>h('option',{value:r.id},(r.name||'Rep')+(r.test?' (test)':''))));
      const nm=h('input',{type:'text',id:'newTeamFor',placeholder:'Team name','aria-label':'Team name'});
      v.append(h('div',{class:'panel'},h('div',{class:'field'},h('label',{for:'newTeamLeader',text:'Add a team for someone'}),
        h('span',{class:'hint',text:'They become the leader. Then use the “Direct to” box under Everyone to move people onto their team.'}),
        h('div',{class:'rule'},who,nm,h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:async()=>{const id=who.value,n=(nm.value||'').trim();if(!id){toast('Choose who leads it');return}if(!n){toast('Name the team first');return}
          const ok=await saveTeam(id,{name:n,leader:id});if(ok){toast('Team created for '+repName(id));setRepTeam(id,id)}}},'Create team')))))}
    const tl=h('div',{class:'panel teamlist'});
    if(!ts.length)tl.append(h('div',{class:'empty'},h('span',{text:'No other teams yet. Everyone is on '+coName()+' until Sales Leaders and up create their own teams.'})));
    ts.forEach(t=>{const n=repListAll(false).filter(r=>r.team===t.id).length;
      tl.append(h('div',{class:'teamrow'},teamLogo(t.id),h('input',{type:'text',id:'tname-'+t.id,value:t.name||'','aria-label':'Team name',onchange:e=>{const nm=e.target.value.trim();if(nm)saveTeam(t.id,{name:nm})}}),
        h('span',{class:'hint',text:`Led by ${repName(t.leader||t.id)} · ${kidsOf(t.leader||t.id).filter(r=>stageIdx(r.id)>=2).length} direct leaders · ${downOf(t.leader||t.id).filter(r=>stageIdx(r.id)>=2).length} total · ${downOf(t.leader||t.id).length} people`}),
        h('button',{onclick:()=>{if(S.confirmDel!=='t'+t.id){S.confirmDel='t'+t.id;render();return}S.confirmDel=null;write(()=>S.db.doc('teams/'+t.id).delete(),'Team removed').then(ok=>{if(ok){delete S.teams[t.id];render()}})}},S.confirmDel==='t'+t.id?'Tap again to remove':'Remove')))});
    v.append(tl)}
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel notice'},h('strong',{text:'Adding people: '}),
    window.KAIZEN_GOOGLE?(OWN?'send everyone the Orexis KPIs link. They tap Create my login, enter their name, choose a PIN and pick their team. Then set team leaders under Logins below so they can see their team.':'Send your team the Orexis KPIs link. When they create their login they pick your team.'):
    (OWN?'invite each person from the Share menu. Give reps Contributor access, so they only see their own detail. Give team leaders Editor access, so they can see their team’s detail. When people open the link they enter their name and pick their team.':'Share the tracker link with your team. When they join they pick your team from the list.')));
  const ros=OWN?repListAll(false,true):repListAll(false,true).filter(r=>r.team===S.uid||r.id===S.uid);
  const list=h('div',{class:'panel teamlist'});
  if(!S.reps[S.uid])list.append(h('div',{class:'teamrow'},h('span',{style:'flex:1',text:'Log your own numbers too?'}),h('button',{onclick:joinSelf},'Add me as a rep')));
  if(!ros.length)list.append(h('div',{class:'empty'},h('span',{text:'No one has joined yet.'})));
  ros.forEach((m,i)=>list.append(h('div',{class:'teamrow'+(m.active===false?' off':'')},
    h('input',{type:'text',id:'name-'+m.id,value:m.name||'','aria-label':'Name',onchange:e=>{const n=e.target.value.trim();if(n){write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+m.id).update({name:n}),'Name updated');if(S.board[m.id])write(()=>S.db.doc('board/'+m.id).update({name:n}))}}}),
    OWN?h('label',{class:'hint'},'Direct to ',(()=>{const top=m.team===m.id||(m.id==='u_owner'&&!m.team);const o=[[m.id,'No one (top)']].concat(m.id!=='u_owner'?[['',repName('u_owner')]]:[],repListAll(false,true).filter(r=>r.id!==m.id&&r.id!=='u_owner').map(r=>[r.id,r.name||'Rep']));return h('select',{id:'team-'+m.id,'aria-label':m.name+' is direct to',onchange:e=>setRepTeam(m.id,e.target.value)},o.map(([v,l])=>h('option',{value:v,selected:v===m.id?top:(!top&&(m.team||'')===v)},l)))})()):h('span',{class:'hint',text:m.team&&m.team!==m.id?'Direct to '+repName(m.team):m.team===m.id?'Top':'Direct to '+repName('u_owner')}),
    h('label',null,h('input',{type:'checkbox',id:'active-'+m.id,checked:m.active!==false,onchange:e=>write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+m.id).update({active:e.target.checked}),e.target.checked?'Marked active':'Marked inactive')}),'Active'),
    OWN?h('label',{title:'Test person: their numbers don’t count towards team totals, leaderboard, team PBs or the weekly wrap'},h('input',{type:'checkbox',id:'test-'+m.id,checked:!!m.test,onchange:e=>{const t=e.target.checked;S.reps[m.id]={...S.reps[m.id],test:t};write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+m.id).update({test:t}),t?'Marked as test person — not counted':'Now counted');if(t&&S.board[m.id]){delete S.board[m.id];write(()=>S.db.doc('board/'+m.id).delete())}else if(!t)setTimeout(()=>pushBoard(m.id,S.week),500);render()}}),'🧪 Test'):null,
    OWN?h('button',{class:'icon','aria-label':'Move up',disabled:i===0,onclick:()=>move(i,-1)},'↑'):null,
    OWN?h('button',{class:'icon','aria-label':'Move down',disabled:i===ros.length-1,onclick:()=>move(i,1)},'↓'):null)));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:OWN?'Everyone':'Members'})));v.append(list);
  if(OWN&&window.kaizenAdmin)v.append(loginsPanel());
  v.append(h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Marking someone inactive hides them from the daily log and goals. Their past numbers stay in the totals. 🧪 Test people (trials) are left out of all team numbers, the leaderboard, team PBs and the weekly wrap until you untick it.'}))}

function adminStandards(v){const cur=S.std||{};const F=[['Bell & gong',[['bell','Bell day ($ retail)'],['gong','Gong day ($ retail)']]],

  v.append(p,h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Used by the zones in the app and by the weekly scorecards (bell/gong days, standards, team KPIs).'}))}

function adminBrand(v){
    v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'Branding & settings'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Company name, logo and campaigns.'}))));

      h('input',{id:'teamName',type:'text',value:(S.settings&&S.settings.teamName)||'Orexis Innovations',placeholder:'e.g. Orexis Innovations',onchange:e=>write(()=>S.db.doc('settings/main').set({...S.settings,teamName:e.target.value.trim()}),'Name saved')})),
      h('div',{class:'field'},h('label',{text:'Company logo'}),logoPicker('logoFile',S.logo&&S.logo.data,data=>write(()=>S.db.doc('settings/logo').set({data,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()}),'Logo saved').then(ok=>{if(ok){S.logo={data};render()}}),()=>write(()=>S.db.doc('settings/logo').delete(),'Logo removed'),'Company logo'))));
  if(S.isOwner){const cl=campaigns();const saveC=(list,def)=>{S.campaignsCfg={list,def:def||defCampaign()};render();write(()=>S.db.doc('settings/campaigns').set(S.campaignsCfg),'Campaigns saved')};
    const cp=h('div',{class:'panel'});cp.append(h('div',{class:'field'},h('label',{text:'Campaigns'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Every logged day is tagged with a campaign. The default is pre-selected when people log. Once there\u2019s more than one, a campaign filter appears at the top for everyone.'})));
    const rl=h('div',{class:'rules'});cl.forEach((c,i)=>rl.append(h('div',{class:'rule'},
      h('input',{type:'text',id:'cname-'+c.id,value:c.name,'aria-label':'Campaign name',onchange:e=>{const n=e.target.value.trim();if(n)saveC(cl.map((x,j)=>j===i?{...x,name:n}:x))}}),
      h('label',{class:'hint'},h('input',{type:'radio',name:'defcamp',id:'cdef-'+c.id,checked:defCampaign()===c.id,onchange:()=>saveC(cl,c.id)}),' Default'),
      h('label',{class:'hint'},h('input',{type:'checkbox',id:'cact-'+c.id,checked:c.active!==false,onchange:e=>saveC(cl.map((x,j)=>j===i?{...x,active:e.target.checked}:x))}),' Active'))));
    cp.append(rl);const ni=h('input',{type:'text',id:'newCamp',placeholder:'New campaign name','aria-label':'New campaign name'});
    cp.append(h('div',{class:'formfoot'},h('div',{class:'rule'},ni,h('button',{class:'primary',onclick:()=>{const n=(ni.value||'').trim();if(!n){toast('Name the campaign first');return}
      const id=slug(n)||('c'+Date.now().toString(36));if(cl.some(x=>x.id===id)){toast('That campaign already exists');return}saveC(cl.concat([{id,name:n,active:true}]))}},'Add campaign'))));
    v.append(cp)}
}
function loginsPanel(){const box=h('div',{class:'stack'});
  box.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Logins'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Make someone a team leader so they can see their team, reset a forgotten PIN, or remove access.'})));
  if(!S.logins){if(!S.loginsLoading){S.loginsLoading=true;window.kaizenAdmin.list().then(l=>{S.logins=l;S.loginsLoading=false;render()}).catch(()=>{S.loginsLoading=false})}
    box.append(h('div',{class:'panel empty'},h('span',{text:'Loading logins\u2026'})));return box}
  const act=async(a,uid,val,msg)=>{try{await window.kaizenAdmin.act(a,uid,val);toast(msg);S.logins=null;render()}catch(e){toast(String(e&&e.message||e).replace(/^Error: /,''))}};
  const list=h('div',{class:'panel teamlist'});
  S.logins.filter(l=>!/\(removed /.test(l.name)).forEach(l=>{const pinIn=h('input',{type:'password',inputmode:'numeric',class:'num',id:'pin-'+l.uid,placeholder:'New PIN','aria-label':'New PIN for '+l.name});
    list.append(h('div',{class:'teamrow'},h('strong',{style:'flex:1;min-width:140px',text:l.name}),
      l.role==='owner'?h('span',{class:'hint',text:l.uid==='u_owner'?'Admin':'Owner'}):h('select',{id:'role-'+l.uid,'aria-label':l.name+' access',onchange:e=>act('role',l.uid,e.target.value,'Access updated')},[['rep','Rep'],['tl','Team leader']].map(([k,t])=>h('option',{value:k,selected:l.role===k},t))),
      l.role==='owner'?null:pinIn,l.role==='owner'?null:h('button',{onclick:()=>act('pin',l.uid,pinIn.value,'PIN reset for '+l.name)},'Reset PIN'),
      l.role==='owner'?null:h('button',{onclick:()=>{if(S.confirmDel!=='l'+l.uid){S.confirmDel='l'+l.uid;render();return}S.confirmDel=null;act('remove',l.uid,null,'Access removed')}},S.confirmDel==='l'+l.uid?'Tap again to remove':'Remove')))});
  box.append(list);
  box.append(h('div',{class:'hint',text:'Admin and owner PINs are changed from the spreadsheet: Orexis KPIs menu → Set or reset owner PIN.'}));return box}
function renderMyTeam(v){const t=S.me&&S.me.team;{const tc=treeCard(S.uid);if(tc)v.append(tc)}

    h('div',{class:'hint',text:'To change teams, ask Ameliah.'})));

/* ---------- SHARED LEADERBOARD (everyone) ---------- */
const BOARD_FIELDS=['retail','stops','closes','buyers','upsells','upsellRetail','medals','hours'];
async function pushBoard(uid,wk){if(!S.db||!uid)return;if((S.reps[uid]||{}).test||(uid===S.uid&&S.me&&S.me.test))return;wk=wk||S.week;const list=sourceAll(uid,'period').filter(e=>e.week===wk);
  const t=blank();const byC={};list.forEach(e=>{sumInto(t,e);sumInto(byC[campOf(e)]=byC[campOf(e)]||blank(),e)});
  const row={};BOARD_FIELDS.forEach(k=>row[k]=Math.round(num(t[k])*100)/100);
  const wcRow={};Object.keys(byC).forEach(c=>{const x={};BOARD_FIELDS.forEach(k=>x[k]=Math.round(num(byC[c][k])*100)/100);wcRow[c]=x});
  const r=S.reps[uid]||{};const cur=S.board[uid];const name=r.name||(cur&&cur.name)||'Team member';const team=r.team||(cur&&cur.team)||'';
  if(cur&&JSON.stringify((cur.weeks||{})[wk])===JSON.stringify(row)&&JSON.stringify((cur.wc||{})[wk]||{})===JSON.stringify(wcRow)&&cur.name===name&&(cur.team||'')===team)return;
  const body={name,team,weeks:{[wk]:row},wc:{[wk]:wcRow}};
  S.board[uid]={...(cur||{}),name,team,weeks:{...((cur&&cur.weeks)||{}),[wk]:row},wc:{...((cur&&cur.wc)||{}),[wk]:wcRow}};
  await write(()=>cur?S.db.doc('board/'+uid).update(body):S.db.doc('board/'+uid).set(body))}
function boardRows(){const m=S.boardPeriod==='month';const mk=monthKey(S.date);
  return Object.entries(S.board).map(([id,b])=>{const t=blank();const w=b.weeks||{};
    const cf=S.campFilter&&S.campFilter!=='all'?S.campFilter:null;
    Object.keys(w).filter(k=>m?monthKey(k)===mk:k===S.week).forEach(k=>{const src=cf?(((b.wc||{})[k]||{})[cf]||(campaigns().length<2||cf===defCampaign()&&!(b.wc||{})[k]?w[k]:null)):w[k];if(src)BOARD_FIELDS.forEach(f=>t[f]+=num(src[f]))});
    return{id,name:b.name||'Team member',team:b.team||'',t}}).filter(x=>(x.t.retail>0||x.t.stops>0)&&!(S.reps[x.id]||{}).test)}
function renderBoard(v){const rows=boardRows();const m=S.boardPeriod==='month';
  const label=m?monthName(monthKey(S.date))+' (weeks starting this month)':'Week of '+niceDate(S.week,{day:'numeric',month:'long'});
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('div',null,h('h2',{text:'Leaderboard'}),h('div',{class:'hint',text:label+'. Everyone in '+(('Orexis Innovations').trim()||'Orexis')+' sees this.'})),
    h('div',{class:'seg'},[['week','Week'],['month','Month']].map(([k,l])=>h('button',{class:(S.boardPeriod||'week')===k?'primary':null,onclick:()=>{S.boardPeriod=k;render()}},l)),
      h('button',{onclick:()=>setDate(m?addDays(monthKey(S.date)+'-01',-1):addDays(S.week,-7))},'‹'),h('button',{onclick:()=>setDate(m?addDays(monthKey(S.date)+'-01',32):addDays(S.week,7))},'›'))));
  // teams
  const byTeam={};rows.forEach(r=>{const x=byTeam[r.team]=byTeam[r.team]||{team:r.team,t:blank(),n:0};BOARD_FIELDS.forEach(f=>x.t[f]+=r.t[f]);x.n++});
  const teams=Object.values(byTeam).sort((a,b)=>b.t.retail-a.t.retail);
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Teams'}),h('span',{class:'hint',text:'Ranked by retail. Everything counts towards Orexis.'})));
  const ttb=h('tbody');teams.forEach((x,i)=>{const t=x.t;const rph=t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:null;
    ttb.append(h('tr',{class:S.me&&S.me.team===x.team?'sel':null},h('td',{class:'name'},h('span',{class:'tcell'},h('b',{class:'rank',text:String(i+1)}),teamLogo(x.team),teamName(x.team))),
      h('td',{text:money(t.retail)}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.stops))}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.buyers))}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.upsells))}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.medals))}),
      h('td',{text:t.buyers>0?money(t.retail/t.buyers):'—'}),h('td',{text:rph!=null?money(rph):'—'}),h('td',null,zoneEl(rph)),h('td',{text:String(x.n)})))});
  if(teams.length){const k=blank();rows.forEach(r=>BOARD_FIELDS.forEach(f=>k[f]+=r.t[f]));const rph=k.hours>0?k.retail/k.hours:null;
    ttb.append(h('tr',{class:'total'},h('td',{text:'All of '+coName()}),h('td',{text:money(k.retail)}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(k.stops))}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(k.buyers))}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(k.upsells))}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(k.medals))}),
      h('td',{text:k.buyers>0?money(k.retail/k.buyers):'—'}),h('td',{text:rph!=null?money(rph):'—'}),h('td',null,zoneEl(rph)),h('td',{text:String(rows.length)})))}
  else ttb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{colspan:10,class:'hint',text:'Nothing logged for this period yet.'})));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,['Team','Retail','Stops','Buyers','Upsells','Medals','Sale avg','RPH','RPH zone','People'].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})))),ttb)));
  // individuals
  const filt=S.boardTeam||'all';const myT=(S.me&&S.me.team)||(S.teams[S.uid]?S.uid:'');
  const ind=rows.filter(r=>filt==='all'||r.team===filt).sort((a,b)=>b.t.retail-a.t.retail);
  v.append(h('div',{class:'bar'},h('h3',{text:'Individuals'}),h('select',{id:'boardTeam','aria-label':'Show team',onchange:e=>{S.boardTeam=e.target.value;render()}},
    h('option',{value:'all'},'All of Orexis'),myT?h('option',{value:myT,selected:filt===myT},'My team'):null,
    teamList().filter(t=>t.id!==myT).map(t=>h('option',{value:t.id,selected:filt===t.id},teamName(t.id))))));
  const itb=h('tbody');ind.forEach((r,i)=>{const t=r.t;const rph=t.hours>0?t.retail/t.hours:null;
    itb.append(h('tr',{class:r.id===S.uid?'sel':null},h('td',{class:'name'},h('span',{class:'tcell'},h('b',{class:'rank',text:String(i+1)}),r.name+(r.id===S.uid?' (you)':''))),h('td',null,h('span',{class:'tcell'},teamLogo(r.team,'tlogo sm'),teamName(r.team))),
      h('td',{text:money(t.retail)}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.stops))}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.buyers))}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.upsells))}),h('td',{text:String(Math.round(t.medals))}),
      h('td',{text:t.buyers>0?money(t.retail/t.buyers):'—'}),h('td',null,zoneEl(rph))))});
  if(!ind.length)itb.append(h('tr',null,h('td',{colspan:9,class:'hint',text:'Nothing logged for this period yet.'})));
  v.append(h('div',{class:'panel scroll'},h('table',null,h('thead',null,h('tr',null,['Name','Team','Retail','Stops','Buyers','Upsells','Medals','Sale avg','RPH zone'].map(x=>h('th',{text:x})))),itb)));
  v.append(legend())}

async function joinSelf(){if(!S.uid){toast('Your account has no id here, so you can’t log as a rep');return}
  await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+S.uid).set({name:'Team leader',active:true,order:0,joinedAt:new Date().toISOString()}),'Added. Rename yourself in the list below.')}
async function move(i,dir){const ros=repList(false);const a=ros[i],b=ros[i+dir];if(!a||!b)return;
  const ao=a.order||0,bo=b.order||0;const na=bo,nb=ao===bo?ao-dir:ao;
  await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+a.id).update({order:na}));await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+b.id).update({order:nb}))}

async function loadLegacy(){try{
  const [r,e]=await Promise.all([S.db.collection('roster').get(),S.db.collection('entries').limit(1000).get()]);
  S.legacy={roster:r.docs.map(d=>({id:d.id,...d.data()})),entries:e.docs.map(d=>({id:d.id,...d.data()}))};render()}catch(_){S.legacy=null}}
function legacyPanel(){const L=S.legacy;const people=L.roster.filter(m=>L.entries.some(e=>e.member===m.id));
  const sel=people.length>1?h('select',{id:'legacyWho','aria-label':'Whose numbers are yours'},people.map(m=>h('option',{value:m.id},m.name))):null;
  const n=L.entries.filter(e=>!people.length||e.member===(people[0]||{}).id).length;
  return h('div',{class:'notice bar'},h('span',null,people.length>1?'Numbers from the earlier version are still here. Pick which person is you to move their days into your private log: ':`You logged ${n} day${n===1?'':'s'} in the earlier version${people[0]?' as '+people[0].name:''}. Move them into your private log so they count in your weeks, months and PBs.`),
    h('span',{style:'display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center'},sel,h('button',{class:'primary',id:'migrateBtn',onclick:migrate},'Move my earlier numbers')))}
async function migrate(){if(!S.uid){toast('Your account has no id here');return}
  const L=S.legacy;const people=L.roster.filter(m=>L.entries.some(e=>e.member===m.id));
  const who=(document.getElementById('legacyWho')||{}).value||(people[0]||{}).id||null;
  const mine=L.entries.filter(e=>!who||e.member===who);const person=L.roster.find(m=>m.id===who);
  const btn=document.getElementById('migrateBtn');if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Moving…'}
  if(!S.reps[S.uid]){const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+S.uid).set({name:(person&&person.name)||'Team leader',active:true,order:0,joinedAt:new Date().toISOString()}));if(!ok)return}
  for(const e of mine){const body={date:e.date,week:weekStart(e.date),month:monthKey(e.date),updatedAt:e.updatedAt||new Date().toISOString()};
    LOGFIELDS.forEach(f=>body[f.k]=num(e[f.k]));
    const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc(`reps/${S.uid}/entries/${e.date}`).set(body));if(!ok)return;
    await write(()=>S.db.doc('entries/'+e.id).delete())}
  if(person&&!L.entries.some(e=>e.member===person.id&&!mine.includes(e)))await write(()=>S.db.doc('roster/'+person.id).delete());
  S.legacy={roster:L.roster.filter(m=>m!==person),entries:L.entries.filter(e=>!mine.includes(e))};
  toast(`Moved ${mine.length} day${mine.length===1?'':'s'} into your log`);render();setTimeout(async()=>{refreshPB(S.uid);for(const wk of [...new Set(mine.map(e=>weekStart(e.date)))])await pushBoard(S.uid,wk)},1500)}

/* ---------- team summary (leader writes, reps read) ---------- */
function scheduleSummary(){return;clearTimeout(S.summaryTimer);S.summaryTimer=setTimeout(writeSummary,2500)}
async function writeSummary(){const wk=S.week;const ids=repList(false).filter(r=>!r.test).map(r=>r.id);const t=totalsFor(ids,'week',wk);const g=goalTotal(ids,wk);
  const totals={};METRICS.forEach(m=>totals[m.k]=t[m.k]);totals.hours=t.hours;
  const sig=JSON.stringify({totals,g});if(S.summarySent[wk]===sig)return;
  if(!g&&!METRICS.some(m=>totals[m.k]))return;
  const ok=await write(()=>S.db.doc('summary/'+wk).set({week:wk,totals,goals:g,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()}));if(ok)S.summarySent[wk]=sig}

function go(t){S.tab=t;S.admin=false;try{history.replaceState(null,'','#'+t)}catch(_){}render();if(['totals','pbs','coaching','bonus','promo'].includes(t))loadAllTeam()}
function setDate(d){if(!d)return;S.date=d;const w=weekStart(d);if(w!==S.week){S.week=w;onWeekChange()}render()}
function onWeekChange(){if(!S.db)return;
  if(leadRole())syncWeekSubs(true);
  if(!leader()){if(S.unsubSummary)S.unsubSummary();S.summary=null;const wk=S.week;
    S.unsubSummary=S.db.doc('summary/'+wk).onSnapshot(s=>{if(wk!==S.week)return;S.summary=s.exists?s.data():null;render()},()=>{})}}
function syncWeekSubs(reset){if(!leadRole()||!S.db)return;
  if(reset||S.weekSubsFor!==S.week){Object.values(S.weekSubs).forEach(u=>{try{u()}catch(_){}});S.weekSubs={};S.weekEntries={};S.weekSubsFor=S.week}
  const wk=S.week;loadConfWeek();
  for(const id of Object.keys(S.reps)){if(S.weekSubs[id]||id===S.uid)continue;
    S.weekSubs[id]=S.db.collection('reps/'+id+'/entries').where('week','==',wk).onSnapshot(snap=>{if(wk!==S.week)return;const o={};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data());S.weekEntries[id]=o;render();scheduleSummary()},onErr)}}
async function loadConfWeek(){const wk=S.week;const need=Object.keys(S.reps).filter(id=>id!==S.uid&&!S.confWeeks[id+'|'+wk]);if(!need.length)return;
  need.forEach(id=>S.confWeeks[id+'|'+wk]=true);
  await Promise.all(need.map(async id=>{try{const snap=await S.db.collection('conf/'+id+'/days').where('week','==',wk).get();const o=S.conf[id]=S.conf[id]||{};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data())}catch(_){}}));
  render();scheduleSummary()}
function onErr(e){if(e&&e.code==='revoked')status('This tracker is no longer available to you.','warn');
  else if(e&&e.code==='resource_exhausted')status('The team is too large to show live. Some people’s numbers may be missing until you reload.','warn');
  else status('Live updates stopped. Reload the page to reconnect.','warn')}

function wkTab(){return ['week','goals','bonus','board'].includes(S.tab)||(S.tab==='reflect'&&S.reflView==='weekly')}
document.getElementById('prevDay').onclick=()=>setDate(addDays(wkTab()?(S.tab==='reflect'?rwk():S.week):S.date,wkTab()?-7:-1));
document.getElementById('nextDay').onclick=()=>setDate(addDays(wkTab()?(S.tab==='reflect'?rwk():S.week):S.date,wkTab()?7:1));
document.getElementById('todayBtn').onclick=()=>setDate(todayStr());
document.getElementById('datePick').onchange=e=>setDate(e.target.value);
document.getElementById('campFilter').onchange=e=>{S.campFilter=e.target.value;render()};
document.getElementById('viewMode').onchange=e=>{if(e.target.value==='admin'){S.admin=true;S.msheet=false;render();scrollTo(0,0);return}S.admin=false;S.viewMe=e.target.value==='me';try{localStorage.setItem('kz_view',S.viewMe?'me':'team')}catch(x){}S.growthSel=null;S.coachSel=null;S.promoSel=null;if(S.viewMe&&!REP_TABS.some(t=>t[0]===S.tab))S.tab='log';onWeekChange();render()};
document.getElementById('teamFilter').onchange=e=>{S.teamFilter=e.target.value;S.growthSel=null;S.coachSel=null;S.promoSel=null;render()};
let hash=(location.hash||'').slice(1);if(hash==='month')hash='totals';if(LEADER_TABS.some(t=>t[0]===hash))S.tab=hash;
S.week=weekStart(S.date);render();

(async()=>{
  const cl=window.claude;
  if(!cl||!cl.use){status('Open this tracker from its claude.ai link to log and see numbers.','warn');S.ready=true;render();return}
  const [db,user]=await Promise.all([cl.use('db'),cl.use('user')]);
  if(!db||!user){status('Sign in to claude.ai to use the tracker.','warn');S.ready=true;render();return}
  S.db=db;S.uid=await user.id();S.isOwner=await user.isOwner();S.isAdmin=!S.isOwner&&await user.canEdit();if(S.isAdmin)S.teamFilter='all';
  if(!S.uid&&!S.isOwner){status('Your account can’t log numbers here. Ask your team leader to invite you by email from the Share menu.','warn');S.ready=true;render();return}
  S.viewMe=true;
  if(!leader()&&!REP_TABS.some(t=>t[0]===S.tab))S.tab='log';
  const parts={settings:false,reps:false,mine:!S.uid};
  const check=()=>{if(!S.ready&&parts.settings&&parts.reps&&parts.mine)S.ready=true;render()};
  db.doc('settings/standards').onSnapshot(s=>{S.std=s.exists?s.data():{};render()},()=>{});db.doc('settings/scorecards').onSnapshot(s=>{S.scSet=s.exists?s.data():{};if(S.admin)render()},()=>{});
  db.doc('settings/main').onSnapshot(s=>{S.settings=s.exists?s.data():{};parts.settings=true;check()},onErr);
  db.collection('teams').onSnapshot(snap=>{const o={};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data());S.teams=o;if(S.ready)render()},()=>{});
  db.collection('board').onSnapshot(snap=>{const o={};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data());S.board=o;if(S.ready&&['board','week'].includes(S.tab))render()},()=>{});
  db.doc('settings/campaigns').onSnapshot(s=>{S.campaignsCfg=s.exists?s.data():null;if(S.ready)render()},()=>{});
  db.doc('settings/logo').onSnapshot(s=>{S.logo=s.exists?s.data():null;renderHeader()},()=>{});
  db.doc('settings/steps').onSnapshot(s=>{S.steps=s.exists?s.data():null;if(S.ready)render()},()=>{});
  db.doc('settings/promo').onSnapshot(s=>{S.promoCfg=s.exists?s.data():null;if(S.ready)render()},()=>{});
  db.doc('settings/coaching').onSnapshot(s=>{S.coach=s.exists?s.data():null;if(S.ready)render()},()=>{});
  db.doc('settings/bonus').onSnapshot(s=>{S.bonus=s.exists?s.data():null;if(S.ready)render()},()=>{});
  if(S.uid)db.collection('reps/'+S.uid+'/growth').onSnapshot(snap=>{const o={};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data());S.growth[S.uid]=o;if(S.ready)render()},()=>{});
  if(leadRole()){
    db.doc('summary/teampb').onSnapshot(s=>{S.teamPbDoc=s.exists?s.data():null;if(S.tab==='pbs')render()},()=>{});
    db.collection('reps').onSnapshot(snap=>{const o={};snap.docs.forEach(d=>{const x=d.data();if(S.isOwner||d.id===S.uid||x.team===S.uid)o[d.id]=x});S.reps=o;S.me=o[S.uid]||null;parts.reps=true;syncWeekSubs(false);check()},onErr);
    if(S.isOwner)loadLegacy();
  }else{
    db.doc('reps/'+S.uid).onSnapshot(s=>{S.me=s.exists?s.data():null;S.reps=S.me?{[S.uid]:S.me}:{};parts.reps=true;check()},onErr);
    db.doc('summary/teampb').onSnapshot(s=>{S.teamPbDoc=s.exists?s.data():null;if(S.tab==='pbs')render()},()=>{});
    onWeekChange();
  }
  if(S.uid)db.collection('conf/'+S.uid+'/days').orderBy('date','desc').limit(1000).onSnapshot(snap=>{const o={};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data());S.conf[S.uid]=o;if(S.ready)render()},()=>{});
  if(S.uid)db.collection('reps/'+S.uid+'/entries').orderBy('date','desc').limit(1000).onSnapshot(snap=>{const o={};snap.docs.forEach(d=>o[d.id]=d.data());S.allMine=o;S.mineReady=true;parts.mine=true;check();if(!S.pbAuto){S.pbAuto=true;setTimeout(()=>refreshPB(S.uid),2000)}if(!S.finJump&&S.me&&S.me.finishedDay===todayStr()&&S.me.finSeen!==todayStr()){S.finJump=true;setTimeout(()=>{S.tab='log';if(leadRole()&&!S.viewMe){S.viewMe=true;onWeekChange()}setDate(todayStr());toast('Final KPIs received ✓ Now add cash/EFTPOS and do your daily reflection',5000);setTimeout(()=>{const el=document.getElementById('reflAnchor');if(el)el.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'})},600);write(()=>S.db.doc('reps/'+S.uid).update({finSeen:todayStr()}))},800)}if(S.me&&!S.boardSynced){S.boardSynced=true;setTimeout(()=>pushBoard(S.uid,S.week),1500)}},onErr);
})();
})();
</script>
